   28:         }

   31:

   33:     }
   34: }

############################################################
FILE: Builders\WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborComparerBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Supplies validation and deterministic tie handling for
    8:     /// Neighbor relationship comparers.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public abstract class NeighborComparerBase :
   11:         INeighborComparer
   12:     {
   13:         public int Compare(
   14:             IBaseObject subject,
   15:             INeighborRelationship left,
   16:             INeighborRelationship right)
   17:         {
   18:             if (subject == null)
   19:             {
   20:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   21:                     nameof(subject));
   22:             }
   23: 
   24:             if (left == null)
   25:             {
   26:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   27:                     nameof(left));
   28:             }
   29: 
   30:             if (right == null)
   31:             {
   32:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   33:                     nameof(right));
   34:             }
   35: 
   36:             var compared =
   37:                 CompareCore(
   38:                     subject,
   39:                     left,
   40:                     right);
   41: 
   42:             if (compared != 0)
   43:             {
   44:                 return compared;
   45:             }
   46: 
   47:             compared = string.Compare(
   48:                 left.Membership,
   49:                 right.Membership,
   50:                 StringComparison.Ordinal);
   51: 
   52:             if (compared != 0)
   53:             {
   54:                 return compared;
   55:             }
   56: 
   57:             return left.Neighbor.Id.CompareTo(
   58:                 right.Neighbor.Id);
   59:         }
   60: 
   61:         protected abstract int CompareCore(
   62:             IBaseObject subject,
   63:             INeighborRelationship left,
   64:             INeighborRelationship right);
   65:     }
   66: }

############################################################
FILE: Builders\WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborCompositeOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Applies a deterministic sequence of Neighbor sequence
    9:     /// operators. Each stage receives the prior stage snapshot.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public abstract class NeighborCompositeOperatorBase :
   12:         NeighborSequenceOperatorBase
   13:     {
   14:         private readonly IReadOnlyList<INeighborSequenceOperator>
   15:             _operators;
   16: 
   17:         protected NeighborCompositeOperatorBase(
   18:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborSequenceOperator> operators)
   19:         {
   20:             if (operators == null)
   21:             {
   22:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   23:                     nameof(operators));
   24:             }
   25: 
   26:             var snapshot =
   27:                 new INeighborSequenceOperator[operators.Count];
   28: 
   29:             for (var index = 0;
   30:                  index < operators.Count;
   31:                  index++)
   32:             {
   33:                 snapshot[index] =
   34:                     operators[index] ??
   35:                     throw new ArgumentException(
   36:                         "Neighbor operator pipelines cannot contain null stages.",
   37:                         nameof(operators));
   38:             }
   39: 
   40:             _operators = snapshot;
   41:         }
   42: 
   43:         protected sealed override
   44:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> ApplyCore(
   45:                 IBaseObject subject,
   46:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   47:         {
   48:             var current =
   49:                 relationships;
   50: 
   51:             for (var index = 0;
   52:                  index < _operators.Count;
   53:                  index++)
   54:             {
   55:                 current =
   56:                     _operators[index].Apply(
   57:                         subject,
   58:                         current);
   59:             }
   60: 
   61:             return current;
   62:         }
   63:     }
   64: }

############################################################
FILE: Builders\WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborFilterOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Filters a relationship snapshot through one reusable
    9:     /// predicate while preserving source order.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public abstract class NeighborFilterOperatorBase :
   12:         NeighborSequenceOperatorBase
   13:     {
   14:         private readonly INeighborPredicate _predicate;
   15: 
   16:         protected NeighborFilterOperatorBase(
   17:             INeighborPredicate predicate)
   18:         {
   19:             _predicate =
   20:                 predicate ??
   21:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   22:                     nameof(predicate));
   23:         }
   24: 
   25:         protected sealed override
   26:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> ApplyCore(
   27:                 IBaseObject subject,
   28:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   29:         {
   30:             var result =
   31:                 new List<INeighborRelationship>();
   32: 
   33:             for (var index = 0;
   34:                  index < relationships.Count;
   35:                  index++)
   36:             {
   37:                 var relationship =
   38:                     relationships[index];
   39: 
   40:                 if (_predicate.Includes(
   41:                         subject,
   42:                         relationship))
   43:                 {
   44:                     result.Add(
   45:                         relationship);
   46:                 }
   47:             }
   48: 
   49:             return Snapshot(
   50:                 result);
   51:         }
   52:     }
   53: }

FILE: Builders\WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborGroupingOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System.Collections.Generic;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Base class for operators that group relationships under
    8:     /// deterministic string keys.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public abstract class NeighborGroupingOperatorBase :
   11:         NeighborOperatorBase<
   12:             IReadOnlyDictionary<
   13:                 string,
   14:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>>>,
   15:         INeighborGroupingOperator
   16:     {
   17:         protected static IReadOnlyDictionary<
   18:             string,
   19:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>> Freeze(
   20:                 Dictionary<
   21:                     string,
   22:                     List<INeighborRelationship>> groups)
   23:         {
   24:             var frozen =
   25:                 new Dictionary<
   26:                     string,
   27:                     IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>>(
   28:                         groups.Count,
   29:                         System.StringComparer.Ordinal);
   30: 
   31:             foreach (var pair in groups)
   32:             {
   33:                 frozen.Add(
   34:                     pair.Key,
   35:                     pair.Value.ToArray());
   36:             }
   37: 
   38:             return frozen;
   39:         }
   40:     }
   41: }

FILE: Builders\WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Supplies argument validation and a stable execution
    9:     /// boundary for Neighbor operators.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public abstract class NeighborOperatorBase<TResult> :
   12:         INeighborOperator<TResult>
   13:     {
   14:         public TResult Apply(
   15:             IBaseObject subject,
   16:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   17:         {
   18:             if (subject == null)
   19:             {
   20:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   21:                     nameof(subject));
   22:             }
   23: 
   24:             if (relationships == null)
   25:             {
   26:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   27:                     nameof(relationships));
   28:             }
   29: 
   30:             return ApplyCore(
   31:                 subject,
   32:                 relationships);
   33:         }
   34: 
   35:         protected abstract TResult ApplyCore(
   36:             IBaseObject subject,
   37:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships);
   38:     }
   39: }

############################################################
FILE: Builders\WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborOrderOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Orders a copied relationship snapshot through a reusable
    9:     /// comparer and never mutates the source sequence.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public abstract class NeighborOrderOperatorBase :
   12:         NeighborSequenceOperatorBase
   13:     {
   14:         private readonly INeighborComparer _comparer;
   15: 
   16:         protected NeighborOrderOperatorBase(
   17:             INeighborComparer comparer)
   18:         {
   19:             _comparer =
   20:                 comparer ??
   21:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   22:                     nameof(comparer));
   23:         }
   24: 
   25:         protected sealed override
   26:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> ApplyCore(
   27:                 IBaseObject subject,
   28:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   29:         {
   30:             var result =
   31:                 new List<INeighborRelationship>(
   32:                     relationships.Count);
   33: 
   34:             for (var index = 0;
   35:                  index < relationships.Count;
   36:                  index++)
   37:             {
   38:                 result.Add(
   39:                     relationships[index]);
   40:             }
   41: 
   42:             result.Sort(
   43:                 (left, right) =>
   44:                     _comparer.Compare(
   45:                         subject,
   46:                         left,
   47:                         right));
   48: 
   49:             return Snapshot(
   50:                 result);
   51:         }
   52:     }
   53: }

FILE: Builders\WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborSamplerBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Base class for bounded, snapshot-safe Neighbor sampling.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public abstract class NeighborSamplerBase :
   11:         NeighborSequenceOperatorBase,
   12:         INeighborSampler
   13:     {
   14:         protected NeighborSamplerBase(
   15:             int count)
   16:         {
   17:             if (count < 0)
   18:             {
   19:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   20:                     nameof(count),
   21:                     count,
   22:                     "Neighbor sample count cannot be negative.");
   23:             }
   24: 
   25:             Count = count;
   26:         }
   27: 
   28:         public int Count { get; }
   29: 
   30:         protected sealed override
   31:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> ApplyCore(
   32:                 IBaseObject subject,
   33:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   34:         {
   35:             if (Count == 0 ||
   36:                 relationships.Count == 0)
   37:             {
   38:                 return Empty();
   39:             }
   40: 
   41:             var boundedCount =
   42:                 Math.Min(
   43:                     Count,
   44:                     relationships.Count);
   45: 
   46:             return SampleCore(
   47:                 subject,
   48:                 relationships,
   49:                 boundedCount);
   50:         }
   51: 
   52:         protected abstract IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>
   53:             SampleCore(
   54:                 IBaseObject subject,
   55:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships,
   56:                 int count);
   57:     }
   58: }

FILE: Builders\WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborSequenceOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System.Collections.Generic;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Base class for operators that return relationship
    8:     /// sequences without mutating the source snapshot.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public abstract class NeighborSequenceOperatorBase :
   11:         NeighborOperatorBase<
   12:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>>,
   13:         INeighborSequenceOperator
   14:     {
   15:         protected static IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>
   16:             Empty()
   17:         {
   18:             return new INeighborRelationship[0];
   19:         }
   20: 
   21:         protected static IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>
   22:             Snapshot(
   23:                 List<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   24:         {
   25:             return relationships.ToArray();
   26:         }
   27:     }
   28: }

   83:         [JsonPropertyName("category")]
   84:         public string Category { get; set; } =
   85:             string.Empty;
   86: 
   87:         [JsonPropertyName("statement")]
   88:         public string Statement { get; set; } =
   89:             string.Empty;
   90: 
   91:         [JsonPropertyName("expected")]
   92:         public string Expected { get; set; } =
   93:             string.Empty;
   94: 
   95:         [JsonPropertyName("output")]
   96:         public string Output { get; set; } =
   97:             string.Empty;
   98: 
   99:         [JsonPropertyName("dependency")]
  100:         public string? Dependency { get; set; }
  101: 
  102:         [JsonPropertyName("status")]
  103:         public WildsQslCertificationStatus Status { get; set; }
  104: 
  105:         [JsonPropertyName("passed")]
  106:         public bool Passed { get; set; }
  107: 
  108:         [JsonPropertyName("qslSucceeded")]
  109:         public bool QslSucceeded { get; set; }
  110: 
  111:         [JsonPropertyName("enteredWilds")]
  112:         public bool EnteredWilds { get; set; }
  113: 
  114:         [JsonPropertyName("momentCompleted")]
  115:         public bool MomentCompleted { get; set; }
  116: 
  117:         [JsonPropertyName("timelineRecorded")]
  118:         public bool TimelineRecorded { get; set; }
  119: 
  120:         [JsonPropertyName("epochRecorded")]
  121:         public bool EpochRecorded { get; set; }
  122: 
  123:         [JsonPropertyName("momentOutcome")]
  124:         public string MomentOutcome { get; set; } =
  125:             string.Empty;
  126: 
  127:         [JsonPropertyName("parseFailed")]
  128:         public bool ParseFailed { get; set; }
  129: 
  130:         [JsonPropertyName("evaluationFailed")]
  131:         public bool EvaluationFailed { get; set; }
  132: 
  133:         [JsonPropertyName("parserDiagnostics")]
  134:         public string[] ParserDiagnostics { get; set; } =
  135:             Array.Empty<string>();
  136: 
  137:         [JsonPropertyName("runtimeDiagnostics")]
  138:         public string[] RuntimeDiagnostics { get; set; } =
  139:             Array.Empty<string>();
  140: 
  141:         [JsonPropertyName("exception")]
  142:         public string? Exception { get; set; }
  143:     }
  144: 
  145:     public static class WildsQslCertification
  146:     {
  147:         private const string SuiteFileName =
  148:             "qsl_core_statement_testing.json";
  149: 
  150:         private const string ReportFileName =
  151:             "wilds_qsl_core_statement_testing_output.json";
  152: 
  153:         private static readonly List<string> Passed =
  154:             new List<string>();
  155: 
  156:         public static async Task<WildsQslCertificationReport> RunAllAsync()
  157:         {
  158:             Passed.Clear();
  159: 
  160:             var suitePath =
  161:                 Path.Combine(
  162:                     AppContext.BaseDirectory,
  163:                     "Certification",
  164:                     SuiteFileName);
  165: 
  166:             var suite =
  167:                 LoadSuite(
  168:                     suitePath);
  169: 
  170:             var qslEngine =
  171:                 CreateQslEngine();
  172: 
  173:             var component =
  174:                 CreateComponent(
  175:                     "certification.qsl.core");
  176: 
  177:             using var wildsEngine =
  178:                 CreateWildsEngine(
  179:                     component);
  180: 
  181:             Assert(
  182:                 wildsEngine.Initialize(),
  183:                 "WildsEngine initialization should succeed.");
  184: 
  185:             var executor =
  186:                 new WildsQslExecutor(
  187:                     qslEngine,
  188:                     wildsEngine);
  189: 
  190:             var report =
  191:                 new WildsQslCertificationReport();
  192: 
  193:             TestLog.Write(
  194:                 "=== Wilds / QSL Core Certification ===");
  195: 
  196:             TestLog.State(
  197:                 "Route: QSL source -> WildsQslExecutor -> " +
  198:                 "WildsEngine -> Pulse -> WorkUnit -> " +
  199:                 "QSL -> Moment -> Timeline -> Epoch");
  200: 
  201:             TestLog.State(
  202:                 "Suite: " +
  203:                 suitePath);
  204: 
  205:             TestLog.State(
  206:                 "Tests: " +
  207:                 suite.Tests.Count);
  208: 
  209:             Console.WriteLine();
  210: 
  211:             for (
  212:                 var index = 0;
  213:                 index < suite.Tests.Count;
  214:                 index++)
  215:             {
  216:                 var test =
  217:                     suite.Tests[index];
  218: 
  219:                 var result =
  220:                     await ExecuteTestAsync(
  221:                             executor,
  222:                             wildsEngine,
  223:                             test)
  224:                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  225: 
  226:                 report.Results.Add(
  227:                     result);
  228: 
  229:                 switch (result.Status)
  230:                 {
  231:                     case WildsQslCertificationStatus.Passed:
  232:                         report.Passed++;
  233:                         Passed.Add(
  234:                             CreateTestName(
  235:                                 index,
  236:                                 test));
  237:                         break;
  238: 
  239:                     case WildsQslCertificationStatus.MissingDependency:
  240:                         report.MissingDependencies++;
  241:                         break;
  242: 
  243:                     case WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed:
  244:                         report.Failed++;
  245:                         break;
  246: 
  247:                     default:
  248:                         throw new InvalidOperationException(
  249:                             "Unknown certification status: " +
  250:                             result.Status);
  251:                 }
  252: 
  253:                 if (result.MomentOutcome ==
  254:                     MomentOutcome.Completed.ToString())
  255:                 {
  256:                     report.CompletedMoments++;
  257:                 }
  258: 
  259:                 if (result.MomentOutcome ==
  260:                     MomentOutcome.Failed.ToString())
  261:                 {
  262:                     report.FailedMoments++;
  263:                 }
  264: 
  265:                 Console.Write(
  266:                     result.Status switch
  267:                     {
  268:                         WildsQslCertificationStatus.Passed => ".",
  269:                         WildsQslCertificationStatus.MissingDependency => "M",
  270:                         WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed => "F",
  271:                         _ => "?"
  272:                     });
  273: 
  274:                 if ((index + 1) % 50 == 0)
  275:                 {
  276:                     Console.WriteLine();
  277:                 }
  278:             }
  279: 
  280:             if (suite.Tests.Count % 50 != 0)
  281:             {
  282:                 Console.WriteLine();
  283:             }
  284: 
  285:             report.Total =
  286:                 report.Results.Count;
  287: 
  288:             report.TimelineMoments =
  289:                 wildsEngine.Timeline.Count;
  290: 
  291:             report.EpochTimelines =
  292:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines.Count;
  293: 
  294:             ValidateCompleteHistory(
  295:                 suite,
  296:                 wildsEngine);
  297: 
  298:             WriteSummary(
  299:                 report);
  300: 
  301:             if (report.Failed > 0)
  302:             {
  303:                 WriteFailures(
  304:                     report.Results);
  305:             }
  306: 
  307:             if (report.MissingDependencies > 0)
  308:             {
  309:                 WriteMissingDependencies(
  310:                     report.Results);
  311:             }
  312: 
  313:             WriteReport(
  314:                 report);
  315: 
  316:             Assert(
  317:                 report.Total ==
  318:                 suite.Tests.Count,
  319:                 "Certification report must contain one result " +
  320:                 "for every suite case.");
  321: 
  322:             Assert(
  323:                 report.Total ==
  324:                 report.Passed +
  325:                 report.Failed +
  326:                 report.MissingDependencies,
  327:                 "Every certification result must be accounted for " +
  328:                 "as passed, failed, or missing a dependency.");
  329:             
  330:             Assert(
  331:                 report.IsCertified,
  332:                 "Wilds / QSL core certification requires every " +
  333:                 "core case to pass with no missing dependencies.");
  334: 
  335:             TestLog.Write(
  336:                 "Wilds / QSL core certification completed: " +
  337:                 report.Passed +
  338:                 "/" +
  339:                 report.Total +
  340:                 " cases passed.");
  341: 
  342:             TestLog.State(
  343:                 "Certification status: " +
  344:                 GetCertificationStatus(
  345:                     report));
  346:             TestLog.State(
  347:                 "Missing Dependencies: " +
  348:                 report.MissingDependencies);
  349: 
  350:             TestLog.State(
  351:                 "Completed Moments:    " +
  352:                 report.CompletedMoments);
  353: 
  354:             TestLog.State(
  355:                 "Failed Moments: " +
  356:                 report.FailedMoments);
  357: 
  358:             TestLog.State(
  359:                 "Timeline Moments: " +
  360:                 report.TimelineMoments);
  361: 
  362:             TestLog.State(
  363:                 "Epoch Timelines: " +
  364:                 report.EpochTimelines);
  365: 
  366:             return report;
  367: 
  368:         }
  369: 
  370:         private static WildsQslCertificationSuite LoadSuite(
  371:             string suitePath)
  372:         {
  373:             if (!File.Exists(
  374:                     suitePath))
  375:             {
  376:                 throw new FileNotFoundException(
  377:                     "Wilds / QSL certification suite " +
  378:                     "was not found.",
  379:                     suitePath);
  380:             }
  381: 
  382:             try
  383:             {
  384:                 var json =
  385:                     File.ReadAllText(
  386:                         suitePath);
  387: 
  388:                 var suite =
  389:                     JsonSerializer.Deserialize
  390:                         <WildsQslCertificationSuite>(
  391:                             json,
  392:                             new JsonSerializerOptions
  393:                             {
  394:                                 PropertyNameCaseInsensitive =
  395:                                     true
  396:                             });
  397: 
  398:                 if (suite == null)
  399:                 {
  400:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  401:                         "Certification suite deserialized to null.");
  402:                 }
  403: 
  404:                 if (suite.Tests.Count == 0)
  405:                 {
  406:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  407:                         "Certification suite contains no tests.");
  408:                 }
  409: 
  410:                 return suite;
  411:             }
  412:             catch (Exception exception)
  413:                 when (exception is not
  414:                       FileNotFoundException)
  415:             {
  416:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  417:                     "Certification suite could not be loaded.",
  418:                     exception);
  419:             }
  420:         }
  421: 
  422:         private static async Task<WildsQslCertificationResult>
  423:             ExecuteTestAsync(
  424:                 WildsQslExecutor executor,
  425:                 global::WildsEngine.Core.WildsEngine
  426:                     wildsEngine,
  427:                 WildsQslCertificationTest test)
  428:         {
  429:             var expectsError =
  430:                 string.Equals(
  431:                     test.Category,
  432:                     "Error",
  433:                     StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
  434: 
  435:             var timelineBefore =
  436:                 wildsEngine.Timeline.Count;
  437: 
  438:             IMoment moment;
  439: 
  440:             try
  441:             {
  442:                 /*
  443:                  * This is the important certification boundary.
  444:                  *
  445:                  * The QSL source enters Wilds directly.
  446:                  * Wilds owns the Pulse and WorkUnit before QSL
  447:                  * evaluates the statement.
  448:                  */
  449:                 moment =
  450:                     await executor.ExecuteAsync(
  451:                             test.Statement)
  452:                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  453:             }
  454:             catch (Exception exception)
  455:             {
  456:                 /*
  457:                  * WildsEngine.ExecuteAsync is expected to turn
  458:                  * operation failures into failed Moments.
  459:                  *
  460:                  * An exception escaping the executor means the
  461:                  * complete Wilds route did not resolve honestly.
  462:                  */
  463:                 return new WildsQslCertificationResult
  464:                 {
  465:                     Category =
  466:                         test.Category,
  467: 
  468:                     Statement =
  469:                         test.Statement,
  470: 
  471:                     Expected =
  472:                         test.Expected,
  473: 
  474:                     Output =
  475:                         string.Empty,
  476:                     Dependency =
  477:                         test.Dependency,
  478:                     Status =
  479:                         WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed,
  480:                     Passed =
  481:                         false,
  482: 
  483:                     QslSucceeded =
  484:                         false,
  485: 
  486:                     EnteredWilds =
  487:                         true,
  488: 
  489:                     MomentCompleted =
  490:                         false,
  491: 
  492:                     TimelineRecorded =
  493:                         false,
  494: 
  495:                     EpochRecorded =
  496:                         false,
  497: 
  498:                     MomentOutcome =
  499:                         string.Empty,
  500: 
  501:                     ParseFailed =
  502:                         false,
  503: 
  504:                     EvaluationFailed =
  505:                         false,
  506: 
  507:                     ParserDiagnostics =
  508:                         Array.Empty<string>(),
  509: 
  510:                     RuntimeDiagnostics =
  511:                         Array.Empty<string>(),
  512: 
  513:                     Exception =
  514:                         exception.GetType().Name +
  515:                         ": " +
  516:                         exception.Message
  517:                 };
  518:             }
  519: 
  520:             var timelineAfter =
  521:                 wildsEngine.Timeline.Count;
  522: 
  523:             var timelineRecorded =
  524:                 timelineAfter ==
  525:                 timelineBefore + 1 &&
  526:                 timelineAfter > 0 &&
  527:                 ReferenceEquals(
  528:                     moment,
  529:                     wildsEngine.Timeline
  530:                         .Moments[timelineAfter - 1]);
  531: 
  532:             var epochRecorded =
  533:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Count == 1 &&
  534:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines.Count == 1 &&
  535:                 ReferenceEquals(
  536:                     wildsEngine.Timeline,
  537:                     wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines[0]) &&
  538:                 wildsEngine.Epoch
  539:                     .Timelines[0]
  540:                     .Moments
  541:                     .Count ==
  542:                     timelineAfter &&
  543:                 ReferenceEquals(
  544:                     moment,
  545:                     wildsEngine.Epoch
  546:                         .Timelines[0]
  547:                         .Moments[timelineAfter - 1]);
  548: 
  549:             if (moment.Outcome ==
  550:                 MomentOutcome.Completed)
  551:             {
  552:                 var output =
  553:                     FormatValue(
  554:                         moment.Result);
  555: 
  556:                 var passed =
  557:                     !expectsError &&
  558:                     moment.Exception == null &&
  559:                     moment.WorkUnitIds.Count == 1 &&
  560:                     timelineRecorded &&
  561:                     epochRecorded &&
  562:                     string.Equals(
  563:                         output,
  564:                         test.Expected,
  565:                         StringComparison.Ordinal);
  566: 
  567:                 return new WildsQslCertificationResult
  568:                 {
  569:                     Category =
  570:                         test.Category,
  571: 
  572:                     Statement =
  573:                         test.Statement,
  574: 
  575:                     Expected =
  576:                         test.Expected,
  577: 
  578:                     Output =
  579:                         output,
  580:                     Dependency =
  581:                         test.Dependency,
  582:                     Status =
  583:                         ClassifyResult(
  584:                             test,
  585:                             passed,
  586:                             dependencyMissing: false),
  587:                     Passed =
  588:                         passed,
  589: 
  590:                     QslSucceeded =
  591:                         true,
  592: 
  593:                     EnteredWilds =
  594:                         true,
  595: 
  596:                     MomentCompleted =
  597:                         true,
  598: 
  599:                     TimelineRecorded =
  600:                         timelineRecorded,
  601: 
  602:                     EpochRecorded =
  603:                         epochRecorded,
  604: 
  605:                     MomentOutcome =
  606:                         moment.Outcome.ToString(),
  607: 
  608:                     ParseFailed =
  609:                         false,
  610: 
  611:                     EvaluationFailed =
  612:                         false,
  613: 
  614:                     ParserDiagnostics =
  615:                         Array.Empty<string>(),
  616: 
  617:                     RuntimeDiagnostics =
  618:                         Array.Empty<string>(),
  619: 
  620:                     Exception =
  621:                         null
  622:                 };
  623:             }
  624: 
  625:             if (moment.Outcome ==
  626:                     MomentOutcome.Failed &&
  627:                 moment.Exception is
  628:                     WildsQslExecutionException
  629:                     qslException)
  630:             {
  631:                 var processingResult =
  632:                     qslException.Result;
  633: 
  634:                 var parserDiagnostics =
  635:                     CopyParserDiagnostics(
  636:                         processingResult);
  637: 
  638:                 var runtimeDiagnostics =
  639:                     CopyRuntimeDiagnostics(
  640:                         processingResult);
  641: 
  642:                 var output =
  643:                     FormatValue(
  644:                         processingResult.Value);
  645:                 var dependencyMissing =
  646:                     IsDeclaredDependencyMissing(
  647:                         test,
  648:                         runtimeDiagnostics);
  649:                 var passed =
  650:                     expectsError &&
  651:                     !processingResult.Succeeded &&
  652:                     moment.Result == null &&
  653:                     moment.WorkUnitIds.Count == 1 &&
  654:                     timelineRecorded &&
  655:                     epochRecorded;
  656: 
  657:                 return new WildsQslCertificationResult
  658:                 {
  659:                     Category =
  660:                         test.Category,
  661: 
  662:                     Statement =
  663:                         test.Statement,
  664: 
  665:                     Expected =
  666:                         test.Expected,
  667: 
  668:                     Output =
  669:                         output,
  670:                     Dependency =
  671:                         test.Dependency,
  672:                     Status =
  673:                         ClassifyResult(
  674:                             test,
  675:                             passed,
  676:                             dependencyMissing),
  677:                     Passed =
  678:                         passed &&
  679:                         !dependencyMissing,
  680:                     QslSucceeded =
  681:                         processingResult.Succeeded,
  682: 
  683:                     EnteredWilds =
  684:                         true,
  685: 
  686:                     MomentCompleted =
  687:                         false,
  688: 
  689:                     TimelineRecorded =
  690:                         timelineRecorded,
  691: 
  692:                     EpochRecorded =
  693:                         epochRecorded,
  694: 
  695:                     MomentOutcome =
  696:                         moment.Outcome.ToString(),
  697: 
  698:                     ParseFailed =
  699:                         processingResult.ParseFailed,
  700: 
  701:                     EvaluationFailed =
  702:                         processingResult.EvaluationFailed,
  703: 
  704:                     ParserDiagnostics =
  705:                         parserDiagnostics,
  706: 
  707:                     RuntimeDiagnostics =
  708:                         runtimeDiagnostics,
  709: 
  710:                     Exception =
  711:                         processingResult.Exception?
  712:                             .Message ??
  713:                         qslException.Message
  714:                 };
  715:             }
  716: 
  717:             return new WildsQslCertificationResult
  718:             {
  719:                 Category =
  720:                     test.Category,
  721: 
  722:                 Statement =
  723:                     test.Statement,
  724: 
  725:                 Expected =
  726:                     test.Expected,
  727: 
  728:                 Output =
  729:                     FormatValue(
  730:                         moment.Result),
  731:                 Dependency =
  732:                     test.Dependency,
  733:                 Status =
  734:                     WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed,
  735:                 Passed =
  736:                     false,
  737: 
  738:                 QslSucceeded =
  739:                     false,
  740: 
  741:                 EnteredWilds =
  742:                     true,
  743: 
  744:                 MomentCompleted =
  745:                     false,
  746: 
  747:                 TimelineRecorded =
  748:                     timelineRecorded,
  749: 
  750:                 EpochRecorded =
  751:                     epochRecorded,
  752: 
  753:                 MomentOutcome =
  754:                     moment.Outcome.ToString(),
  755: 
  756:                 ParseFailed =
  757:                     false,
  758: 
  759:                 EvaluationFailed =
  760:                     false,
  761: 
  762:                 ParserDiagnostics =
  763:                     Array.Empty<string>(),
  764: 
  765:                 RuntimeDiagnostics =
  766:                     Array.Empty<string>(),
  767: 
  768:                 Exception =
  769:                     moment.Exception?
  770:                         .Message ??
  771:                     "Unexpected terminal Moment outcome: " +
  772:                     moment.Outcome
  773:             };
  774:         }
  775: 
  776:         private static void ValidateCompleteHistory(
  777:             WildsQslCertificationSuite suite,
  778:             global::WildsEngine.Core.WildsEngine
  779:                 wildsEngine)
  780:         {
  781:             Assert(
  782:                 wildsEngine.Timeline.Count ==
  783:                 suite.Tests.Count,
  784:                 "Wilds Timeline must contain exactly one " +
  785:                 "Moment per certification statement.");
  786: 
  787:             Assert(
  788:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Count == 1,
  789:                 "The isolated certification Epoch must " +
  790:                 "contain exactly one Timeline.");
  791: 
  792:             Assert(
  793:                 wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines.Count == 1,
  794:                 "The certification Epoch Timeline collection " +
  795:                 "must contain exactly one Timeline.");
  796: 
  797:             Assert(
  798:                 ReferenceEquals(
  799:                     wildsEngine.Timeline,
  800:                     wildsEngine.Epoch.Timelines[0]),
  801:                 "The Epoch must retain the engine Timeline.");
  802: 
  803:             Assert(
  804:                 wildsEngine.Epoch
  805:                     .Timelines[0]
  806:                     .Moments
  807:                     .Count ==
  808:                 suite.Tests.Count,
  809:                 "The Epoch must expose the complete " +
  810:                 "certification Moment history.");
  811:         }
  812: 
  813:         private static string[]
  814:             CopyParserDiagnostics(
  815:                 QslProcessingResult processingResult)
  816:         {
  817:             var diagnostics =
  818:                 new string[
  819:                     processingResult
  820:                         .ParserDiagnostics
  821:                         .Count
  822:                 ];
  823: 
  824:             for (
  825:                 var index = 0;
  826:                 index < diagnostics.Length;
  827:                 index++)
  828:             {
  829:                 diagnostics[index] =
  830:                     processingResult
  831:                         .ParserDiagnostics[index];
  832:             }
  833: 
  834:             return diagnostics;
  835:         }
  836: 
  837:         private static string[]
  838:             CopyRuntimeDiagnostics(
  839:                 QslProcessingResult processingResult)
  840:         {
  841:             var diagnostics =
  842:                 new string[
  843:                     processingResult
  844:                         .RuntimeDiagnostics
  845:                         .Count
  846:                 ];
  847: 
  848:             for (
  849:                 var index = 0;
  850:                 index < diagnostics.Length;
  851:                 index++)
  852:             {
  853:                 var entry =
  854:                     processingResult
  855:                         .RuntimeDiagnostics[index];
  856: 
  857:                 diagnostics[index] =
  858:                     "[" +
  859:                     entry.EntryType +
  860:                     "] " +
  861:                     entry.Source +
  862:                     ": " +
  863:                     entry.Message;
  864:             }
  865: 
  866:             return diagnostics;
  867:         }
  868: 
  869:         private static string FormatValue(
  870:             object? value)
  871:         {
  872:             if (value == null)
  873:             {
  874:                 return string.Empty;
  875:             }
  876: 
  877:             if (value is bool boolean)
  878:             {
  879:                 return boolean
  880:                     ? "true"
  881:                     : "false";
  882:             }
  883: 
  884:             if (value is string text)
  885:             {
  886:                 return text;
  887:             }
  888: 
  889:             if (value is char character)
  890:             {
  891:                 return character.ToString();
  892:             }
  893: 
  894:             if (value is IFormattable formattable)
  895:             {
  896:                 return formattable.ToString(
  897:                            null,
  898:                            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ??
  899:                        string.Empty;
  900:             }
  901: 
  902:             return value.ToString() ??
  903:                    string.Empty;
  904:         }
  905: 
  906:         private static QslEngine CreateQslEngine()
  907:         {
  908:             var registry =
  909:                 new QslRuntimeRegistry();
  910: 
  911:             registry.RegisterDefaultEvaluators();
  912: 
  913:             var context =
  914:                 new QslRuntimeContext(
  915:                     registry);
  916: 
  917:             return new QslEngine(
  918:                 context);
  919:         }
  920: 
  921:         private static IComponent CreateComponent(
  922:             string layer)
  923:         {
  924:             return new TestComponent(
  925:                 layer);
  926:         }
  927: 
  928:         private static
  929:             global::WildsEngine.Core.WildsEngine
  930:             CreateWildsEngine(
  931:                 IComponent component)
  932:         {
  933:             return new
  934:                 global::WildsEngine.Core.WildsEngine(
  935:                     component,
  936:                     new WildsEngineOptions
  937:                     {
  938:                         Layer =
  939:                             "certification.qsl.core",
  940: 
  941:                         RetainedInstructionsProcessors =
  942:                             1,
  943: 
  944:                         ConcurrentInstructionsProcessors =
  945:                             1,
  946: 
  947:                         RetainedTaskProcessors =
  948:                             1,
  949: 
  950:                         ConcurrentTaskProcessors =
  951:                             1
  952:                     });
  953:         }
  954: 
  955:         private static string CreateTestName(
  956:             int index,
  957:             WildsQslCertificationTest test)
  958:         {
  959:             return
  960:                 (index + 1) +
  961:                 ": [" +
  962:                 test.Category +
  963:                 "] " +
  964:                 test.Statement;
  965:         }
  966: 
  967:         private static void WriteSummary(
  968:             WildsQslCertificationReport report)
  969:         {
  970:             Console.WriteLine();
  971:             Console.WriteLine(
  972:                 "========================================");
  973: 
  974:             Console.WriteLine(
  975:                 "Total:                " +
  976:                 report.Total);
  977: 
  978:             Console.WriteLine(
  979:                 "Passed:               " +
  980:                 report.Passed);
  981: 
  982:             Console.WriteLine(
  983:                 "Failed:               " +
  984:                 report.Failed);
  985:             Console.WriteLine(
  986:                 "Missing Dependencies: " +
  987:                 report.MissingDependencies);
  988: 
  989:             Console.WriteLine(
  990:                 "Completed Moments:    " +
  991:                 report.CompletedMoments);
  992: 
  993:             Console.WriteLine(
  994:                 "Failed Moments:       " +
  995:                 report.FailedMoments);
  996: 
  997:             Console.WriteLine(
  998:                 "Timeline Moments:     " +
  999:                 report.TimelineMoments);
 1000: 
 1001:             Console.WriteLine(
 1002:                 "Epoch Timelines:      " +
 1003:                 report.EpochTimelines);
 1004: 
 1005:             Console.WriteLine(
 1006:                 "========================================");
 1007:         }
 1008: 
 1009:         private static void WriteFailures(
 1010:             IReadOnlyList<WildsQslCertificationResult>
 1011:                 results)
 1012:         {
 1013:             Console.WriteLine();
 1014:             Console.WriteLine(
 1015:                 "Failures:");
 1016: 
 1017:             foreach (var result in results)
 1018:             {
 1019:                 if (result.Status !=
 1020:                     WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed)
 1021:                 {
 1022:                     continue;
 1023:                 }
 1024: 
 1025:                 Console.WriteLine();
 1026:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1027:                     "[" +
 1028:                     result.Category +
 1029:                     "] " +
 1030:                     result.Statement);
 1031: 
 1032:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1033:                     "  Status:   " +
 1034:                     result.Status);
 1035:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1036:                     "  Expected: " +
 1037:                     (
 1038:                         string.IsNullOrEmpty(
 1039:                             result.Expected)
 1040:                             ? "<error>"
 1041:                             : result.Expected
 1042:                     ));
 1043: 
 1044:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1045:                     "  Actual:   " +
 1046:                     (
 1047:                         string.IsNullOrEmpty(
 1048:                             result.Output)
 1049:                             ? "<null>"
 1050:                             : result.Output
 1051:                     ));
 1052: 
 1053:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1054:                     "  Outcome: " +
 1055:                     (
 1056:                         string.IsNullOrEmpty(
 1057:                             result.MomentOutcome)
 1058:                             ? "<none>"
 1059:                             : result.MomentOutcome
 1060:                     ));
 1061: 
 1062:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1063:                     "  QSL succeeded: " +
 1064:                     result.QslSucceeded);
 1065: 
 1066:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1067:                     "  Entered Wilds: " +
 1068:                     result.EnteredWilds);
 1069: 
 1070:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1071:                     "  Moment completed: " +
 1072:                     result.MomentCompleted);
 1073: 
 1074:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1075:                     "  Timeline recorded: " +
 1076:                     result.TimelineRecorded);
 1077: 
 1078:                 Console.WriteLine(
 1079:                     "  Epoch recorded: " +
 1080:                     result.EpochRecorded);
 1081: 
 1082:                 foreach (var diagnostic in
 1083:                          result.ParserDiagnostics)
 1084:                 {
 1085:                     Console.WriteLine(
 1086:                         "  Parser: " +
 1087:                         diagnostic);
 1088:                 }
 1089: 
 1090:                 foreach (var diagnostic in
 1091:                          result.RuntimeDiagnostics)
 1092:                 {
 1093:                     Console.WriteLine(
 1094:                         "  Runtime: " +
 1095:                         diagnostic);
 1096:                 }
 1097: 
 1098:                 if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(
 1099:                         result.Exception))
 1100:                 {
 1101:                     Console.WriteLine(
 1102:                         "  Exception: " +
 1103:                         result.Exception);
 1104:                 }
 1105:             }
 1106:         }
 1107: 
 1108:         private static void WriteMissingDependencies(
 1109:             IReadOnlyList<WildsQslCertificationResult> results)
 1110:         {
 1111:             Console.WriteLine();
 1112:             Console.WriteLine("Missing consumer dependencies:");
 1113:             foreach (var result in results)
 1114:             {
 1115:                 if (result.Status != WildsQslCertificationStatus.MissingDependency)
 1116:                 {
 1117:                     continue;
 1118:                 }
 1119:                 Console.WriteLine();
 1120:                 Console.WriteLine("[" + result.Category + "] " + result.Statement);
 1121:                 Console.WriteLine("  Dependency: " + (result.Dependency ?? "<unspecified>"));
 1122:                 Console.WriteLine("  Moment outcome: " + result.MomentOutcome);
 1123:                 foreach (var diagnostic in result.RuntimeDiagnostics)
 1124:                 {
 1125:                     Console.WriteLine("  Runtime: " + diagnostic);
 1126:                 }
 1127:             }
 1128:         }
 1129: 
 1130:         private static void WriteReport(
 1131:             WildsQslCertificationReport report)
 1132:         {
 1133:             var reportPath =
 1134:                 Path.Combine(
 1135:                     AppContext.BaseDirectory,
 1136:                     "Certification",
 1137:                     ReportFileName);
 1138: 
 1139:             try
 1140:             {
 1141:                 var reportDirectory = Path.GetDirectoryName(reportPath);
 1142:                 if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(reportDirectory))
 1143:                 {
 1144:                     Directory.CreateDirectory(reportDirectory);
 1145:                 }
 1146:                 var reportJson =
 1147:                     JsonSerializer.Serialize(
 1148:                         report,
 1149:                         new JsonSerializerOptions
 1150:                         {
 1151:                             WriteIndented = true,
 1152:                             Converters = { new JsonStringEnumConverter() }
 1153:                         });
 1154: 
 1155:                 File.WriteAllText(
 1156:                     reportPath,
 1157:                     reportJson);
 1158: 
 1159:                 TestLog.State(
 1160:                     "Certification report: " +
 1161:                     reportPath);
 1162:             }
 1163:             catch (Exception exception)
 1164:             {
 1165:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
 1166:                     "Certification report could not be written.",
 1167:                     exception);
 1168:             }
 1169:         }
 1170:         
 1171:         private static WildsQslCertificationStatus ClassifyResult(
 1172:             WildsQslCertificationTest test,
 1173:             bool behavioralPass,
 1174:             bool dependencyMissing)
 1175:         {
 1176:             if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(test.Dependency) && dependencyMissing)
 1177:             {
 1178:                 return WildsQslCertificationStatus.MissingDependency;
 1179:             }
 1180:             return behavioralPass
 1181:                 ? WildsQslCertificationStatus.Passed
 1182:                 : WildsQslCertificationStatus.Failed;
 1183:         }
 1184: 
 1185:         private static bool IsDeclaredDependencyMissing(
 1186:             WildsQslCertificationTest test,
 1187:             IReadOnlyList<string> runtimeDiagnostics)
 1188:         {
 1189:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(test.Dependency))
 1190:             {
 1191:                 return false;
 1192:             }
 1193:             var expected = "Unresolved identifier: " + test.Dependency;
 1194:             foreach (var diagnostic in runtimeDiagnostics)
 1195:             {
 1196:                 if (diagnostic.Contains(expected, StringComparison.Ordinal))
 1197:                 {
 1198:                     return true;
 1199:                 }
 1200:             }
 1201:             return false;
 1202:         }
 1203: 
 1204:         private static string GetCertificationStatus(
 1205:             WildsQslCertificationReport report)
 1206:         {
 1207:             if (report.IsCertified)
 1208:             {
 1209:                 return "CERTIFIED";
 1210:             }
 1211:             if (report.Failed == 0 && report.MissingDependencies > 0)
 1212:             {
 1213:                 return "INCOMPLETE - CONSUMER DEPENDENCIES MISSING";
 1214:             }
 1215:             return "NOT CERTIFIED";
 1216:         }
 1217: 
 1218:         private static void Assert(
 1219:             bool condition,
 1220:             string message)
 1221:         {
 1222:             if (!condition)
 1223:             {
 1224:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
 1225:                     message);
 1226:             }
 1227:         }
 1228:     }
 1229: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\CertificationLogger.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Shared console logger for WildsEngine certification
    8:     /// and diagnostic test output.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public static class CertificationLogger
   11:     {
   12:         public static void LogSection(string title)
   13:         {
   14:             Console.WriteLine();
   15:             Console.WriteLine(
   16:                 "============================================");
   17:             Console.WriteLine(
   18:                 $" {title}");
   19:             Console.WriteLine(
   20:                 "============================================");
   21:         }
   22: 
   23:         public static void LogTest(string name)
   24:         {
   25:             Console.WriteLine(
   26:                 $"[TEST] {name}");
   27:         }
   28: 
   29:         public static void LogPass(string name)
   30:         {
   31:             WriteColored(
   32:                 ConsoleColor.Green,
   33:                 $"[PASS] {name}");
   34:         }
   35: 
   36:         public static void LogTask(string message)
   37:         {
   38:             WriteColored(
   39:                 ConsoleColor.DarkGray,
   40:                 $"    [TASK] {message}");
   41:         }
   42: 
   43:         public static void LogState(
   44:             string name,
   45:             object? value)
   46:         {
   47:             WriteColored(
   48:                 ConsoleColor.Cyan,
   49:                 $"    [STATE] {name} = {value}");
   50:         }
   51: 
   52:         public static void LogPool<T>(
   53:             string name,
   54:             IObjectPool<T> pool)
   55:             where T : class
   56:         {
   57:             if (pool == null)
   58:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(pool));
   59: 
   60:             WriteColored(
   61:                 ConsoleColor.DarkCyan,
   62:                 $"    [POOL] {name}");
   63: 
   64:             Console.WriteLine(
   65:                 $"           Active     : {pool.ActiveCount}");
   66: 
   67:             Console.WriteLine(
   68:                 $"           Available  : {pool.AvailableCount}");
   69: 
   70:             Console.WriteLine(
   71:                 $"           Created    : {pool.CreatedCount}");
   72: 
   73:             Console.WriteLine(
   74:                 $"           Retained   : {pool.MaximumRetained}");
   75: 
   76:             Console.WriteLine(
   77:                 $"           Concurrent : {pool.MaximumConcurrent}");
   78:         }
   79: 
   80:         public static void LogExpected<TException>()
   81:             where TException : Exception
   82:         {
   83:             WriteColored(
   84:                 ConsoleColor.DarkYellow,
   85:                 $"    [EXPECTED] {typeof(TException).Name}");
   86:         }
   87: 
   88:         private static void WriteColored(
   89:             ConsoleColor color,
   90:             string message)
   91:         {
   92:             var previousColor =
   93:                 Console.ForegroundColor;
   94: 
   95:             try
   96:             {
   97:                 Console.ForegroundColor = color;
   98: 
   99:                 Console.WriteLine(message);
  100:             }
  101:             finally
  102:             {
  103:                 Console.ForegroundColor =
  104:                     previousColor;
  105:             }
  106:         }
  107:     }
  108: }
