############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\EndToEndProcessingTests.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using System.Threading;
    4: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    6: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing;
    7: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    8: using WildsEngine.Tests.Registry;
    9: using Xunit;
   10: 
   11: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests
   12: {
   13:     /// <summary>
   14:     /// Certifies the current complete processing route:
   15:     ///
   16:     /// WildsEngine
   17:     ///     -> instruction processor lease
   18:     ///     -> instruction registry
   19:     ///     -> task processor lease
   20:     ///     -> task registry
   21:     ///     -> processor return
   22:     ///     -> instruction removal
   23:     ///
   24:     /// These tests intentionally use the current composition surface.
   25:     /// They do not invent the future WildsEngine.Process(...) contract.
   26:     /// </summary>
   27:     public static class EndToEndProcessingTests
   28:     {
   29:         private static readonly List<string> Passed =
   30:             new List<string>();
   31: 
   32:         public static async Task RunAllAsync()
   33:         {
   34:             Passed.Clear();
   35: 
   36:             TestLog.Write(
   37:                 "=== End-to-End Processing Tests ===");
   38: 
   39:             await RunAsync(
   40:                     "Instruction flows through complete processing route",
   41:                     InstructionFlowsThroughCompleteProcessingRoute)
   42:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
   43: 
   44:             await RunAsync(
   45:                     "Processing failure returns processors and removes instruction",
   46:                     ProcessingFailureReturnsProcessorsAndRemovesInstruction)
   47:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
   48: 
   49:             await RunAsync(
   50:                     "Processing cancellation returns processors and removes instruction",
   51:                     ProcessingCancellationReturnsProcessorsAndRemovesInstruction)
   52:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
   53: 
   54:             TestLog.Write(
   55:                 $"End-to-end processing tests passed: " +
   56:                 $"{Passed.Count}/{Passed.Count}");
   57:         }
   58: 
   59: private static async Task
   60:     InstructionFlowsThroughCompleteProcessingRoute()
   61: {
   62:     var component =
   63:         CreateComponent(
   64:             "certification.end-to-end.success");
   65: 
   66:     using var engine =
   67:         CreateEngine(
   68:             component);
   69: 
   70:     Assert.True(
   71:         engine.Initialize());
   72: 
   73:     var instruction =
   74:         new SetContextVariableInstruction(
   75:             "PlayerHealth",
   76:             10);
   77: 
   78:     IInstructionsRegistry? observedInstructionRegistry =
   79:         null;
   80: 
   81:     ITaskRegistry? observedTaskRegistry =
   82:         null;
   83: 
   84:     ITaskProcessor? observedTaskProcessor =
   85:         null;
   86: 
   87:     SetContextVariable? observedTask =
   88:         null;
   89: 
   90:     TestLog.Write(
   91:         $"[PARK] Instruction {instruction.Id}");
   92: 
   93:     var result =
   94:         await engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
   95:                 async (
   96:                     instructionProcessor,
   97:                     cancellationToken) =>
   98:                 {
   99:                     cancellationToken
  100:                         .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  101: 
  102:                     Assert.Same(
  103:                         component,
  104:                         instructionProcessor.Component);
  105: 
  106:                     observedInstructionRegistry =
  107:                         instructionProcessor.Registry;
  108: 
  109:                     Assert.Same(
  110:                         component,
  111:                         observedInstructionRegistry.Component);
  112: 
  113:                     Assert.True(
  114:                         observedInstructionRegistry.Register(
  115:                             instruction));
  116: 
  117:                     Assert.True(
  118:                         observedInstructionRegistry.TryGet(
  119:                             instruction.Id,
  120:                             out var parkedInstruction));
  121: 
  122:                     Assert.Same(
  123:                         instruction,
  124:                         parkedInstruction);
  125: 
  126:                     TestLog.Write(
  127:                         $"[READ] Instruction {instruction.Id}");
  128: 
  129:                     try
  130:                     {
  131:                         using var taskLease =
  132:                             await instructionProcessor
  133:                                 .RentTaskProcessorAsync(
  134:                                     cancellationToken)
  135:                                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
  136: 
  137:                         cancellationToken
  138:                             .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  139: 
  140:                         observedTaskProcessor =
  141:                             taskLease.Instance;
  142: 
  143:                         observedTaskRegistry =
  144:                             observedTaskProcessor.Registry;
  145: 
  146:                         Assert.NotNull(
  147:                             observedTaskProcessor);
  148: 
  149:                         Assert.NotNull(
  150:                             observedTaskRegistry);
  151: 
  152:                         Assert.Same(
  153:                             component,
  154:                             observedTaskProcessor.Component);
  155: 
  156:                         Assert.Same(
  157:                             component,
  158:                             observedTaskRegistry.Component);
  159: 
  160:                         observedTask =
  161:                             new SetContextVariable(
  162:                                 component,
  163:                                 component.State.Context,
  164:                                 instruction.Key,
  165:                                 instruction.Value);
  166: 
  167:                         Assert.Same(
  168:                             component,
  169:                             observedTask.Component);
  170: 
  171:                         Assert.Same(
  172:                             component.State.Context,
  173:                             observedTask.Context);
  174: 
  175:                         Assert.True(
  176:                             observedTaskProcessor.RegisterTask(
  177:                                 observedTask));
  178: 
  179:                         Assert.True(
  180:                             observedTaskProcessor.ContainsTask(
  181:                                 observedTask.Id));
  182: 
  183:                         Assert.True(
  184:                             observedTaskProcessor.TryGetTask(
  185:                                 observedTask.Id,
  186:                                 out var parkedTask));
  187: 
  188:                         Assert.Same(
  189:                             observedTask,
  190:                             parkedTask);
  191: 
  192:                         TestLog.Write(
  193:                             $"[TASK] Parked task {observedTask.Id}");
  194: 
  195:                         try
  196:                         {
  197:                             cancellationToken
  198:                                 .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  199: 
  200:                             Assert.True(
  201:                                 component.State.Process(
  202:                                     instruction));
  203: 
  204:                             Assert.True(
  205:                                 component.State.Context.Variables
  206:                                     .TryGetValue(
  207:                                         instruction.Key,
  208:                                         out var processedValue));
  209: 
  210:                             Assert.Equal(
  211:                                 instruction.Value,
  212:                                 processedValue);
  213: 
  214:                             TestLog.State(
  215:                                 $"{instruction.Key}={processedValue}");
  216: 
  217:                             return Assert.IsType<int>(
  218:                                 processedValue);
  219:                         }
  220:                         finally
  221:                         {
  222:                             Assert.True(
  223:                                 observedTaskProcessor.RemoveTask(
  224:                                     observedTask.Id));
  225: 
  226:                             TestLog.Write(
  227:                                 $"[TASK] Removed task {observedTask.Id}");
  228:                         }
  229:                     }
  230:                     finally
  231:                     {
  232:                         Assert.True(
  233:                             observedInstructionRegistry.Remove(
  234:                                 instruction.Id));
  235: 
  236:                         TestLog.Write(
  237:                             $"[REMOVE] Instruction {instruction.Id}");
  238:                     }
  239:                 })
  240:             .ConfigureAwait(false);
  241: 
  242:     Assert.Equal(
  243:         10,
  244:         result);
  245: 
  246:     Assert.NotNull(
  247:         observedInstructionRegistry);
  248: 
  249:     Assert.NotNull(
  250:         observedTaskProcessor);
  251: 
  252:     Assert.NotNull(
  253:         observedTaskRegistry);
  254: 
  255:     Assert.NotNull(
  256:         observedTask);
  257: 
  258:     Assert.False(
  259:         observedInstructionRegistry.TryGet(
  260:             instruction.Id,
  261:             out _));
  262: 
  263:     Assert.False(
  264:         observedTaskProcessor.TryGetTask(
  265:             observedTask.Id,
  266:             out _));
  267: 
  268:     Assert.True(
  269:         component.State.Context.Variables
  270:             .TryGetValue(
  271:                 "PlayerHealth",
  272:                 out var finalPlayerHealth));
  273: 
  274:     Assert.Equal(
  275:         10,
  276:         Assert.IsType<int>(
  277:             finalPlayerHealth));
  278: 
  279:     Assert.Equal(
  280:         0,
  281:         engine.Instructions.ActiveCount);
  282: 
  283:     Assert.Equal(
  284:         1,
  285:         engine.Instructions.AvailableCount);
  286: 
  287:     TestLog.State(
  288:         "Successful stateful route complete | " +
  289:         $"PlayerHealth={finalPlayerHealth}, " +
  290:         $"Active={engine.Instructions.ActiveCount}, " +
  291:         $"Available={engine.Instructions.AvailableCount}, " +
  292:         $"Created={engine.Instructions.CreatedCount}");
  293: }
  294: 
  295:         private static async Task
  296:             ProcessingFailureReturnsProcessorsAndRemovesInstruction()
  297:         {
  298:             var component =
  299:                 CreateComponent(
  300:                     "certification.end-to-end.failure");
  301: 
  302:             using var engine =
  303:                 CreateEngine(
  304:                     component);
  305: 
  306:             Assert.True(
  307:                 engine.Initialize());
  308: 
  309:             var instruction =
  310:                 new SetContextVariableInstruction(
  311:                     "PlayerHealth",
  312:                     10);
  313: 
  314:             IInstructionsRegistry? observedInstructionRegistry =
  315:                 null;
  316: 
  317:             ITaskRegistry? observedTaskRegistry =
  318:                 null;
  319: 
  320:             TestLog.Write(
  321:                 $"[PARK] Failure instruction {instruction.Id}");
  322: 
  323:             await Assert.ThrowsAsync<ExpectedProcessingException>(
  324:                 async () =>
  325:                 {
  326:                     await engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  327:                             async (
  328:                                 instructionProcessor,
  329:                                 cancellationToken) =>
  330:                             {
  331:                                 Assert.Same(
  332:                                     component,
  333:                                     instructionProcessor.Component);
  334: 
  335:                                 observedInstructionRegistry =
  336:                                     instructionProcessor.Registry;
  337: 
  338:                                 Assert.Same(
  339:                                     component,
  340:                                     observedInstructionRegistry.Component);
  341: 
  342:                                 Assert.True(
  343:                                     observedInstructionRegistry.Register(
  344:                                         instruction));
  345: 
  346:                                 try
  347:                                 {
  348:                                     using var taskLease =
  349:                                         await instructionProcessor
  350:                                             .RentTaskProcessorAsync(
  351:                                                 cancellationToken)
  352:                                             .ConfigureAwait(false);
  353: 
  354:                                     Assert.Same(
  355:                                         component,
  356:                                         taskLease.Instance.Component);
  357: 
  358:                                     observedTaskRegistry =
  359:                                         taskLease.Instance.Registry;
  360: 
  361:                                     Assert.NotNull(
  362:                                         observedTaskRegistry);
  363: 
  364:                                     Assert.Same(
  365:                                         component,
  366:                                         observedTaskRegistry.Component);
  367: 
  368:                                     throw new ExpectedProcessingException();
  369:                                 }
  370:                                 finally
  371:                                 {
  372:                                     Assert.True(
  373:                                         observedInstructionRegistry.Remove(
  374:                                             instruction.Id));
  375: 
  376:                                     TestLog.Write(
  377:                                         $"[REMOVE] Failure instruction " +
  378:                                         $"{instruction.Id}");
  379:                                 }
  380:                             })
  381:                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  382:                 });
  383: 
  384:             TestLog.Expected(
  385:                 nameof(ExpectedProcessingException));
  386: 
  387:             Assert.NotNull(
  388:                 observedInstructionRegistry);
  389: 
  390:             Assert.NotNull(
  391:                 observedTaskRegistry);
  392: 
  393:             Assert.False(
  394:                 observedInstructionRegistry.TryGet(
  395:                     instruction.Id,
  396:                     out _));
  397: 
  398:             Assert.Equal(
  399:                 0,
  400:                 engine.Instructions.ActiveCount);
  401: 
  402:             Assert.Equal(
  403:                 1,
  404:                 engine.Instructions.AvailableCount);
  405: 
  406:             TestLog.State(
  407:                 "Failure cleanup complete | " +
  408:                 $"Active={engine.Instructions.ActiveCount}, " +
  409:                 $"Available={engine.Instructions.AvailableCount}");
  410: 
  411:             var recoveryResult =
  412:                 await ExecuteRecoveryAsync(
  413:                         engine,
  414:                         component)
  415:                     .ConfigureAwait(false);
  416: 
  417:             Assert.Equal(
  418:                 42,
  419:                 recoveryResult);
  420: 
  421:             Assert.Equal(
  422:                 0,
  423:                 engine.Instructions.ActiveCount);
  424: 
  425:             TestLog.State(
  426:                 "Engine recovered after processing failure");
  427:         }
  428: 
  429:         private static async Task
  430:             ProcessingCancellationReturnsProcessorsAndRemovesInstruction()
  431:         {
  432:             var component =
  433:                 CreateComponent(
  434:                     "certification.end-to-end.cancellation");
  435: 
  436:             using var engine =
  437:                 CreateEngine(
  438:                     component);
  439: 
  440:             Assert.True(
  441:                 engine.Initialize());
  442: 
  443:             var instruction =
  444:                 new SetContextVariableInstruction(
  445:                     "PlayerHealth",
  446:                     10);
  447: 
  448:             IInstructionsRegistry? observedInstructionRegistry =
  449:                 null;
  450: 
  451:             ITaskRegistry? observedTaskRegistry =
  452:                 null;
  453: 
  454:             var enteredProcessing =
  455:                 new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(
  456:                     TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
  457: 
  458:             using var cancellation =
  459:                 new CancellationTokenSource();
  460: 
  461:             TestLog.Write(
  462:                 $"[PARK] Cancellation instruction {instruction.Id}");
  463: 
  464:             var processing =
  465:                 engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  466:                         async (
  467:                             instructionProcessor,
  468:                             cancellationToken) =>
  469:                         {
  470:                             Assert.Same(
  471:                                 component,
  472:                                 instructionProcessor.Component);
  473: 
  474:                             observedInstructionRegistry =
  475:                                 instructionProcessor.Registry;
  476: 
  477:                             Assert.Same(
  478:                                 component,
  479:                                 observedInstructionRegistry.Component);
  480: 
  481:                             Assert.True(
  482:                                 observedInstructionRegistry.Register(
  483:                                     instruction));
  484: 
  485:                             try
  486:                             {
  487:                                 using var taskLease =
  488:                                     await instructionProcessor
  489:                                         .RentTaskProcessorAsync(
  490:                                             cancellationToken)
  491:                                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  492: 
  493:                                 Assert.Same(
  494:                                     component,
  495:                                     taskLease.Instance.Component);
  496: 
  497:                                 observedTaskRegistry =
  498:                                     taskLease.Instance.Registry;
  499: 
  500:                                 Assert.NotNull(
  501:                                     observedTaskRegistry);
  502: 
  503:                                 Assert.Same(
  504:                                     component,
  505:                                     observedTaskRegistry.Component);
  506: 
  507:                                 enteredProcessing.TrySetResult(
  508:                                     true);
  509: 
  510:                                 await Task.Delay(
  511:                                         Timeout.Infinite,
  512:                                         cancellationToken)
  513:                                     .ConfigureAwait(false);
  514: 
  515:                                 return 10;
  516:                             }
  517:                             finally
  518:                             {
  519:                                 Assert.True(
  520:                                     observedInstructionRegistry.Remove(
  521:                                         instruction.Id));
  522: 
  523:                                 TestLog.Write(
  524:                                     $"[REMOVE] Cancellation instruction " +
  525:                                     $"{instruction.Id}");
  526:                             }
  527:                         },
  528:                         cancellation.Token)
  529:                     .AsTask();
  530: 
  531:             await enteredProcessing.Task
  532:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
  533: 
  534:             TestLog.Write(
  535:                 "[TASK] Cancelling active processing route");
  536: 
  537:             cancellation.Cancel();
  538: 
  539:             await Assert.ThrowsAnyAsync<OperationCanceledException>(
  540:                 async () =>
  541:                 {
  542:                     await processing
  543:                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  544:                 });
  545: 
  546:             TestLog.Expected(
  547:                 nameof(OperationCanceledException));
  548: 
  549:             Assert.NotNull(
  550:                 observedInstructionRegistry);
  551: 
  552:             Assert.NotNull(
  553:                 observedTaskRegistry);
  554: 
  555:             Assert.False(
  556:                 observedInstructionRegistry.TryGet(
  557:                     instruction.Id,
  558:                     out _));
  559: 
  560:             Assert.Equal(
  561:                 0,
  562:                 engine.Instructions.ActiveCount);
  563: 
  564:             Assert.Equal(
  565:                 1,
  566:                 engine.Instructions.AvailableCount);
  567: 
  568:             TestLog.State(
  569:                 "Cancellation cleanup complete | " +
  570:                 $"Active={engine.Instructions.ActiveCount}, " +
  571:                 $"Available={engine.Instructions.AvailableCount}");
  572: 
  573:             var recoveryResult =
  574:                 await ExecuteRecoveryAsync(
  575:                         engine,
  576:                         component)
  577:                     .ConfigureAwait(false);
  578: 
  579:             Assert.Equal(
  580:                 42,
  581:                 recoveryResult);
  582: 
  583:             Assert.Equal(
  584:                 0,
  585:                 engine.Instructions.ActiveCount);
  586: 
  587:             TestLog.State(
  588:                 "Engine recovered after processing cancellation");
  589:         }
  590: 
  591:         private static async Task<int> ExecuteRecoveryAsync(
  592:             Core.WildsEngine engine,
  593:             IComponent component)
  594:         {
  595:             return await engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  596:                     async (
  597:                         instructionProcessor,
  598:                         cancellationToken) =>
  599:                     {
  600:                         Assert.Same(
  601:                             component,
  602:                             instructionProcessor.Component);
  603: 
  604:                         Assert.Same(
  605:                             component,
  606:                             instructionProcessor.Registry.Component);
  607: 
  608:                         using var taskLease =
  609:                             await instructionProcessor
  610:                                 .RentTaskProcessorAsync(
  611:                                     cancellationToken)
  612:                                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
  613: 
  614:                         Assert.NotNull(
  615:                             taskLease.Instance);
  616: 
  617:                         Assert.NotNull(
  618:                             taskLease.Instance.Registry);
  619: 
  620:                         Assert.Same(
  621:                             component,
  622:                             taskLease.Instance.Component);
  623: 
  624:                         Assert.Same(
  625:                             component,
  626:                             taskLease.Instance.Registry.Component);
  627: 
  628:                         return 42;
  629:                     })
  630:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
  631:         }
  632: 
  633:         private static Core.WildsEngine CreateEngine(
  634:             IComponent component)
  635:         {
  636:             return new Core.WildsEngine(
  637:                 component,
  638:                 CreateOptions());
  639:         }
  640: 
  641:         private static IComponent CreateComponent(
  642:             string layer)
  643:         {
  644:             return new TestComponent(
  645:                 layer);
  646:         }
  647: 
  648:         private static WildsEngineOptions CreateOptions()
  649:         {
  650:             return new WildsEngineOptions
  651:             {
  652:                 Layer =
  653:                     "certification.end-to-end",
  654: 
  655:                 RetainedInstructionsProcessors =
  656:                     1,
  657: 
  658:                 ConcurrentInstructionsProcessors =
  659:                     1,
  660: 
  661:                 RetainedTaskProcessors =
  662:                     1,
  663: 
  664:                 ConcurrentTaskProcessors =
  665:                     1
  666:             };
  667:         }
  668: 
  669:         private static async Task RunAsync(
  670:             string name,
  671:             Func<Task> test)
  672:         {
  673:             TestLog.Test(
  674:                 name);
  675: 
  676:             try
  677:             {
  678:                 await test()
  679:                     .ConfigureAwait(false);
  680: 
  681:                 Passed.Add(
  682:                     name);
  683: 
  684:                 TestLog.Pass(
  685:                     name);
  686:             }
  687:             catch (Exception exception)
  688:             {
  689:                 TestLog.Fail(
  690:                     name,
  691:                     exception);
  692: 
  693:                 throw;
  694:             }
  695:         }

  698:             Exception
  699:         {
  700:         }
  701:     }

    1: using System.Linq;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators;

    9: public class NeighborTopologyTests
   10: {
   11:     [Fact]
   12:     public void Topology_CanMutateThroughState_AndBeQueriedThroughOperators()
   13:     {
   14:         TestLog.Section(
   15:             "NEIGHBOR TOPOLOGY - STATE MUTATION AND OPERATOR QUERY");
   16: 
   17:         TestLog.Test(
   18:             "Creating independent topology objects A, B, C, and D.");
   19: 
   20:         var topology =
   21:             new NeighborTopologyFixture();
   22: 
   23:         TestLog.State(
   24:             $"A = {topology.A.Slug}");
   25: 
   26:         TestLog.State(
   27:             $"B = {topology.B.Slug}");
   28: 
   29:         TestLog.State(
   30:             $"C = {topology.C.Slug}");
   31: 
   32:         TestLog.State(
   33:             $"D = {topology.D.Slug}");
   34: 
   35:         TestLog.Task(
   36:             "Building topology through A.State.Process().");
   37: 
   38:         Assert.True(
   39:             topology.A.State.Process(
   40:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
   41:                     topology.B,
   42:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
   43:                     6.0)));
   44: 
   45:         TestLog.State(
   46:             "A -> B | membership=local | distance=6");
   47: 
   48:         Assert.True(
   49:             topology.A.State.Process(
   50:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
   51:                     topology.C,
   52:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
   53:                     2.0)));
   54: 
   55:         TestLog.State(
   56:             "A -> C | membership=local | distance=2");
   57: 
   58:         Assert.True(
   59:             topology.A.State.Process(
   60:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
   61:                     topology.D,
   62:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.RemoteMembership,
   63:                     1.0)));
   64: 
   65:         TestLog.State(
   66:             "A -> D | membership=remote | distance=1");
   67: 
   68:         TestLog.Expected(
   69:             "A should now contain exactly three Neighbor relationships.");
   70: 
   71:         Assert.Equal(
   72:             3,
   73:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
   74: 
   75:         Assert.True(
   76:             topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
   77:                 topology.B.Id,
   78:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
   79: 
   80:         Assert.True(
   81:             topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
   82:                 topology.C.Id,
   83:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
   84: 
   85:         Assert.True(
   86:             topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
   87:                 topology.D.Id,
   88:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.RemoteMembership));
   89: 
   90:         TestLog.Pass(
   91:             "Topology established through the state machine.");
   92: 
   93:         var originalOrder =
   94:             topology.A.Neighbors
   95:                 .Relationships
   96:                 .Select(
   97:                     relationship =>
   98:                         relationship.Neighbor.Id)
   99:                 .ToArray();
  100: 
  101:         TestLog.Task(
  102:             "Querying topology through ApplyNeighborOperator().");
  103: 
  104:         var nearest =
  105:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  106:                 new NearestNeighbor());
  107: 
  108:         var localNeighbors =
  109:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  110:                 new MembershipNeighborFilter(
  111:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  112: 
  113:         var orderedNeighbors =
  114:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  115:                 new NearestNeighborOrder());
  116: 
  117:         TestLog.Expected(
  118:             "D should be the nearest Neighbor at distance 1.");
  119: 
  120:         Assert.NotNull(
  121:             nearest);
  122: 
  123:         Assert.Equal(
  124:             topology.D.Id,
  125:             nearest!.Neighbor.Id);
  126: 
  127:         Assert.Equal(
  128:             1.0,
  129:             nearest.Distance);
  130: 
  131:         TestLog.State(
  132:             $"Nearest Neighbor = {nearest.Neighbor.Slug} | distance={nearest.Distance}");
  133: 
  134:         TestLog.Expected(
  135:             "The local membership should contain exactly B and C.");
  136: 
  137:         Assert.Equal(
  138:             2,
  139:             localNeighbors.Count);
  140: 
  141:         Assert.All(
  142:             localNeighbors,
  143:             relationship =>
  144:                 Assert.Equal(
  145:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  146:                     relationship.Membership));
  147: 
  148:         TestLog.State(
  149:             $"Local Neighbor count = {localNeighbors.Count}");
  150: 
  151:         Assert.Equal(
  152:             topology.D.Id,
  153:             orderedNeighbors[0].Neighbor.Id);
  154: 
  155:         TestLog.Pass(
  156:             "Topology operators returned the expected initial results.");
  157: 
  158:         TestLog.Task(
  159:             "Verifying query operators did not mutate source topology.");
  160: 
  161:         Assert.Equal(
  162:             originalOrder,
  163:             topology.A.Neighbors
  164:                 .Relationships
  165:                 .Select(
  166:                     relationship =>
  167:                         relationship.Neighbor.Id)
  168:                 .ToArray());
  169: 
  170:         Assert.Equal(
  171:             3,
  172:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  173: 
  174:         TestLog.Pass(
  175:             "Operator queries preserved source topology.");
  176: 
  177:         TestLog.Task(
  178:             "Moving B from distance 6 to distance 0.5 through the state machine.");
  179: 
  180:         Assert.True(
  181:             topology.A.State.Process(
  182:                 new SetNeighborDistanceInstruction(
  183:                     topology.B.Id,
  184:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  185:                     0.5)));
  186: 
  187:         var nearestAfterDistanceChange =
  188:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  189:                 new NearestNeighbor());
  190: 
  191:         TestLog.Expected(
  192:             "B should now replace D as the nearest Neighbor.");
  193: 
  194:         Assert.NotNull(
  195:             nearestAfterDistanceChange);
  196: 
  197:         Assert.Equal(
  198:             topology.B.Id,
  199:             nearestAfterDistanceChange!.Neighbor.Id);
  200: 
  201:         Assert.Equal(
  202:             0.5,
  203:             nearestAfterDistanceChange.Distance);
  204: 
  205:         TestLog.State(
  206:             $"Nearest Neighbor = {nearestAfterDistanceChange.Neighbor.Slug} | distance={nearestAfterDistanceChange.Distance}");
  207: 
  208:         TestLog.Pass(
  209:             "Topology operators immediately observed the state-driven distance change.");
  210: 
  211:         TestLog.Task(
  212:             "Removing B through the state machine.");
  213: 
  214:         Assert.True(
  215:             topology.A.State.Process(
  216:                 new RemoveNeighborInstruction(
  217:                     topology.B.Id,
  218:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership)));
  219: 
  220:         Assert.False(
  221:             topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  222:                 topology.B.Id,
  223:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  224: 
  225:         Assert.Equal(
  226:             2,
  227:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  228: 
  229:         TestLog.State(
  230:             $"Neighbor count after B removal = {topology.A.Neighbors.Count}");
  231: 
  232:         var nearestAfterRemoval =
  233:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  234:                 new NearestNeighbor());
  235: 
  236:         TestLog.Expected(
  237:             "D should become the nearest Neighbor again after B is removed.");
  238: 
  239:         Assert.NotNull(
  240:             nearestAfterRemoval);
  241: 
  242:         Assert.Equal(
  243:             topology.D.Id,
  244:             nearestAfterRemoval!.Neighbor.Id);
  245: 
  246:         TestLog.State(
  247:             $"Nearest Neighbor = {nearestAfterRemoval.Neighbor.Slug} | distance={nearestAfterRemoval.Distance}");
  248: 
  249:         TestLog.Pass(
  250:             "Removal was immediately reflected by subsequent topology computation.");
  251: 
  252:         TestLog.Task(
  253:             "Running repeated topology queries and checking source integrity.");
  254: 
  255:         var countBeforeFinalQueries =
  256:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count;
  257: 
  258:         _ =
  259:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  260:                 new NearestNeighborOrder());
  261: 
  262:         _ =
  263:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  264:                 new MembershipNeighborFilter(
  265:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  266: 
  267:         _ =
  268:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  269:                 new NeighborCount());
  270: 
  271:         Assert.Equal(
  272:             countBeforeFinalQueries,
  273:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  274: 
  275:         TestLog.Pass(
  276:             "Repeated topology queries did not mutate the Neighbor collection.");
  277: 
  278:         TestLog.Pass(
  279:             "Neighbor topology vertical-slice certification completed successfully.");
  280:     }
  281:     
  282:     [Fact]
  283:     public void Topology_DuplicateRelationship_IsRejectedDeterministically()
  284:     {
  285:         TestLog.Section(
  286:             "NEIGHBOR TOPOLOGY - DUPLICATE RELATIONSHIP IDENTITY");
  287: 
  288:         TestLog.Test(
  289:             "Proving Neighbor.Id + Membership defines relationship identity.");
  290: 
  291:         var topology =
  292:             new NeighborTopologyFixture();
  293: 
  294:         TestLog.Task(
  295:             "Adding A -> B as a local Neighbor at distance 6.");
  296: 
  297:         var firstAdd =
  298:             topology.A.State.Process(
  299:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  300:                     topology.B,
  301:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  302:                     6.0));
  303: 
  304:         Assert.True(
  305:             firstAdd);
  306: 
  307:         Assert.Equal(
  308:             1,
  309:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  310: 
  311:         TestLog.Pass(
  312:             "Initial relationship was accepted.");
  313: 
  314:         TestLog.Task(
  315:             "Attempting to add the same Neighbor and membership again.");
  316: 
  317:         var duplicateAdd =
  318:             topology.A.State.Process(
  319:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  320:                     topology.B,
  321:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  322:                     2.0));
  323: 
  324:         TestLog.Expected(
  325:             "The duplicate relationship should be rejected and the original relationship preserved.");
  326: 
  327:         Assert.False(
  328:             duplicateAdd);
  329: 
  330:         Assert.Equal(
  331:             1,
  332:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  333: 
  334:         Assert.True(
  335:             topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
  336:                 topology.B.Id,
  337:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  338:                 out var relationship));
  339: 
  340:         Assert.NotNull(
  341:             relationship);
  342: 
  343:         Assert.Equal(
  344:             6.0,
  345:             relationship!.Distance);
  346: 
  347:         TestLog.State(
  348:             $"Relationship count = {topology.A.Neighbors.Count}");
  349: 
  350:         TestLog.State(
  351:             $"Preserved distance = {relationship.Distance}");
  352: 
  353:         TestLog.Pass(
  354:             "Duplicate relationship was rejected without changing existing relationship state.");
  355:     }
  356: 
  357:     [Fact]
  358:     public void Topology_SameNeighbor_CanParticipateInDistinctMemberships()
  359:     {
  360:         TestLog.Section(
  361:             "NEIGHBOR TOPOLOGY - DISTINCT MEMBERSHIPS");
  362: 
  363:         TestLog.Test(
  364:             "Proving one Neighbor may participate through more than one membership.");
  365: 
  366:         var topology =
  367:             new NeighborTopologyFixture();
  368: 
  369:         TestLog.Task(
  370:             "Adding B to A through the local membership.");
  371: 
  372:         Assert.True(
  373:             topology.A.State.Process(
  374:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  375:                     topology.B,
  376:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  377:                     6.0)));
  378: 
  379:         TestLog.Task(
  380:             "Adding the same B to A through the remote membership.");
  381: 
  382:         Assert.True(
  383:             topology.A.State.Process(
  384:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  385:                     topology.B,
  386:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.RemoteMembership,
  387:                     12.0)));
  388: 
  389:         TestLog.Expected(
  390:             "A should contain two relationships to B distinguished by membership.");
  391: 
  392:         Assert.Equal(
  393:             2,
  394:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  395: 
  396:         Assert.True(
  397:             topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  398:                 topology.B.Id,
  399:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  400: 
  401:         Assert.True(
  402:             topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  403:                 topology.B.Id,
  404:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.RemoteMembership));
  405: 
  406:         Assert.True(
  407:             topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
  408:                 topology.B.Id,
  409:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  410:                 out var localRelationship));
  411: 
  412:         Assert.True(
  413:             topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
  414:                 topology.B.Id,
  415:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.RemoteMembership,
  416:                 out var remoteRelationship));
  417: 
  418:         Assert.NotNull(
  419:             localRelationship);
  420: 
  421:         Assert.NotNull(
  422:             remoteRelationship);
  423: 
  424:         Assert.Equal(
  425:             6.0,
  426:             localRelationship!.Distance);
  427: 
  428:         Assert.Equal(
  429:             12.0,
  430:             remoteRelationship!.Distance);
  431: 
  432:         TestLog.State(
  433:             $"B local distance = {localRelationship.Distance}");
  434: 
  435:         TestLog.State(
  436:             $"B remote distance = {remoteRelationship.Distance}");
  437: 
  438:         TestLog.Task(
  439:             "Removing only the local relationship.");
  440: 
  441:         Assert.True(
  442:             topology.A.State.Process(
  443:                 new RemoveNeighborInstruction(
  444:                     topology.B.Id,
  445:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership)));
  446: 
  447:         TestLog.Expected(
  448:             "Removing one membership must not remove another membership to the same Neighbor.");
  449: 
  450:         Assert.False(
  451:             topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  452:                 topology.B.Id,
  453:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  454: 
  455:         Assert.True(
  456:             topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  457:                 topology.B.Id,
  458:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.RemoteMembership));
  459: 
  460:         Assert.Equal(
  461:             1,
  462:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  463: 
  464:         TestLog.Pass(
  465:             "Membership identities remained independent.");
  466:     }
  467: 
  468:     [Fact]
  469:     public void Topology_OperatorPipeline_IsDeterministic_AndPreservesSource()
  470:     {
  471:         TestLog.Section(
  472:             "NEIGHBOR TOPOLOGY - OPERATOR PIPELINE DETERMINISM");
  473: 
  474:         TestLog.Test(
  475:             "Proving composed topology operations are deterministic and source-safe.");
  476: 
  477:         var topology =
  478:             new NeighborTopologyFixture();
  479: 
  480:         Assert.True(
  481:             topology.A.State.Process(
  482:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  483:                     topology.B,
  484:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  485:                     6.0)));
  486: 
  487:         Assert.True(
  488:             topology.A.State.Process(
  489:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  490:                     topology.C,
  491:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  492:                     2.0)));
  493: 
  494:         Assert.True(
  495:             topology.A.State.Process(
  496:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  497:                     topology.D,
  498:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.RemoteMembership,
  499:                     1.0)));
  500: 
  501:         var sourceBefore =
  502:             topology.A.Neighbors
  503:                 .Relationships
  504:                 .Select(
  505:                     relationship =>
  506:                         (
  507:                             relationship.Neighbor.Id,
  508:                             relationship.Membership,
  509:                             relationship.Distance))
  510:                 .ToArray();
  511: 
  512:         TestLog.State(
  513:             $"Source relationship count = {sourceBefore.Length}");
  514: 
  515:         TestLog.Task(
  516:             "Building local -> nearest-first topology pipeline.");
  517: 
  518:         var pipeline =
  519:             new NeighborPipeline(
  520:                 new MembershipNeighborFilter(
  521:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership),
  522:                 new NearestNeighborOrder());
  523: 
  524:         var first =
  525:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  526:                 pipeline);
  527: 
  528:         var second =
  529:             topology.A.ApplyNeighborOperator(
  530:                 pipeline);
  531: 
  532:         TestLog.Expected(
  533:             "Both executions should produce C then B.");
  534: 
  535:         Assert.Equal(
  536:             2,
  537:             first.Count);
  538: 
  539:         Assert.Equal(
  540:             2,
  541:             second.Count);
  542: 
  543:         Assert.Equal(
  544:             topology.C.Id,
  545:             first[0].Neighbor.Id);
  546: 
  547:         Assert.Equal(
  548:             topology.B.Id,
  549:             first[1].Neighbor.Id);
  550: 
  551:         Assert.Equal(
  552:             first.Select(
  553:                 relationship =>
  554:                     relationship.Neighbor.Id),
  555:             second.Select(
  556:                 relationship =>
  557:                     relationship.Neighbor.Id));
  558: 
  559:         TestLog.State(
  560:             $"Pipeline result 1 = {first[0].Neighbor.Slug} -> {first[1].Neighbor.Slug}");
  561: 
  562:         TestLog.State(
  563:             $"Pipeline result 2 = {second[0].Neighbor.Slug} -> {second[1].Neighbor.Slug}");
  564: 
  565:         TestLog.Task(
  566:             "Comparing source topology before and after pipeline execution.");
  567: 
  568:         var sourceAfter =
  569:             topology.A.Neighbors
  570:                 .Relationships
  571:                 .Select(
  572:                     relationship =>
  573:                         (
  574:                             relationship.Neighbor.Id,
  575:                             relationship.Membership,
  576:                             relationship.Distance))
  577:                 .ToArray();
  578: 
  579:         Assert.Equal(
  580:             sourceBefore,
  581:             sourceAfter);
  582: 
  583:         Assert.Equal(
  584:             3,
  585:             topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  586: 
  587:         TestLog.Pass(
  588:             "Operator pipeline was deterministic.");
  589: 
  590:         TestLog.Pass(
  591:             "Operator pipeline preserved the source Neighbor topology.");
  592:     }
  593:     
  594:     [Fact]
  595: public void Topology_Relationships_AreDirectional_AndDoNotImplyReciprocity()
  596: {
  597:     TestLog.Section(
  598:         "NEIGHBOR TOPOLOGY - DIRECTIONAL INDEPENDENCE");
  599: 
  600:     TestLog.Test(
  601:         "Proving A -> B does not implicitly create B -> A.");
  602: 
  603:     var topology =
  604:         new NeighborTopologyFixture();
  605: 
  606:     TestLog.Task(
  607:         "Creating A -> B through the local membership.");
  608: 
  609:     Assert.True(
  610:         topology.A.State.Process(
  611:             new AddNeighborInstruction(
  612:                 topology.B,
  613:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  614:                 4.0)));
  615: 
  616:     TestLog.Expected(
  617:         "A should contain B, while B should remain topologically independent.");
  618: 
  619:     Assert.True(
  620:         topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  621:             topology.B.Id,
  622:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  623: 
  624:     Assert.False(
  625:         topology.B.Neighbors.Contains(
  626:             topology.A.Id,
  627:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  628: 
  629:     Assert.Equal(
  630:         1,
  631:         topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  632: 
  633:     Assert.Equal(
  634:         0,
  635:         topology.B.Neighbors.Count);
  636: 
  637:     TestLog.State(
  638:         $"A Neighbor count = {topology.A.Neighbors.Count}");
  639: 
  640:     TestLog.State(
  641:         $"B Neighbor count = {topology.B.Neighbors.Count}");
  642: 
  643:     TestLog.Pass(
  644:         "A -> B did not implicitly create B -> A.");
  645: 
  646:     TestLog.Task(
  647:         "Creating an explicit B -> A relationship with independent distance.");
  648: 
  649:     Assert.True(
  650:         topology.B.State.Process(
  651:             new AddNeighborInstruction(
  652:                 topology.A,
  653:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  654:                 9.0)));
  655: 
  656:     TestLog.Expected(
  657:         "A -> B and B -> A should now exist as independent relationships.");
  658: 
  659:     Assert.True(
  660:         topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
  661:             topology.B.Id,
  662:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  663:             out var aToB));
  664: 
  665:     Assert.True(
  666:         topology.B.Neighbors.TryGet(
  667:             topology.A.Id,
  668:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  669:             out var bToA));
  670: 
  671:     Assert.NotNull(
  672:         aToB);
  673: 
  674:     Assert.NotNull(
  675:         bToA);
  676: 
  677:     Assert.Equal(
  678:         4.0,
  679:         aToB!.Distance);
  680: 
  681:     Assert.Equal(
  682:         9.0,
  683:         bToA!.Distance);
  684: 
  685:     TestLog.State(
  686:         $"A -> B distance = {aToB.Distance}");
  687: 
  688:     TestLog.State(
  689:         $"B -> A distance = {bToA.Distance}");
  690: 
  691:     TestLog.Pass(
  692:         "Reciprocal topology can exist without sharing relationship state.");
  693: 
  694:     TestLog.Task(
  695:         "Changing A -> B without modifying B -> A.");
  696: 
  697:     Assert.True(
  698:         topology.A.State.Process(
  699:             new SetNeighborDistanceInstruction(
  700:                 topology.B.Id,
  701:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  702:                 1.5)));
  703: 
  704:     Assert.True(
  705:         topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
  706:             topology.B.Id,
  707:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  708:             out aToB));
  709: 
  710:     Assert.True(
  711:         topology.B.Neighbors.TryGet(
  712:             topology.A.Id,
  713:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  714:             out bToA));
  715: 
  716:     Assert.NotNull(
  717:         aToB);
  718: 
  719:     Assert.NotNull(
  720:         bToA);
  721: 
  722:     Assert.Equal(
  723:         1.5,
  724:         aToB!.Distance);
  725: 
  726:     Assert.Equal(
  727:         9.0,
  728:         bToA!.Distance);
  729: 
  730:     TestLog.State(
  731:         $"Updated A -> B distance = {aToB.Distance}");
  732: 
  733:     TestLog.State(
  734:         $"Preserved B -> A distance = {bToA.Distance}");
  735: 
  736:     TestLog.Pass(
  737:         "Directional relationship state remained independent.");
  738: 
  739:     TestLog.Task(
  740:         "Removing A -> B while preserving B -> A.");
  741: 
  742:     Assert.True(
  743:         topology.A.State.Process(
  744:             new RemoveNeighborInstruction(
  745:                 topology.B.Id,
  746:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership)));
  747: 
  748:     Assert.False(
  749:         topology.A.Neighbors.Contains(
  750:             topology.B.Id,
  751:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  752: 
  753:     Assert.True(
  754:         topology.B.Neighbors.Contains(
  755:             topology.A.Id,
  756:             NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership));
  757: 
  758:     Assert.Equal(
  759:         0,
  760:         topology.A.Neighbors.Count);
  761: 
  762:     Assert.Equal(
  763:         1,
  764:         topology.B.Neighbors.Count);
  765: 
  766:     TestLog.Pass(
  767:         "Removing one direction did not alter the reciprocal relationship.");
  768: 
  769:     TestLog.Pass(
  770:         "Neighbor topology directional-independence certification completed successfully.");
  771: }
  772: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\ObjectPoolTests.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Threading;
    3: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Pooling;
    6: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    7: 
    8: namespace WildsEngine.Tests.Pooling
    9: {
   10:     public static class ObjectPoolTests
   11:     {
   12:         private static int _passed;
   13: 
   14:         public static async Task RunAllAsync()
   15:         {
   16:             _passed = 0;
   17:             TestLog.Write("=== ObjectPool Tests ===");
   18:             Run("Rent creates instance", RentCreatesInstance);
   19:             Run("Return retains instance", ReturnRetainsInstance);
   20:             Run("Rent reuses instance", RentReusesInstance);
   21:             Run("Double lease dispose is safe", DoubleLeaseDisposeIsSafe);
   22:             Run("Retention limit is enforced", RetentionLimitIsEnforced);
   23:             Run("Reset is called on return", ResetIsCalledOnReturn);
   24:             Run("Failed reset rejects instance", FailedResetRejectsInstance);
   25:             Run("Dispose clears retained instances", DisposeClearsRetainedInstances);
   26:             Run("Rent after dispose throws", RentAfterDisposeThrows);
   27:             await RunAsync("Concurrent rent is bounded", ConcurrentRentIsBoundedAsync);
   28:             await RunAsync("Waiting rent can be cancelled", WaitingRentCanBeCancelledAsync);
   29:             TestLog.Write($"=== ObjectPool Tests Passed: {_passed}/{_passed} ===");
   30:         }
   31: 
   32:         private static void RentCreatesInstance()
   33:         {
   34:             using var pool = CreatePool();
   35:             using var lease = pool.Rent();
   36:             LogState(pool, "After first rent");
   37:             Assert(lease.Instance != null, "Rent should return an instance.");
   38:             Assert(pool.CreatedCount == 1, "First rent should create one instance.");
   39:             Assert(pool.ActiveCount == 1, "First rent should create one active lease.");
   40:         }
   41: 
   42:         private static void ReturnRetainsInstance()
   43:         {
   44:             using var pool = CreatePool();
   45:             var lease = pool.Rent();
   46:             lease.Dispose();
   47:             LogState(pool, "After return");
   48:             Assert(pool.ActiveCount == 0, "Returned instance should no longer be active.");
   49:             Assert(pool.AvailableCount == 1, "Returned instance should be retained.");
   50:         }
   51: 
   52:         private static void RentReusesInstance()
   53:         {
   54:             using var pool = CreatePool();
   55:             TestPoolObject first;
   56:             using (var lease = pool.Rent()) first = lease.Instance;
   57:             using (var lease = pool.Rent())
   58:                 Assert(ReferenceEquals(first, lease.Instance), "Second rent should reuse retained instance.");
   59:             Assert(pool.CreatedCount == 1, "Reusing an instance should not create another object.");
   60:         }
   61: 
   62:         private static void DoubleLeaseDisposeIsSafe()
   63:         {
   64:             using var pool = CreatePool();
   65:             var lease = pool.Rent();
   66:             lease.Dispose();
   67:             lease.Dispose();
   68:             Assert(pool.ActiveCount == 0, "Double lease disposal must not double-return.");
   69:             Assert(pool.AvailableCount == 1, "Double disposal must retain only one instance.");
   70:         }
   71: 
   72:         private static void RetentionLimitIsEnforced()
   73:         {
   74:             using var pool = new ObjectPool<TestPoolObject>(() => new TestPoolObject(), 1, 2);
   75:             var first = pool.Rent();
   76:             var second = pool.Rent();
   77:             first.Dispose();
   78:             second.Dispose();
   79:             LogState(pool, "After retention test");
   80:             Assert(pool.AvailableCount == 1, "Pool should retain no more than MaximumRetained.");
   81:             Assert(pool.CreatedCount == 2, "Two simultaneous leases should require two instances.");
   82:         }
   83: 
   84:         private static void ResetIsCalledOnReturn()
   85:         {
   86:             using var pool = CreatePool();
   87:             TestPoolObject instance;
   88:             using (var lease = pool.Rent()) { instance = lease.Instance; instance.Value = 42; }
   89:             TestLog.State($"ResetCount = {instance.ResetCount}; Value = {instance.Value}");
   90:             Assert(instance.ResetCount == 1, "Returning pooled object should call TryReset.");
   91:             Assert(instance.Value == 0, "Reset should clear transient state.");
   92:         }
   93: 
   94:         private static void FailedResetRejectsInstance()
   95:         {
   96:             using var pool = new ObjectPool<TestPoolObject>(() => new TestPoolObject { AllowReset = false }, 4, 4);
   97:             using (var lease = pool.Rent()) { }
   98:             Assert(pool.ActiveCount == 0, "Rejected instance should no longer be active.");
   99:             Assert(pool.AvailableCount == 0, "Failed reset should prevent retention.");
  100:         }
  101: 
  102:         private static void DisposeClearsRetainedInstances()
  103:         {
  104:             var pool = CreatePool();
  105:             TestPoolObject instance;
  106:             using (var lease = pool.Rent()) instance = lease.Instance;
  107:             pool.Dispose();
  108:             Assert(pool.AvailableCount == 0, "Disposal should clear retained instances.");
  109:             Assert(instance.IsDisposed, "Retained disposable instance should be disposed.");
  110:         }
  111: 
  112:         private static void RentAfterDisposeThrows()
  113:         {
  114:             var pool = CreatePool();
  115:             pool.Dispose();
  116:             var threw = false;
  117:             try { pool.Rent(); }
  118:             catch (ObjectDisposedException) { threw = true; TestLog.Expected("ObjectDisposedException"); }
  119:             Assert(threw, "Rent after pool disposal should throw.");
  120:         }
  121: 
  122:         private static async Task ConcurrentRentIsBoundedAsync()
  123:         {
  124:             using var pool = new ObjectPool<TestPoolObject>(() => new TestPoolObject(), 2, 2);
  125:             using var first = await pool.RentAsync();
  126:             using var second = await pool.RentAsync();
  127:             using var cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
  128:             var waiting = pool.RentAsync(cancellation.Token);
  129:             Assert(!waiting.IsCompletedSuccessfully, "Additional asynchronous rent should wait.");
  130:             cancellation.Cancel();
  131:             var cancelled = false;
  132:             try { using var ignored = await waiting; }
  133:             catch (OperationCanceledException) { cancelled = true; TestLog.Expected("OperationCanceledException"); }
  134:             Assert(cancelled, "Waiting rent should observe cancellation.");
  135:             Assert(pool.ActiveCount == 2, "Existing leases should remain active.");
  136:         }
  137: 
  138:         private static async Task WaitingRentCanBeCancelledAsync()
  139:         {
  140:             using var pool = new ObjectPool<TestPoolObject>(() => new TestPoolObject(), 1, 1);
  141:             using var active = await pool.RentAsync();
  142:             using var cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
  143:             var waiting = pool.RentAsync(cancellation.Token);
  144:             cancellation.Cancel();
  145:             var cancelled = false;
  146:             try { using var ignored = await waiting; }
  147:             catch (OperationCanceledException) { cancelled = true; TestLog.Expected("OperationCanceledException"); }
  148:             Assert(cancelled, "Cancellation should release a waiting renter.");
  149:             Assert(pool.ActiveCount == 1, "Cancellation must not affect existing lease.");
  150:         }
  151: 
  152:         private static ObjectPool<TestPoolObject> CreatePool() =>
  153:             new ObjectPool<TestPoolObject>(() => new TestPoolObject(), 4, 4);
  154: 
  155:         private static void Run(string name, Action test)
  156:         {
  157:             TestLog.Test(name);
  158:             try { test(); _passed++; TestLog.Pass(name); }
  159:             catch (Exception ex) { TestLog.Fail(name, ex); throw; }
  160:         }
  161: 
  162:         private static async Task RunAsync(string name, Func<Task> test)
  163:         {
  164:             TestLog.Test(name);
  165:             try { await test().ConfigureAwait(false); _passed++; TestLog.Pass(name); }
  166:             catch (Exception ex) { TestLog.Fail(name, ex); throw; }
  167:         }
  168: 
  169:         private static void LogState(ObjectPool<TestPoolObject> pool, string message) =>
  170:             TestLog.State($"{message} | Active={pool.ActiveCount}, Available={pool.AvailableCount}, Created={pool.CreatedCount}, Maximum={pool.MaximumConcurrent}");
  171: 
  172:         private static void Assert(bool condition, string message)
  173:         {
  174:             if (!condition) throw new InvalidOperationException($"ObjectPool test failed: {message}");
  175:         }
  176: 
  177:         private sealed class TestPoolObject : IResettablePoolObject, IDisposable
  178:         {
  179:             public int Value { get; set; }
  180:             public int ResetCount { get; private set; }
  181:             public bool AllowReset { get; set; } = true;
  182:             public bool IsDisposed { get; private set; }
  183:             public bool TryReset() { ResetCount++; if (!AllowReset) return false; Value = 0; return true; }
  184:             public void Dispose() => IsDisposed = true;
  185:         }
  186:     }
  187: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Propagation\NeighborPropagationTests.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Propagation;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Fixtures;
    5: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    6: 
    7: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests;
    8: 
    9: public class NeighborPropagationTests
   10: {
   11:     [Fact]
   12:     public void ExponentialPropagation_AttenuatesIntensity_ByNeighborDistance()
   13:     {
   14:         TestLog.Section(
   15:             "NEIGHBOR PROPAGATION - EXPONENTIAL DISTANCE ATTENUATION");
   16: 
   17:         TestLog.Test(
   18:             "Proving propagation intensity attenuates according to Neighbor distance.");
   19: 
   20:         var topology =
   21:             new NeighborTopologyFixture();
   22: 
   23:         const double sourceIntensity =
   24:             1.0;
   25: 
   26:         const double decay =
   27:             0.5;
   28: 
   29:         TestLog.Task(
   30:             "Creating A -> B at distance 1.");
   31: 
   32:         Assert.True(
   33:             topology.A.State.Process(
   34:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
   35:                     topology.B,
   36:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
   37:                     1.0)));
   38: 
   39:         TestLog.Task(
   40:             "Creating A -> C at distance 3.");
   41: 
   42:         Assert.True(
   43:             topology.A.State.Process(
   44:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
   45:                     topology.C,
   46:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
   47:                     3.0)));
   48: 
   49:         Assert.True(
   50:             topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
   51:                 topology.B.Id,
   52:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
   53:                 out var relationshipB));
   54: 
   55:         Assert.True(
   56:             topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
   57:                 topology.C.Id,
   58:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
   59:                 out var relationshipC));
   60: 
   61:         Assert.NotNull(
   62:             relationshipB);
   63: 
   64:         Assert.NotNull(
   65:             relationshipC);
   66: 
   67:         var propagation =
   68:             new ExponentialDistancePropagation(
   69:                 decay);
   70: 
   71:         TestLog.State(
   72:             $"Source intensity = {sourceIntensity}");
   73: 
   74:         TestLog.State(
   75:             $"Decay = {decay}");
   76: 
   77:         TestLog.State(
   78:             $"B distance = {relationshipB!.Distance}");
   79: 
   80:         TestLog.State(
   81:             $"C distance = {relationshipC!.Distance}");
   82: 
   83:         TestLog.Task(
   84:             "Propagating source intensity independently to B and C.");
   85: 
   86:         var intensityB =
   87:             propagation.Propagate(
   88:                 sourceIntensity,
   89:                 relationshipB);
   90: 
   91:         var intensityC =
   92:             propagation.Propagate(
   93:                 sourceIntensity,
   94:                 relationshipC);
   95: 
   96:         var expectedB =
   97:             sourceIntensity *
   98:             Math.Exp(
   99:                 -decay *
  100:                 relationshipB.Distance);
  101: 
  102:         var expectedC =
  103:             sourceIntensity *
  104:             Math.Exp(
  105:                 -decay *
  106:                 relationshipC.Distance);
  107: 
  108:         TestLog.Expected(
  109:             $"B intensity = {expectedB:R}");
  110: 
  111:         TestLog.Expected(
  112:             $"C intensity = {expectedC:R}");
  113: 
  114:         TestLog.State(
  115:             $"B received intensity = {intensityB:R}");
  116: 
  117:         TestLog.State(
  118:             $"C received intensity = {intensityC:R}");
  119: 
  120:         Assert.Equal(
  121:             expectedB,
  122:             intensityB,
  123:             12);
  124: 
  125:         Assert.Equal(
  126:             expectedC,
  127:             intensityC,
  128:             12);
  129: 
  130:         Assert.True(
  131:             intensityB <
  132:             sourceIntensity);
  133: 
  134:         Assert.True(
  135:             intensityC <
  136:             intensityB);
  137: 
  138:         Assert.True(
  139:             intensityC >
  140:             0.0);
  141: 
  142:         TestLog.Pass(
  143:             "Propagation attenuated monotonically with increasing Neighbor distance.");
  144: 
  145:         TestLog.Pass(
  146:             "Exponential Neighbor propagation certification completed successfully.");
  147:     }
  148: 
  149:     [Fact]
  150:     public void ExponentialPropagation_ZeroDistance_PreservesIntensity()
  151:     {
  152:         TestLog.Section(
  153:             "NEIGHBOR PROPAGATION - ZERO DISTANCE");
  154: 
  155:         var topology =
  156:             new NeighborTopologyFixture();
  157: 
  158:         const double sourceIntensity =
  159:             0.75;
  160: 
  161:         Assert.True(
  162:             topology.A.State.Process(
  163:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  164:                     topology.B,
  165:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  166:                     0.0)));
  167: 
  168:         Assert.True(
  169:             topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
  170:                 topology.B.Id,
  171:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  172:                 out var relationship));
  173: 
  174:         Assert.NotNull(
  175:             relationship);
  176: 
  177:         var propagation =
  178:             new ExponentialDistancePropagation(
  179:                 0.5);
  180: 
  181:         var result =
  182:             propagation.Propagate(
  183:                 sourceIntensity,
  184:                 relationship!);
  185: 
  186:         TestLog.Expected(
  187:             "Distance zero should preserve the complete source intensity.");
  188: 
  189:         TestLog.State(
  190:             $"Source intensity = {sourceIntensity:R}");
  191: 
  192:         TestLog.State(
  193:             $"Received intensity = {result:R}");
  194: 
  195:         Assert.Equal(
  196:             sourceIntensity,
  197:             result,
  198:             12);
  199: 
  200:         TestLog.Pass(
  201:             "Zero-distance propagation preserved source intensity.");
  202:     }

  260:     public void ExponentialPropagation_RejectsNegativeDecayAndIntensity()
  261:     {

  269:             () =>
  270:                 new ExponentialDistancePropagation(
  271:                     -0.1));
  272: 
  273:         TestLog.Pass(
  274:             "Negative decay was rejected.");
  275: 
  276:         var topology =
  277:             new NeighborTopologyFixture();
  278: 
  279:         Assert.True(
  280:             topology.A.State.Process(
  281:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  282:                     topology.B,
  283:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  284:                     1.0)));
  285: 
  286:         Assert.True(

  288:                 topology.B.Id,
  289:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  290:                 out var relationship));
  291:

  293:             relationship);
  294: 
  295:         var propagation =
  296:             new ExponentialDistancePropagation(
  297:                 0.5);
  298: 
  299:         Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(
  300:             () =>
  301:                 propagation.Propagate(
  302:                     -1.0,
  303:                     relationship!));
  304: 
  305:         TestLog.Pass(

  307: 
  308:         TestLog.Pass(
  309:             "Propagation input validation completed successfully.");
  310:     }

  312:     [Fact]
  313:     public void MinimumIntensityEligibility_AcceptsIntensityAtOrAboveThreshold()
  314:     {
  315:         TestLog.Section(
  316:             "NEIGHBOR PROPAGATION - ELIGIBILITY THRESHOLD");
  317: 
  318:         TestLog.Test(
  319:             "Proving the minimum-intensity threshold is inclusive.");
  320: 
  321:         const double threshold =
  322:             0.25;
  323: 
  324:         var eligibility =
  325:             new MinimumIntensityEligibility(
  326:                 threshold);
  327: 
  328:         TestLog.Task(
  329:             "Testing intensity above the threshold.");
  330: 
  331:         Assert.True(
  332:             eligibility.IsEligible(
  333:                 0.50));
  334: 
  335:         TestLog.Pass(
  336:             "Intensity above the threshold was eligible.");
  337: 
  338:         TestLog.Task(
  339:             "Testing intensity exactly at the threshold.");
  340: 
  341:         TestLog.Expected(
  342:             "An intensity equal to the threshold should be eligible.");
  343: 
  344:         Assert.True(
  345:             eligibility.IsEligible(
  346:                 threshold));
  347: 
  348:         TestLog.Pass(
  349:             "Threshold boundary was inclusive.");
  350:     }
  351: 
  352:     [Fact]
  353:     public void MinimumIntensityEligibility_RejectsIntensityBelowThreshold()
  354:     {
  355:         TestLog.Section(
  356:             "NEIGHBOR PROPAGATION - BELOW ELIGIBILITY THRESHOLD");
  357: 
  358:         const double threshold =
  359:             0.25;
  360: 
  361:         var eligibility =
  362:             new MinimumIntensityEligibility(
  363:                 threshold);
  364: 
  365:         var intensity =
  366:             Math.BitDecrement(
  367:                 threshold);
  368: 
  369:         TestLog.State(
  370:             $"Threshold = {threshold:R}");
  371: 
  372:         TestLog.State(
  373:             $"Test intensity = {intensity:R}");
  374: 
  375:         TestLog.Expected(
  376:             "The nearest representable double below the threshold should not be eligible.");
  377: 
  378:         Assert.False(
  379:             eligibility.IsEligible(
  380:                 intensity));
  381: 
  382:         TestLog.Pass(
  383:             "Intensity immediately below the threshold was rejected.");
  384:     }
  385: 
  386:     [Fact]
  387:     public void MinimumIntensityEligibility_ZeroThreshold_AcceptsZeroIntensity()
  388:     {
  389:         TestLog.Section(

  391: 
  392:         TestLog.Test(

  394: 
  395:         var eligibility =
  396:             new MinimumIntensityEligibility(

  405: 
  406:         Assert.True(
