  444:     [Fact]
  445:     public void PropagatedIntensity_DeterminesNeighborEligibility()
  446:     {
  447:         TestLog.Section(
  448:             "NEIGHBOR PROPAGATION - ATTENUATION TO ELIGIBILITY");
  449: 
  450:         TestLog.Test(
  451:             "Proving certified topology and attenuation determine which Neighbors qualify.");
  452: 
  453:         var topology =
  454:             new NeighborTopologyFixture();
  455: 
  456:         const double sourceIntensity =
  457:             1.0;
  458: 
  459:         const double decay =
  460:             0.5;
  461: 
  462:         const double threshold =
  463:             0.25;
  464: 
  465:         Assert.True(
  466:             topology.A.State.Process(
  467:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  468:                     topology.B,
  469:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  470:                     1.0)));
  471: 
  472:         Assert.True(
  473:             topology.A.State.Process(
  474:                 new AddNeighborInstruction(
  475:                     topology.C,
  476:                     NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  477:                     3.0)));
  478: 
  479:         Assert.True(
  480:             topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
  481:                 topology.B.Id,
  482:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  483:                 out var relationshipB));
  484: 
  485:         Assert.True(
  486:             topology.A.Neighbors.TryGet(
  487:                 topology.C.Id,
  488:                 NeighborTopologyFixture.LocalMembership,
  489:                 out var relationshipC));
  490: 
  491:         Assert.NotNull(
  492:             relationshipB);
  493: 
  494:         Assert.NotNull(
  495:             relationshipC);
  496: 
  497:         var propagation =
  498:             new ExponentialDistancePropagation(
  499:                 decay);
  500: 
  501:         var eligibility =
  502:             new MinimumIntensityEligibility(
  503:                 threshold);
  504: 
  505:         var intensityB =
  506:             propagation.Propagate(
  507:                 sourceIntensity,
  508:                 relationshipB!);
  509: 
  510:         var intensityC =
  511:             propagation.Propagate(
  512:                 sourceIntensity,
  513:                 relationshipC!);
  514: 
  515:         TestLog.State(
  516:             $"Source intensity = {sourceIntensity:R}");
  517: 
  518:         TestLog.State(
  519:             $"Eligibility threshold = {threshold:R}");
  520: 
  521:         TestLog.State(
  522:             $"B distance = {relationshipB.Distance:R}");
  523: 
  524:         TestLog.State(
  525:             $"B received intensity = {intensityB:R}");
  526: 
  527:         TestLog.State(
  528:             $"C distance = {relationshipC.Distance:R}");
  529: 
  530:         TestLog.State(
  531:             $"C received intensity = {intensityC:R}");
  532: 
  533:         TestLog.Expected(
  534:             "B should qualify and C should fall below the response threshold.");
  535: 
  536:         Assert.True(
  537:             eligibility.IsEligible(
  538:                 intensityB));
  539: 
  540:         Assert.False(
  541:             eligibility.IsEligible(
  542:                 intensityC));
  543: 
  544:         TestLog.Pass(
  545:             "B was selected as an eligible propagation recipient.");
  546: 
  547:         TestLog.Pass(
  548:             "C remained below the propagation eligibility threshold.");
  549: 
  550:         TestLog.Pass(
  551:             "Topology -> propagation -> eligibility boundary completed successfully.");
  552:     }
  553: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\PulseCoordinatorCertificationTests.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Coordinator;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Units;
    5: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    6: using Xunit;
    7: 
    8: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests
    9: {
   10:     /// <summary>
   11:     /// Certifies Pulse resolution semantics.
   12:     ///
   13:     /// A complete consumer request cannot resolve until
   14:     /// every required internal WorkUnit has completed
   15:     /// successfully.
   16:     /// </summary>
   17:     public sealed class PulseCoordinatorCertificationTests
   18:     {
   19:         [Fact]
   20:         public void PulseCannotResolveUntilAllRequiredWorkCompletes()
   21:         {
   22:             TestLog.Section(
   23:                 "Pulse Coordination Certification");
   24: 
   25:             TestLog.Test(
   26:                 "Pulse waits for every required WorkUnit");
   27: 
   28:             var request =
   29:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
   30:                     "player-request");
   31: 
   32:             var pulse =
   33:                 new Pulse(
   34:                     request);
   35: 
   36:             Assert.True(
   37:                 pulse.TryBeginDecomposition());
   38: 
   39:             var first =
   40:                 CreateWork(
   41:                     pulse,
   42:                     "QSL");
   43: 
   44:             var second =
   45:                 CreateWork(
   46:                     pulse,
   47:                     "Instruction");
   48: 
   49:             var third =
   50:                 CreateWork(
   51:                     pulse,
   52:                     "Task");
   53: 
   54:             var coordinator =
   55:                 new PulseCoordinator(
   56:                     pulse);
   57: 
   58:             Assert.True(
   59:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
   60:                     first));
   61: 
   62:             Assert.True(
   63:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
   64:                     second));
   65: 
   66:             Assert.True(
   67:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
   68:                     third));
   69: 
   70:             TestLog.State(
   71:                 $"Pulse {pulse.Id} owns " +
   72:                 $"{coordinator.WorkUnitCount} required WorkUnits");
   73: 
   74:             Assert.True(
   75:                 pulse.TryMarkPending());
   76: 
   77:             Assert.True(
   78:                 pulse.TryMarkReady());
   79: 
   80:             Assert.True(
   81:                 pulse.TryBeginProcessing());
   82: 
   83:             BeginAndComplete(
   84:                 first,
   85:                 "qsl-result");
   86: 
   87:             TestLog.Task(
   88:                 "WorkUnit A completed");
   89: 
   90:             BeginAndComplete(
   91:                 second,
   92:                 "instruction-result");
   93: 
   94:             TestLog.Task(
   95:                 "WorkUnit B completed");
   96: 
   97:             Assert.True(
   98:                 third.TryBeginProcessing());
   99: 
  100:             TestLog.Task(
  101:                 "WorkUnit C still processing");
  102: 
  103:             Assert.False(
  104:                 coordinator.CanResolve);
  105: 
  106:             Assert.False(
  107:                 coordinator.TryResolve(
  108:                     "too-early",
  109:                     out var earlyMoment));
  110: 
  111:             Assert.Null(
  112:                 earlyMoment);
  113: 
  114:             Assert.Equal(
  115:                 PulseStatus.Processing,
  116:                 pulse.Status);
  117: 
  118:             TestLog.Expected(
  119:                 "Pulse refused early completion");
  120: 
  121:             var finalResult =
  122:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
  123:                     "final-result");
  124: 
  125:             Assert.True(
  126:                 third.TryComplete(
  127:                     finalResult));
  128: 
  129:             TestLog.Task(
  130:                 "WorkUnit C completed");
  131: 
  132:             Assert.True(
  133:                 coordinator.CanResolve);
  134: 
  135:             Assert.True(
  136:                 coordinator.TryResolve(
  137:                     finalResult,
  138:                     out var moment));
  139: 
  140:             Assert.NotNull(
  141:                 moment);
  142: 
  143:             Assert.Equal(
  144:                 PulseStatus.Completed,
  145:                 pulse.Status);
  146: 
  147:             Assert.True(
  148:                 pulse.IsTerminal);
  149: 
  150:             Assert.Equal(
  151:                 pulse.Id,
  152:                 moment!.PulseId);
  153: 
  154:             Assert.Equal(
  155:                 3,
  156:                 moment.WorkUnitIds.Count);
  157: 
  158:             Assert.Contains(
  159:                 first.Id,
  160:                 moment.WorkUnitIds);
  161: 
  162:             Assert.Contains(
  163:                 second.Id,
  164:                 moment.WorkUnitIds);
  165: 
  166:             Assert.Contains(
  167:                 third.Id,
  168:                 moment.WorkUnitIds);
  169: 
  170:             Assert.Same(
  171:                 finalResult,
  172:                 moment.Result);
  173: 
  174:             TestLog.State(
  175:                 "All required WorkUnits resolved");
  176: 
  177:             TestLog.State(
  178:                 $"Moment {moment.Id} closed Pulse {pulse.Id}");
  179: 
  180:             TestLog.Pass(
  181:                 "Pulse completed only after all required work completed");
  182:         }
  183: 
  184:         [Fact]
  185:         public void CoordinatorRejectsWorkFromAnotherPulse()
  186:         {
  187:             var firstPulse =
  188:                 new Pulse(
  189:                     "first");
  190: 
  191:             var secondPulse =
  192:                 new Pulse(
  193:                     "second");
  194: 
  195:             var foreignWork =
  196:                 new WorkUnit(
  197:                     secondPulse.Id,
  198:                     "foreign-work");
  199: 
  200:             var coordinator =
  201:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  202:                     firstPulse);
  203: 
  204:             Assert.False(
  205:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  206:                     foreignWork));
  207: 
  208:             Assert.Equal(
  209:                 0,
  210:                 coordinator.WorkUnitCount);
  211:         }
  212: 
  213:         [Fact]
  214:         public void CoordinatorRejectsDuplicateWorkUnit()
  215:         {
  216:             var pulse =
  217:                 new Pulse(
  218:                     "request");
  219: 
  220:             var work =
  221:                 new WorkUnit(
  222:                     pulse.Id,
  223:                     "work");
  224: 
  225:             var coordinator =
  226:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  227:                     pulse);
  228: 
  229:             Assert.True(
  230:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  231:                     work));
  232: 
  233:             Assert.False(
  234:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  235:                     work));
  236: 
  237:             Assert.Equal(
  238:                 1,
  239:                 coordinator.WorkUnitCount);
  240:         }
  241: 
  242:         [Fact]
  243:         public void FailedWorkPreventsSuccessfulResolution()
  244:         {
  245:             TestLog.Test(
  246:                 "Failed WorkUnit prevents successful Pulse resolution");
  247: 
  248:             var pulse =
  249:                 CreateProcessingPulse(
  250:                     "failure-request");
  251: 
  252:             var completed =
  253:                 CreateWork(
  254:                     pulse,
  255:                     "completed");
  256: 
  257:             var failed =
  258:                 CreateWork(
  259:                     pulse,
  260:                     "failed");
  261: 
  262:             var coordinator =
  263:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  264:                     pulse);
  265: 
  266:             Assert.True(
  267:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  268:                     completed));
  269: 
  270:             Assert.True(
  271:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  272:                     failed));
  273: 
  274:             BeginAndComplete(
  275:                 completed,
  276:                 "completed-result");
  277: 
  278:             Assert.True(
  279:                 failed.TryBeginProcessing());
  280: 
  281:             var failure =
  282:                 new InvalidOperationException(
  283:                     "Expected WorkUnit failure.");
  284: 
  285:             Assert.True(
  286:                 failed.TryFail(
  287:                     failure));
  288: 
  289:             Assert.False(
  290:                 coordinator.CanResolve);
  291: 
  292:             Assert.False(
  293:                 coordinator.TryResolve(
  294:                     "must-not-succeed",
  295:                     out var moment));
  296: 
  297:             Assert.Null(
  298:                 moment);
  299: 
  300:             Assert.Equal(
  301:                 PulseStatus.Processing,
  302:                 pulse.Status);
  303: 
  304:             Assert.Same(
  305:                 failure,
  306:                 failed.Exception);
  307: 
  308:             TestLog.Expected(
  309:                 "Failed WorkUnit blocked successful Pulse resolution");
  310: 
  311:             TestLog.Pass(
  312:                 "Failure cannot masquerade as successful completion");
  313:         }
  314: 
  315:         [Fact]
  316:         public void CancelledWorkPreventsSuccessfulResolution()
  317:         {
  318:             TestLog.Test(
  319:                 "Cancelled WorkUnit prevents successful Pulse resolution");
  320: 
  321:             var pulse =
  322:                 CreateProcessingPulse(
  323:                     "cancellation-request");
  324: 
  325:             var completed =
  326:                 CreateWork(
  327:                     pulse,
  328:                     "completed");
  329: 
  330:             var cancelled =
  331:                 CreateWork(
  332:                     pulse,
  333:                     "cancelled");
  334: 
  335:             var coordinator =
  336:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  337:                     pulse);
  338: 
  339:             Assert.True(
  340:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  341:                     completed));
  342: 
  343:             Assert.True(
  344:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  345:                     cancelled));
  346: 
  347:             BeginAndComplete(
  348:                 completed,
  349:                 "completed-result");
  350: 
  351:             Assert.Equal(
  352:                 WorkUnitStatus.Pending,
  353:                 cancelled.Status);
  354: 
  355:             Assert.True(
  356:                 cancelled.TryCancel());
  357: 
  358:             Assert.False(
  359:                 coordinator.CanResolve);
  360: 
  361:             Assert.False(
  362:                 coordinator.TryResolve(
  363:                     "must-not-succeed",
  364:                     out var moment));
  365: 
  366:             Assert.Null(
  367:                 moment);
  368: 
  369:             Assert.Equal(
  370:                 PulseStatus.Processing,
  371:                 pulse.Status);
  372: 
  373:             TestLog.Expected(
  374:                 "Cancelled WorkUnit blocked successful Pulse resolution");
  375: 
  376:             TestLog.Pass(
  377:                 "Cancellation cannot masquerade as successful completion");
  378:         }
  379: 
  380:         [Fact]
  381:         public void EmptyCoordinatorCannotResolvePulse()
  382:         {
  383:             var pulse =
  384:                 CreateProcessingPulse(
  385:                     "empty-request");
  386: 
  387:             var coordinator =
  388:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  389:                     pulse);
  390: 
  391:             Assert.Equal(
  392:                 0,
  393:                 coordinator.WorkUnitCount);
  394: 
  395:             Assert.False(
  396:                 coordinator.CanResolve);
  397: 
  398:             Assert.False(
  399:                 coordinator.TryResolve(
  400:                     "result",
  401:                     out var moment));
  402: 
  403:             Assert.Null(
  404:                 moment);
  405: 
  406:             Assert.Equal(
  407:                 PulseStatus.Processing,
  408:                 pulse.Status);
  409:         }
  410: 
  411:         private static Pulse CreateProcessingPulse(
  412:             object? request)
  413:         {
  414:             var pulse =
  415:                 new Pulse(
  416:                     request);
  417: 
  418:             Assert.True(
  419:                 pulse.TryBeginDecomposition());
  420: 
  421:             Assert.True(
  422:                 pulse.TryMarkPending());
  423: 
  424:             Assert.True(
  425:                 pulse.TryMarkReady());
  426: 
  427:             Assert.True(
  428:                 pulse.TryBeginProcessing());
  429: 
  430:             return pulse;
  431:         }
  432: 
  433:         private static WorkUnit CreateWork(
  434:             Pulse pulse,
  435:             object? payload)
  436:         {
  437:             var work =
  438:                 new WorkUnit(
  439:                     pulse.Id,
  440:                     payload);
  441: 
  442:             Assert.True(
  443:                 work.TryMarkPending());
  444: 
  445:             return work;
  446:         }
  447: 
  448:         private static void BeginAndComplete(
  449:             WorkUnit work,
  450:             object? result)
  451:         {
  452:             Assert.True(
  453:                 work.TryBeginProcessing());
  454: 
  455:             Assert.True(
  456:                 work.TryComplete(
  457:                     result));
  458:         }
  459: 
  460:         private sealed class TestRuntimeObject
  461:         {
  462:             public TestRuntimeObject(
  463:                 string name)
  464:             {
  465:                 Name = name;
  466:             }
  467: 
  468:             public string Name { get; }
  469:         }
  470:     }
  471: }

    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Coordinator;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Units;
    5: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;

    8: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests
    9: {
   10:     public sealed class PulseOutcomeCertificationTests
   11:     {
   12:         [Fact]
   13:         public void CompletedWorkProducesCompletedMoment()
   14:         {
   15:             TestLog.Test(
   16:                 "Completed WorkUnits produce a completed Moment");
   17: 
   18:             var pulse =
   19:                 CreateProcessingPulse(
   20:                     "success-request");
   21: 
   22:             var work =
   23:                 CreateProcessingWork(
   24:                     pulse,
   25:                     "success-work");
   26: 
   27:             var result =
   28:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
   29:                     "success-result");
   30: 
   31:             Assert.True(
   32:                 work.TryComplete(
   33:                     result));
   34: 
   35:             var coordinator =
   36:                 new PulseCoordinator(
   37:                     pulse);
   38: 
   39:             Assert.True(
   40:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
   41:                     work));
   42: 
   43:             Assert.True(
   44:                 coordinator.CanResolve);
   45: 
   46:             Assert.True(
   47:                 coordinator.CanFinalize);
   48: 
   49:             Assert.True(
   50:                 coordinator.TryFinalize(
   51:                     result,
   52:                     out var moment));
   53: 
   54:             Assert.NotNull(
   55:                 moment);
   56: 
   57:             Assert.Equal(
   58:                 MomentOutcome.Completed,
   59:                 moment!.Outcome);
   60: 
   61:             Assert.Equal(
   62:                 PulseStatus.Completed,
   63:                 pulse.Status);
   64: 
   65:             Assert.Same(
   66:                 result,
   67:                 moment.Result);
   68: 
   69:             Assert.Null(
   70:                 moment.Exception);
   71: 
   72:             TestLog.Pass(
   73:                 "Completed Pulse recorded as completed Moment");
   74:         }
   75: 
   76:         [Fact]
   77:         public void FailedWorkProducesFailedMoment()
   78:         {
   79:             TestLog.Test(
   80:                 "Failed WorkUnit produces a failed Moment");
   81: 
   82:             var pulse =
   83:                 CreateProcessingPulse(
   84:                     "failure-request");
   85: 
   86:             var work =
   87:                 CreateProcessingWork(
   88:                     pulse,
   89:                     "failure-work");
   90: 
   91:             var exception =
   92:                 new InvalidOperationException(
   93:                     "Expected processing failure.");
   94: 
   95:             Assert.True(
   96:                 work.TryFail(
   97:                     exception));
   98: 
   99:             var coordinator =
  100:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  101:                     pulse);
  102: 
  103:             Assert.True(
  104:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  105:                     work));
  106: 
  107:             Assert.False(
  108:                 coordinator.CanResolve);
  109: 
  110:             Assert.True(
  111:                 coordinator.CanFinalize);
  112: 
  113:             Assert.True(
  114:                 coordinator.TryFinalize(
  115:                     "must-not-be-used",
  116:                     out var moment));
  117: 
  118:             Assert.NotNull(
  119:                 moment);
  120: 
  121:             Assert.Equal(
  122:                 MomentOutcome.Failed,
  123:                 moment!.Outcome);
  124: 
  125:             Assert.Equal(
  126:                 PulseStatus.Failed,
  127:                 pulse.Status);
  128: 
  129:             Assert.Same(
  130:                 exception,
  131:                 moment.Exception);
  132: 
  133:             Assert.Null(
  134:                 moment.Result);
  135: 
  136:             TestLog.State(
  137:                 $"Failed Pulse recorded | " +
  138:                 $"Exception={moment.Exception!.Message}");
  139: 
  140:             TestLog.Pass(
  141:                 "Failure became honest temporal history");
  142:         }
  143: 
  144:         [Fact]
  145:         public void CancelledWorkProducesCancelledMoment()

  148:                 "Cancelled WorkUnit produces a cancelled Moment");

  153: 
  154:             var work =
  155:                 new WorkUnit(

  163:                 work.TryCancel());

  166:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  167:                     pulse);
  168:

  170:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  171:                     work));
  172: 
  173:             Assert.False(
  174:                 coordinator.CanResolve);
  175: 
  176:             Assert.True(
  177:                 coordinator.CanFinalize);
  178: 
  179:             Assert.True(
  180:                 coordinator.TryFinalize(
  181:                     "must-not-be-used",
  182:                     out var moment));
  183: 
  184:             Assert.NotNull(
  185:                 moment);
  186: 
  187:             Assert.Equal(
  188:                 MomentOutcome.Cancelled,
  189:                 moment!.Outcome);
  190:

  193:                 pulse.Status);
  194: 
  195:             Assert.Null(
  196:                 moment.Result);
  197: 
  198:             Assert.Null(
  199:                 moment.Exception);
  200: 
  201:             TestLog.Pass(
  202:                 "Cancellation became honest temporal history");
  203:         }
  204: 
  205:         [Fact]
  206:         public void FailureTakesPrecedenceOverCancellation()
  207:         {
  208:             var pulse =
  209:                 CreateProcessingPulse(
  210:                     "mixed-terminal-request");
  211: 
  212:             var failed =
  213:                 CreateProcessingWork(
  214:                     pulse,
  215:                     "failed-work");
  216: 
  217:             var cancelled =
  218:                 new WorkUnit(
  219:                     pulse.Id,
  220:                     "cancelled-work");
  221: 
  222:             var exception =
  223:                 new InvalidOperationException(
  224:                     "Failure wins.");
  225: 
  226:             Assert.True(
  227:                 failed.TryFail(
  228:                     exception));
  229: 
  230:             Assert.True(
  231:                 cancelled.TryMarkPending());
  232: 
  233:             Assert.True(
  234:                 cancelled.TryCancel());
  235: 
  236:             var coordinator =
  237:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  238:                     pulse);
  239: 
  240:             Assert.True(
  241:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  242:                     failed));
  243: 
  244:             Assert.True(
  245:                 coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  246:                     cancelled));
  247: 
  248:             Assert.True(
  249:                 coordinator.CanFinalize);
  250: 
  251:             Assert.True(
  252:                 coordinator.TryFinalize(
  253:                     null,
  254:                     out var moment));
  255: 
  256:             Assert.NotNull(
  257:                 moment);
  258: 
  259:             Assert.Equal(
  260:                 MomentOutcome.Failed,
  261:                 moment!.Outcome);
  262: 
  263:             Assert.Equal(
  264:                 PulseStatus.Failed,
  265:                 pulse.Status);
  266: 
  267:             Assert.Same(
  268:                 exception,
  269:                 moment.Exception);
  270:         }
  271: 
  272:         [Fact]
  273:         public void UnresolvedWorkCannotProduceAnyMoment()
  274:         {
  275:             var pulse =
  276:                 CreateProcessingPulse(
  277:                     "unresolved-request");
  278: 
  279:             var work =
  280:                 new WorkUnit(

  282:                     "pending-work");

  285:                 work.TryMarkPending());

  288:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  289:                     pulse);

  293:                     work));
  294: 
  295:             Assert.False(
  296:                 coordinator.CanResolve);
  297: 
  298:             Assert.False(
  299:                 coordinator.CanFinalize);
  300: 
  301:             Assert.False(
  302:                 coordinator.TryFinalize(
  303:                     null,
  304:                     out var moment));
  305: 
  306:             Assert.Null(
  307:                 moment);
  308: 
  309:             Assert.Equal(
  310:                 PulseStatus.Processing,
  311:                 pulse.Status);
  312:         }
  313:

  315:             object? request)
  316:         {
  317:             var pulse =

  319:                     request);
  320: 
  321:             Assert.True(
  322:                 pulse.TryBeginDecomposition());
  323: 
  324:             Assert.True(
  325:                 pulse.TryMarkPending());
  326: 
  327:             Assert.True(
  328:                 pulse.TryMarkReady());
  329: 
  330:             Assert.True(
  331:                 pulse.TryBeginProcessing());
  332: 
  333:             return pulse;
  334:         }

  346:                 work.TryMarkPending());
  347:

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\RegistryTests.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using System.Linq;
    4: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    6: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing;
    7: using WildsEngine.Core.Tests;
    8: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    9: 
   10: namespace WildsEngine.Tests.Registry
   11: {
   12:     /// <summary>
   13:     /// Dependency-free certification suite for registry storage.
   14:     ///
   15:     /// Certifies that instructions and tasks can be parked,
   16:     /// inspected, retrieved, removed, cleared, and manipulated
   17:     /// concurrently without losing registry consistency.
   18:     /// </summary>
   19:     public static class RegistryTests
   20:     {
   21:         private const int ConcurrentItemCount = 1000;
   22: 
   23:         public static async Task RunAllAsync()
   24:         {
   25:             TestLog.Write(
   26:                 "=== Registry Tests ===");
   27: 
   28:             TestLog.Write(
   29:                 string.Empty);
   30: 
   31:             RegistryStartsEmpty();
   32:             ParkInstruction();
   33:             CountBecomesOne();
   34:             EnumerateParkedInstruction();
   35:             RetrieveInstructionByGuid();
   36:             VerifyInstructionReferenceIdentity();
   37:             RejectDuplicateRegistration();
   38:             ParkMultipleInstructions();
   39:             ObserveAllParkedInstructions();
   40:             RemoveInstruction();
   41:             VerifyRemovedInstructionIsGone();
   42:             VerifyRemainingInstructionsAreUnaffected();
   43:             ClearInstructionRegistry();
   44:             VerifyInstructionRegistryIsEmpty();
   45:             TaskRegistrySupportsCoreBehavior();
   46: 
   47:             await ConcurrentRegistrationAndRemovalStressAsync()
   48:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
   49: 
   50:             TestLog.Write(
   51:                 string.Empty);
   52: 
   53:             TestLog.Write(
   54:                 "=== Registry Tests Passed: 16/16 ===");
   55:         }
   56: 
   57:         private static void RegistryStartsEmpty()
   58:         {
   59:             TestLog.Write(
   60:                 "[TEST] Registry starts empty");
   61: 
   62:             var component =
   63:                 CreateComponent(
   64:                     "certification.registry.empty");
   65: 
   66:             var registry =
   67:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
   68:                     component);
   69: 
   70:             PrintInstructionRegistry(
   71:                 registry,
   72:                 "Initial registry state");
   73: 
   74:             Assert(
   75:                 registry.Count == 0,
   76:                 "A new registry should have a count of zero.");
   77: 
   78:             Assert(
   79:                 registry.Items.Count == 0,
   80:                 "A new registry should expose no parked items.");
   81: 
   82:             TestLog.Pass(
   83:                 "Registry starts empty");
   84:         }
   85: 
   86:         private static void ParkInstruction()
   87:         {
   88:             TestLog.Write(
   89:                 "[TEST] Park instruction");
   90: 
   91:             var component =
   92:                 CreateComponent(
   93:                     "certification.registry.park");
   94: 
   95:             var registry =
   96:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
   97:                     component);
   98: 
   99:             var instruction =
  100:                 CreateInstruction(
  101:                     "certification.instruction.park");
  102: 
  103:             TestLog.Write(
  104:                 $"    [PARK] {DescribeInstruction(instruction)}");
  105: 
  106:             var registered =
  107:                 registry.Register(
  108:                     instruction);
  109: 
  110:             Assert(
  111:                 registered,
  112:                 "The first registration should succeed.");
  113: 
  114:             Assert(
  115:                 registry.Contains(
  116:                     instruction.Id),
  117:                 "The registry should contain the parked instruction.");
  118: 
  119:             PrintInstructionRegistry(
  120:                 registry,
  121:                 "After instruction park");
  122: 
  123:             TestLog.Pass(
  124:                 "Park instruction");
  125:         }
  126: 
  127:         private static void CountBecomesOne()
  128:         {
  129:             TestLog.Write(
  130:                 "[TEST] Count becomes 1");
  131: 
  132:             var component =
  133:                 CreateComponent(
  134:                     "certification.registry.count");
  135: 
  136:             var registry =
  137:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
  138:                     component);
  139: 
  140:             var instruction =
  141:                 CreateInstruction(
  142:                     "certification.instruction.count");
  143: 
  144:             registry.Register(
  145:                 instruction);
  146: 
  147:             TestLog.Write(
  148:                 $"    [STATE] Count = {registry.Count}");
  149: 
  150:             Assert(
  151:                 registry.Count == 1,
  152:                 "Parking one instruction should produce a count of one.");
  153: 
  154:             TestLog.Pass(
  155:                 "Count becomes 1");
  156:         }
  157: 
  158:         private static void EnumerateParkedInstruction()
  159:         {
  160:             TestLog.Write(
  161:                 "[TEST] Enumerate Items and print parked instruction");
  162: 
  163:             var component =
  164:                 CreateComponent(
  165:                     "certification.registry.enumerate");
  166: 
  167:             var registry =
  168:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
  169:                     component);
  170: 
  171:             var instruction =
  172:                 CreateInstruction(
  173:                     "certification.instruction.enumerate");
  174: 
  175:             registry.Register(
  176:                 instruction);
  177: 
  178:             var observedCount = 0;
  179: 
  180:             foreach (var parked in registry.Items)
  181:             {
  182:                 observedCount++;
  183: 
  184:                 TestLog.Write(
  185:                     $"    [ITEM] {DescribeInstruction(parked)}");
  186:             }
  187: 
  188:             Assert(
  189:                 observedCount == 1,
  190:                 "Items should enumerate the parked instruction.");
  191: 
  192:             TestLog.Pass(
  193:                 "Enumerate Items and print parked instruction");
  194:         }
  195: 
  196:         private static void RetrieveInstructionByGuid()
  197:         {
  198:             TestLog.Write(
  199:                 "[TEST] Retrieve instruction by GUID");
  200: 
  201:             var component =
  202:                 CreateComponent(
  203:                     "certification.registry.retrieve");
  204: 
  205:             var registry =
  206:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
  207:                     component);
  208: 
  209:             var instruction =
  210:                 CreateInstruction(
  211:                     "certification.instruction.retrieve");
  212: 
  213:             registry.Register(
  214:                 instruction);
  215: 
  216:             TestLog.Write(
  217:                 $"    [READ] Instruction {instruction.Id}");
  218: 
  219:             var retrieved =
  220:                 registry.Get(
  221:                     instruction.Id);
  222: 
  223:             Assert(
  224:                 retrieved != null,
  225:                 "Get should retrieve a parked instruction.");
  226: 
  227:             if (retrieved == null)
  228:             {
  229:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  230:                     "Registry test failed: " +
  231:                     "Get returned null for a parked instruction.");
  232:             }
  233: 
  234:             Assert(
  235:                 retrieved.Id == instruction.Id,
  236:                 "The retrieved instruction should have the requested GUID.");
  237: 
  238:             var found =
  239:                 registry.TryGet(
  240:                     instruction.Id,
  241:                     out var tried);
  242: 
  243:             Assert(
  244:                 found,
  245:                 "TryGet should report the parked instruction.");
  246: 
  247:             Assert(
  248:                 tried != null,
  249:                 "TryGet should supply the parked instruction.");
  250: 
  251:             TestLog.Write(
  252:                 $"    [FOUND] {DescribeInstruction(retrieved)}");
  253: 
  254:             TestLog.Pass(
  255:                 "Retrieve instruction by GUID");
  256:         }

  310:                     "certification.registry.duplicate");
  311: 
  312:             var registry =
  313:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
  314:                     component);
  315: 
  316:             var instruction =
  317:                 CreateInstruction(

  321:                 registry.Register(
  322:                     instruction);
  323: 
  324:             var second =
  325:                 registry.Register(
  326:                     instruction);
  327: 
  328:             TestLog.Write(

  330: 
  331:             TestLog.Write(
  332:                 $"    [STATE] Duplicate registration = {second}");

  335:                 first,

  339:                 !second,

  344:                 "Duplicate registration must not increase registry count.");

  347:                 ReferenceEquals(
  348:                     instruction,

  350:                         instruction.Id)),
  351:                 "Duplicate rejection must not replace the original item.");

  401:         private static void ObserveAllParkedInstructions()
  402:         {
  403:             TestLog.Write(
  404:                 "[TEST] Observe all currently parked objects");
  405: 
  406:             var component =
  407:                 CreateComponent(
  408:                     "certification.registry.observe");
  409: 
  410:             var registry =
  411:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
  412:                     component);
  413: 
  414:             var instructions =
  415:                 CreateInstructionSet(
  416:                     5,
  417:                     "certification.instruction.observe");
  418: 
  419:             foreach (var instruction in instructions)
  420:             {
  421:                 registry.Register(
  422:                     instruction);
  423:             }
  424: 
  425:             var parked =
  426:                 registry.Items.ToArray();
  427: 
  428:             TestLog.Write(
  429:                 $"    [STATE] Parked instruction count = " +
  430:                 $"{parked.Length}");
  431: 
  432:             foreach (var instruction in parked)
  433:             {
  434:                 TestLog.Write(
  435:                     $"    [ITEM] {DescribeInstruction(instruction)}");
  436:             }
  437: 
  438:             Assert(
  439:                 parked.Length == instructions.Count,
  440:                 "Items should expose every parked instruction.");
  441: 
  442:             foreach (var expected in instructions)
  443:             {
  444:                 Assert(
  445:                     parked.Any(
  446:                         candidate =>
  447:                             candidate.Id == expected.Id),
  448:                     $"Instruction {expected.Id} should be observable.");
  449:             }
  450: 
  451:             TestLog.Pass(
  452:                 "Observe all currently parked objects");
  453:         }

  494:         private static void VerifyRemovedInstructionIsGone()
  495:         {
  496:             TestLog.Write(
  497:                 "[TEST] Verify removed instruction is gone");
  498: 
  499:             var component =
  500:                 CreateComponent(
  501:                     "certification.registry.gone");
  502: 
  503:             var registry =
  504:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
  505:                     component);
  506: 
  507:             var instruction =
  508:                 CreateInstruction(
  509:                     "certification.instruction.gone");
  510: 
  511:             registry.Register(
  512:                 instruction);
  513: 
  514:             registry.Remove(
  515:                 instruction.Id);
  516: 
  517:             Assert(
  518:                 !registry.Contains(
  519:                     instruction.Id),
  520:                 "Contains should be false after removal.");
  521: 
  522:             Assert(
  523:                 registry.Get(
  524:                     instruction.Id) == null,
  525:                 "Get should return null after removal.");
  526: 
  527:             var found =
  528:                 registry.TryGet(
  529:                     instruction.Id,
  530:                     out var retrieved);
  531: 
  532:             Assert(
  533:                 !found,
  534:                 "TryGet should return false after removal.");
  535: 
  536:             Assert(
  537:                 retrieved == null,
  538:                 "TryGet should not return an item after removal.");
  539: 
  540:             TestLog.Write(
  541:                 $"    [STATE] Contains = " +
  542:                 $"{registry.Contains(instruction.Id)}");
  543: 
  544:             TestLog.Write(
  545:                 $"    [STATE] Count = {registry.Count}");
  546: 
  547:             TestLog.Pass(
  548:                 "Verify removed instruction is gone");
  549:         }

  551:         private static void VerifyRemainingInstructionsAreUnaffected()
  552:         {
  553:             TestLog.Write(
  554:                 "[TEST] Verify remaining items unaffected");
  555: 
  556:             var component =
  557:                 CreateComponent(
  558:                     "certification.registry.remaining");
  559: 
  560:             var registry =
  561:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
  562:                     component);
  563: 
  564:             var first =
  565:                 CreateInstruction(
  566:                     "certification.instruction.remaining.first");
  567: 
  568:             var removed =
  569:                 CreateInstruction(
  570:                     "certification.instruction.remaining.removed");
  571: 
  572:             var third =
  573:                 CreateInstruction(
  574:                     "certification.instruction.remaining.third");
  575: 
  576:             registry.Register(
  577:                 first);
  578: 
  579:             registry.Register(
  580:                 removed);
  581: 
  582:             registry.Register(
  583:                 third);
  584: 
  585:             registry.Remove(
  586:                 removed.Id);
  587: 
  588:             Assert(
  589:                 registry.Count == 2,
  590:                 "Removing one instruction should leave two parked.");
  591: 
  592:             Assert(
  593:                 registry.Contains(
  594:                     first.Id),
  595:                 "The first instruction should remain.");
  596: 
  597:             Assert(
  598:                 registry.Contains(
  599:                     third.Id),
  600:                 "The third instruction should remain.");
  601: 
  602:             Assert(
  603:                 !registry.Contains(
  604:                     removed.Id),
  605:                 "Only the selected instruction should be removed.");
  606: 
  607:             Assert(
  608:                 ReferenceEquals(
  609:                     first,
  610:                     registry.Get(first.Id)),
  611:                 "The first parked reference should remain unchanged.");
  612: 
  613:             Assert(
  614:                 ReferenceEquals(
  615:                     third,
  616:                     registry.Get(third.Id)),
  617:                 "The third parked reference should remain unchanged.");
  618: 
  619:             PrintInstructionRegistry(
  620:                 registry,
  621:                 "After selective removal");
  622: 
  623:             TestLog.Pass(
  624:                 "Verify remaining items unaffected");
  625:         }

  634:                     "certification.registry.clear");

  636:             var registry =

  642:                     8,

  647:                 registry.Register(
  648:                     instruction);
  649:             }

  653:                 "Instructions should be parked before Clear.");
  654: 
  655:             TestLog.Write(

  657:                 $"{registry.Count}");

  662:                 $"    [STATE] Count after Clear = " +
  663:                 $"{registry.Count}");
  664: 
  665:             Assert(
  666:                 registry.Count == 0,
  667:                 "Clear should remove every parked instruction.");
  668: 
  669:             TestLog.Pass(
  670:                 "Clear registry");
  671:         }

  710:                     instruction.Id) == null,
  711:                 "Cleared registry should not retrieve the prior instruction.");
  712:

  715:                 "Final empty state");
  716: 
  717:             TestLog.Pass(
  718:                 "Verify registry empty");

  720: 
  721:         private static void TaskRegistrySupportsCoreBehavior()
  722:         {
  723:             TestLog.Write(
  724:                 "[TEST] Repeat core behavior for task registry");
  725: 
  726:             var component =
  727:                 CreateComponent(
  728:                     "certification.task");
  729: 
  730:             var registry =
  731:                 CreateTaskRegistry(
  732:                     component);
  733: 
  734:             Assert(
  735:                 registry.Count == 0,
  736:                 "A new task registry should start empty.");
  737: 
  738:             var first =
  739:                 new TestTask(
  740:                     component,
  741:                     "certification.task.first");
  742: 
  743:             var second =
  744:                 new TestTask(
  745:                     component,
  746:                     "certification.task.second");
  747: 
  748:             TestLog.Write(
  749:                 $"    [PARK] {DescribeTask(first)}");
  750: 
  751:             TestLog.Write(
  752:                 $"    [PARK] {DescribeTask(second)}");
  753: 
  754:             Assert(
  755:                 registry.Register(
  756:                     first),
  757:                 "The first task should be parked.");
  758: 
  759:             Assert(
  760:                 registry.Register(
  761:                     second),
  762:                 "The second task should be parked.");
  763: 
  764:             Assert(
  765:                 registry.Count == 2,
  766:                 "The task registry should contain both tasks.");
  767: 
  768:             Assert(
  769:                 ReferenceEquals(
  770:                     first,
  771:                     registry.Get(first.Id)),
  772:                 "The task registry should preserve reference identity.");
  773: 
  774:             Assert(
  775:                 !registry.Register(
  776:                     first),
  777:                 "Duplicate task registration should be rejected.");
  778: 
  779:             foreach (var task in registry.Items)
  780:             {
  781:                 TestLog.Write(
  782:                     $"    [ITEM] {DescribeTask(task)}");
  783:             }
  784: 
  785:             TestLog.Write(
  786:                 $"    [REMOVE] Task {first.Id}");
  787: 
  788:             Assert(
  789:                 registry.Remove(
  790:                     first.Id),
  791:                 "Removing the first task should succeed.");
  792: 
  793:             Assert(
  794:                 !registry.Contains(
  795:                     first.Id),
  796:                 "The removed task should no longer be parked.");
  797: 
  798:             Assert(
  799:                 registry.Contains(
  800:                     second.Id),
  801:                 "The remaining task should be unaffected.");
  802: 
  803:             registry.Clear();
  804: 
  805:             Assert(
  806:                 registry.Count == 0,
  807:                 "Clearing should empty the task registry.");
  808: 
  809:             Assert(
  810:                 registry.Items.Count == 0,
  811:                 "The task registry should enumerate no items after Clear.");
  812: 
  813:             TestLog.Pass(
  814:                 "Repeat core behavior for task registry");
  815:         }
  816: 
  817:         private static async Task
  818:             ConcurrentRegistrationAndRemovalStressAsync()
  819:         {
  820:             TestLog.Write(
  821:                 "[TEST] Concurrent registration/removal stress");
  822: 
  823:             var component =
  824:                 CreateComponent(
  825:                     "certification.registry.concurrent");
  826: 
  827:             var registry =
  828:                 CreateInstructionRegistry(
  829:                     component);
  830: 
  831:             var instructions =
  832:                 CreateInstructionSet(
  833:                     ConcurrentItemCount,
  834:                     "certification.instruction.concurrent");
  835: 
  836:             TestLog.Write(
  837:                 $"    [TASK] Concurrently parking " +
  838:                 $"{instructions.Count} instructions");
  839: 
  840:             var registrationTasks =
  841:                 new List<Task>(
  842:                     instructions.Count);
  843: 
  844:             foreach (var instruction in instructions)
  845:             {
  846:                 registrationTasks.Add(
  847:                     Task.Run(
  848:                         () =>
  849:                         {
  850:                             var registered =
  851:                                 registry.Register(
  852:                                     instruction);
  853: 
  854:                             Assert(
  855:                                 registered,
  856:                                 $"Concurrent registration failed " +
  857:                                 $"for {instruction.Id}.");
  858:                         }));
  859:             }
  860: 
  861:             await Task.WhenAll(
  862:                     registrationTasks)
  863:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
  864: 
  865:             TestLog.Write(
  866:                 $"    [STATE] Count after registration = " +
  867:                 $"{registry.Count}");
  868: 
  869:             Assert(
  870:                 registry.Count == instructions.Count,
  871:                 "Concurrent registration should retain " +
  872:                 "every unique instruction.");
  873: 
  874:             foreach (var instruction in instructions)
  875:             {
  876:                 Assert(
  877:                     registry.Contains(
  878:                         instruction.Id),
  879:                     $"Instruction {instruction.Id} should be parked.");
  880:             }
  881: 
  882:             var removedInstructions =
  883:                 instructions
  884:                     .Where(
  885:                         (_, index) =>
  886:                             index % 2 == 0)
  887:                     .ToArray();
  888: 
  889:             var retainedInstructions =
  890:                 instructions
  891:                     .Where(
  892:                         (_, index) =>
  893:                             index % 2 != 0)
  894:                     .ToArray();
  895: 
  896:             TestLog.Write(
  897:                 $"    [TASK] Concurrently removing " +
  898:                 $"{removedInstructions.Length} instructions");
  899: 
  900:             var removalTasks =
  901:                 new List<Task>(
  902:                     removedInstructions.Length);
  903: 
  904:             foreach (var instruction in removedInstructions)
  905:             {
  906:                 removalTasks.Add(
  907:                     Task.Run(
  908:                         () =>
  909:                         {
  910:                             var removed =
  911:                                 registry.Remove(
  912:                                     instruction.Id);
  913: 
  914:                             Assert(
  915:                                 removed,
  916:                                 $"Concurrent removal failed " +
  917:                                 $"for {instruction.Id}.");
  918:                         }));
  919:             }
  920: 
  921:             await Task.WhenAll(
  922:                     removalTasks)
  923:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
  924: 
  925:             TestLog.Write(
  926:                 $"    [STATE] Count after removal = " +
  927:                 $"{registry.Count}");
  928: 
  929:             Assert(
  930:                 registry.Count == retainedInstructions.Length,
  931:                 "Concurrent removal should leave the " +
  932:                 "expected instruction count.");
  933: 
  934:             foreach (var instruction in removedInstructions)
  935:             {
  936:                 Assert(
  937:                     !registry.Contains(
  938:                         instruction.Id),
  939:                     $"Removed instruction {instruction.Id} " +
  940:                     "should be absent.");
  941:             }
  942: 
  943:             foreach (var instruction in retainedInstructions)
  944:             {
  945:                 Assert(
  946:                     registry.Contains(
  947:                         instruction.Id),
  948:                     $"Retained instruction {instruction.Id} " +
  949:                     "should remain parked.");
  950: 
  951:                 Assert(
  952:                     ReferenceEquals(
  953:                         instruction,
  954:                         registry.Get(
  955:                             instruction.Id)),
  956:                     $"Retained instruction {instruction.Id} " +
  957:                     "should preserve its reference.");
  958:             }
  959: 
  960:             registry.Clear();
  961: 
  962:             Assert(
  963:                 registry.Count == 0,
  964:                 "The stressed registry should remain clearable.");
  965: 
  966:             TestLog.Pass(
  967:                 "Concurrent registration/removal stress");
  968:         }
  969:

  972:         {
  973:             return new TestComponent(
  974:                 layer);

  983:                 "certification.instruction-registry");

  994: 
  995:         private static TestInstruction
  996:             CreateInstruction(
  997:                 string intent)
  998:         {
  999:             return new TestInstruction(
 1000:                 intent,
 1001:                 payload: null);
 1002:         }
 1003: 
 1004:         private static List<TestInstruction>
 1005:             CreateInstructionSet(
 1006:                 int count,
 1007:                 string intentPrefix)
 1008:         {
 1009:             var instructions =
 1010:                 new List<TestInstruction>(
 1011:                     count);
 1012: 
 1013:             for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
 1014:             {
 1015:                 instructions.Add(
 1016:                     new TestInstruction(
 1017:                         $"{intentPrefix}.{i:D4}",
 1018:                         payload: i));
 1019:             }
 1020: 
 1021:             return instructions;
 1022:         }
 1023: 
 1024:         private static string DescribeInstruction(
 1025:             IStateInstruction instruction)
 1026:         {
 1027:             return
 1028:                 $"Instruction " +
 1029:                 $"Id={instruction.Id}, " +
 1030:                 $"Intent={instruction.Intent}";
 1031:         }
 1032: 
 1033:         private static string DescribeTask(
 1034:             ITask task)
 1035:         {
 1036:             return
 1037:                 $"Task " +
 1038:                 $"Id={task.Id}, " +
 1039:                 $"Slug={task.Slug}";
 1040:         }
 1041: 
 1042:         private static void PrintInstructionRegistry(
 1043:             TestInstructionRegistry registry,
 1044:             string label)
 1045:         {
 1046:             TestLog.Write(
 1047:                 $"    [REGISTRY] {label}");
 1048: 
 1049:             TestLog.Write(
 1050:                 $"               Count : {registry.Count}");
 1051: 
 1052:             foreach (var instruction in registry.Items)
 1053:             {
 1054:                 TestLog.Write(
 1055:                     $"               Item  : " +
 1056:                     $"{DescribeInstruction(instruction)}");
 1057:             }
 1058:         }
 1059: 
 1060:         private static void Assert(
 1061:             bool condition,
 1062:             string message)
 1063:         {
 1064:             if (!condition)
 1065:             {
 1066:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
 1067:                     $"Registry test failed: {message}");
 1068:             }
 1069:         }

 1142:                     new Dictionary<string, object?>();
 1143:

 1145:                     new Dictionary<string, object?>();

 1155:                 Arguments =>

 1159:                 Metadata =>
 1160:                     _metadata;

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\TaskRegistryTests.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Diagnostics;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Registry;
    6: using WildsEngine.Core.Tests;
    7: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    8: 
    9: namespace WildsEngine.Tests.Registry
   10: {
   11:     /// <summary>
   12:     /// Certifies task registry storage, retrieval, enumeration,
   13:     /// duplicate protection, removal, and high-volume behavior.
   14:     /// </summary>
   15:     public static class TaskRegistryTests
   16:     {
   17:         private static readonly int[] Volumes =
   18:         {
   19:             1_000,
   20:             10_000,
   21:             100_000,
   22:             1_000_000
   23:         };
   24: 
   25:         public static void RunAll()
   26:         {
   27:             TestLog.Write(
   28:                 "=== Task Registry Tests ===");
   29: 
   30:             TestLog.Write(
   31:                 string.Empty);
   32: 
   33:             BasicTaskLifecycle();
   34: 
   35:             for (var i = 0; i < Volumes.Length; i++)
   36:             {
   37:                 HighVolumeTaskLifecycle(
   38:                     Volumes[i]);
   39:             }
   40: 
   41:             TestLog.Write(
   42:                 string.Empty);
   43: 
   44:             TestLog.Write(
   45:                 $"=== Task Registry Tests Passed: " +
   46:                 $"{Volumes.Length + 1}/{Volumes.Length + 1} ===");
   47:         }
   48: 
   49:         private static void BasicTaskLifecycle()
   50:         {
   51:             const string testName =
   52:                 "Task registry basic lifecycle";
   53: 
   54:             TestLog.Write(
   55:                 $"[TEST] {testName}");
   56: 
   57:             var component =
   58:                 CreateComponent(
   59:                     "test.task-registry.basic");
   60: 
   61:             var registry =
   62:                 new DefaultTasksRegistry(
   63:                     component,
   64:                     "test.task-registry.basic");
   65: 
   66:             TestLog.Write(
   67:                 "    [TASK] Initializing task registry");
   68: 
   69:             Assert(
   70:                 registry.Initialize(),
   71:                 "First registry initialization should succeed.");
   72: 
   73:             Assert(
   74:                 registry.IsInitialized,
   75:                 "Registry should report initialized.");
   76: 
   77:             Assert(
   78:                 registry.Count == 0,
   79:                 "Registry should begin empty.");
   80: 
   81:             var task =
   82:                 new TestTask(
   83:                     component,
   84:                     "test.task.basic");
   85: 
   86:             TestLog.Write(
   87:                 $"    [PARK] Task {task.Id}");
   88: 
   89:             Assert(
   90:                 registry.Register(task),
   91:                 "Task should register successfully.");
   92: 
   93:             Assert(
   94:                 registry.Count == 1,
   95:                 "Registry count should become one.");
   96: 
   97:             Assert(
   98:                 registry.Contains(task.Id),
   99:                 "Registry should contain the parked task.");
  100: 
  101:             var retrieved =
  102:                 registry.Get(
  103:                     task.Id);
  104: 
  105:             TestLog.Write(
  106:                 $"    [READ] Task {task.Id}");
  107: 
  108:             Assert(
  109:                 retrieved != null,
  110:                 "Parked task should be retrievable.");
  111: 
  112:             if (retrieved == null)
  113:             {
  114:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  115:                     "TaskRegistry test failed: " +
  116:                     "Parked task retrieval returned null.");
  117:             }
  118: 
  119:             Assert(
  120:                 ReferenceEquals(
  121:                     task,
  122:                     retrieved),
  123:                 "Retrieved task should be the parked instance.");
  124: 
  125:             Assert(
  126:                 registry.TryGet(
  127:                     task.Id,
  128:                     out var tried),
  129:                 "TryGet should find the parked task.");
  130: 
  131:             Assert(
  132:                 tried != null,
  133:                 "TryGet should return a task.");
  134: 
  135:             Assert(
  136:                 ReferenceEquals(
  137:                     task,
  138:                     tried),
  139:                 "TryGet should return the parked instance.");
  140: 
  141:             Assert(
  142:                 !registry.Register(task),
  143:                 "Duplicate task registration should be rejected.");
  144: 
  145:             Assert(
  146:                 registry.Count == 1,
  147:                 "Duplicate registration must not change count.");
  148: 
  149:             TestLog.Write(
  150:                 $"    [REMOVE] Task {task.Id}");
  151: 
  152:             Assert(
  153:                 registry.Remove(task.Id),
  154:                 "Parked task should be removable.");
  155: 
  156:             Assert(
  157:                 registry.Count == 0,
  158:                 "Registry should be empty after removal.");
  159: 
  160:             Assert(
  161:                 !registry.Contains(task.Id),
  162:                 "Removed task should no longer be contained.");
  163: 
  164:             Assert(
  165:                 registry.Get(task.Id) == null,
  166:                 "Removed task should no longer be retrievable.");
  167: 
  168:             Assert(
  169:                 !registry.Remove(task.Id),
  170:                 "Removing an absent task should return false.");
  171: 
  172:             Assert(
  173:                 registry.Shutdown(),
  174:                 "First registry shutdown should succeed.");
  175: 
  176:             Assert(
  177:                 !registry.IsInitialized,
  178:                 "Registry should report stopped.");
  179: 
  180:             TestLog.Write(
  181:                 $"[PASS] {testName}");
  182:         }
  183: 
  184:         private static void HighVolumeTaskLifecycle(
  185:             int taskCount)
  186:         {
  187:             var testName =
  188:                 $"Task registry lifecycle at {taskCount:N0}";
  189: 
  190:             TestLog.Write(
  191:                 $"[TEST] {testName}");
  192: 
  193:             var component =
  194:                 CreateComponent(
  195:                     $"test.task-registry.{taskCount}");
  196: 
  197:             var registry =
  198:                 new DefaultTasksRegistry(
  199:                     component,
  200:                     $"test.task-registry.{taskCount}");
  201: 
  202:             Assert(
  203:                 registry.Initialize(),
  204:                 "Registry initialization should succeed.");
  205: 
  206:             var taskIds =
  207:                 new Guid[taskCount];
  208: 
  209:             var registrationWatch =
  210:                 Stopwatch.StartNew();
  211: 
  212:             TestLog.Write(
  213:                 $"    [TASK] Parking {taskCount:N0} tasks");
  214: 
  215:             for (var i = 0; i < taskCount; i++)
  216:             {
  217:                 var task =
  218:                     new TestTask(
  219:                         component,
  220:                         "test.task.parked");
  221: 
  222:                 taskIds[i] =
  223:                     task.Id;
  224: 
  225:                 if (!registry.Register(task))
  226:                 {
  227:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  228:                         $"Task registration failed at index {i:N0}.");
  229:                 }
  230:             }
  231: 
  232:             registrationWatch.Stop();
  233: 
  234:             TestLog.Write(
  235:                 $"    [STATE] Parked count = {registry.Count:N0}");
  236: 
  237:             TestLog.Write(
  238:                 $"    [TIME] Registration = " +
  239:                 $"{registrationWatch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds:F3} ms");
  240: 
  241:             Assert(
  242:                 registry.Count == taskCount,
  243:                 $"Registry should contain {taskCount:N0} tasks.");
  244: 
  245:             Assert(
  246:                 registry.Items.Count == taskCount,
  247:                 "Items should expose every parked task.");
  248: 
  249:             VerifyRepresentativeTasks(
  250:                 registry,
  251:                 taskIds);
  252: 
  253:             var duplicateId =
  254:                 taskIds[taskCount / 2];
  255: 
  256:             var duplicateTask =
  257:                 registry.Get(
  258:                     duplicateId);
  259: 
  260:             Assert(
  261:                 duplicateTask != null,
  262:                 "Representative task should be available.");
  263: 
  264:             if (duplicateTask == null)
  265:             {
  266:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  267:                     "TaskRegistry test failed: " +
  268:                     "Representative task retrieval returned null.");
  269:             }
  270: 
  271:             Assert(
  272:                 !registry.Register(duplicateTask),
  273:                 "Duplicate task registration should be rejected.");
  274: 
  275:             Assert(
  276:                 registry.Count == taskCount,
  277:                 "Duplicate registration must not alter count.");
  278: 
  279:             var retrievalWatch =
  280:                 Stopwatch.StartNew();
  281: 
  282:             TestLog.Write(
  283:                 $"    [TASK] Reading {taskCount:N0} parked tasks");
  284: 
  285:             for (var i = 0; i < taskIds.Length; i++)
  286:             {
  287:                 if (!registry.TryGet(
  288:                         taskIds[i],
  289:                         out var task))
  290:                 {
  291:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  292:                         $"Task retrieval failed at index {i:N0}.");
  293:                 }
  294: 
  295:                 if (task == null)
  296:                 {
  297:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  298:                         $"Task retrieval returned null at index {i:N0}.");
  299:                 }
  300: 
  301:                 if (task.Id != taskIds[i])
  302:                 {
  303:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  304:                         $"Task identity mismatch at index {i:N0}.");
  305:                 }
  306:             }
  307: 
  308:             retrievalWatch.Stop();
  309: 
  310:             TestLog.Write(
  311:                 $"    [TIME] Retrieval = " +
  312:                 $"{retrievalWatch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds:F3} ms");
  313: 
  314:             var removalWatch =
  315:                 Stopwatch.StartNew();
  316: 
  317:             TestLog.Write(
  318:                 $"    [TASK] Removing {taskCount:N0} parked tasks");
  319: 
  320:             for (var i = 0; i < taskIds.Length; i++)
  321:             {
  322:                 if (!registry.Remove(
  323:                         taskIds[i]))
  324:                 {
  325:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  326:                         $"Task removal failed at index {i:N0}.");
  327:                 }
  328:             }
  329: 
  330:             removalWatch.Stop();
  331: 
  332:             TestLog.Write(
  333:                 $"    [STATE] Remaining count = {registry.Count:N0}");
  334: 
  335:             TestLog.Write(
  336:                 $"    [TIME] Removal = " +
  337:                 $"{removalWatch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds:F3} ms");
  338: 
  339:             Assert(
  340:                 registry.Count == 0,
  341:                 "Registry should be empty after every task is removed.");
  342: 
  343:             Assert(
  344:                 registry.Items.Count == 0,
  345:                 "Items should be empty after removal.");
  346: 
  347:             VerifyTasksAreAbsent(
  348:                 registry,
  349:                 taskIds);
  350: 
  351:             Assert(
  352:                 registry.Shutdown(),
  353:                 "Registry shutdown should succeed.");
  354: 
  355:             TestLog.Write(
  356:                 $"[PASS] {testName}");
  357:         }
  358: 
  359:         private static void VerifyRepresentativeTasks(
  360:             ITaskRegistry registry,
  361:             Guid[] taskIds)
  362:         {
  363:             var indexes =
  364:                 new[]
  365:                 {
  366:                     0,
  367:                     taskIds.Length / 2,
  368:                     taskIds.Length - 1
  369:                 };
  370: 
  371:             for (var i = 0; i < indexes.Length; i++)
  372:             {
  373:                 var index =
  374:                     indexes[i];
  375: 
  376:                 var id =
  377:                     taskIds[index];
  378: 
  379:                 TestLog.Write(
  380:                     $"    [READ] Representative task " +
  381:                     $"{index:N0}: {id}");
  382: 
  383:                 Assert(
  384:                     registry.Contains(id),
  385:                     $"Registry should contain task at index {index:N0}.");
  386: 
  387:                 var task =
  388:                     registry.Get(id);
  389: 
  390:                 Assert(
  391:                     task != null,
  392:                     $"Task at index {index:N0} should be retrievable.");
  393: 
  394:                 if (task == null)
  395:                 {
  396:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  397:                         "TaskRegistry test failed: " +
  398:                         $"Task at index {index:N0} returned null.");
  399:                 }
  400: 
  401:                 Assert(
  402:                     task.Id == id,
  403:                     $"Task at index {index:N0} should preserve identity.");
  404:             }
  405:         }
  406: 
  407:         private static void VerifyTasksAreAbsent(
  408:             ITaskRegistry registry,
  409:             Guid[] taskIds)
  410:         {
  411:             var indexes =
  412:                 new[]
  413:                 {
  414:                     0,
  415:                     taskIds.Length / 2,
  416:                     taskIds.Length - 1
  417:                 };
  418: 
  419:             for (var i = 0; i < indexes.Length; i++)
  420:             {
  421:                 var index =
  422:                     indexes[i];
  423: 
  424:                 var id =
  425:                     taskIds[index];
  426: 
  427:                 TestLog.Write(
  428:                     $"    [VERIFY] Removed task " +
  429:                     $"{index:N0}: {id}");
  430: 
  431:                 Assert(
  432:                     !registry.Contains(id),
  433:                     $"Removed task at index {index:N0} " +
  434:                     "should not be contained.");
  435: 
  436:                 Assert(
  437:                     registry.Get(id) == null,
  438:                     $"Removed task at index {index:N0} " +
  439:                     "should not be retrievable.");
  440: 
  441:                 Assert(
  442:                     !registry.TryGet(
  443:                         id,
  444:                         out var task),
  445:                     $"TryGet should reject removed task " +
  446:                     $"at index {index:N0}.");
  447: 
  448:                 Assert(
  449:                     task == null,
  450:                     "Failed TryGet should return null.");
  451:             }
  452:         }
  453: 
  454:         private static IComponent CreateComponent(
  455:             string layer)
  456:         {
  457:             return new TestComponent(
  458:                 layer);
  459:         }
  460: 
  461:         private static void Assert(
  462:             bool condition,
  463:             string message)
  464:         {
  465:             if (!condition)
  466:             {
  467:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  468:                     $"TaskRegistry test failed: {message}");
  469:             }
  470:         }

  479:                     component,

  482:             }
  483:         }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\TestLog.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Globalization;
    3: using System.IO;
    4: using System.Text;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Tests.Logging
    7: {
    8:     public static class TestLog
    9:     {
   10:         private static readonly object Sync = new object();
   11:         private static string? _path;
   12: 
   13:         public static string Path => _path ?? string.Empty;
   14: 
   15:         public static void Initialize(string? fileName = null)
   16:         {
   17:             lock (Sync)
   18:             {
   19:                 if (_path != null)
   20:                     return;
   21: 
   22:                 var logs = System.IO.Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "Logs");
   23:                 Directory.CreateDirectory(logs);
   24:                 var name = fileName ?? $"wildsengine-tests-{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd-HHmmss}.log";
   25:                 _path = System.IO.Path.Combine(logs, name);
   26:                 File.WriteAllText(_path, $"WildsEngine test log{Environment.NewLine}", Encoding.UTF8);
   27:             }
   28:         }
   29: 
   30:         public static void Section(string title)
   31:         {
   32:             Write("");
   33:             Write("============================================");
   34:             Write($" {title}");
   35:             Write("============================================");
   36:         }
   37: 
   38:         public static void Write(string message)
   39:         {
   40:             lock (Sync)
   41:             {
   42:                 if (_path == null)
   43:                     Initialize();
   44: 
   45:                 Console.WriteLine(message);
   46:                 File.AppendAllText(_path!,
   47:                     $"{DateTime.Now.ToString("O", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)} {message}{Environment.NewLine}",
   48:                     Encoding.UTF8);
   49:             }
   50:         }
   51: 
   52:         public static void Test(string message) => Write($"[TEST] {message}");
   53:         public static void Pass(string message) => Write($"[PASS] {message}");
   54:         public static void Task(string message) => Write($"    [TASK] {message}");
   55:         public static void State(string message) => Write($"    [STATE] {message}");
   56:         public static void Expected(string message) => Write($"    [EXPECTED] {message}");
   57:         public static void Fail(string message, Exception ex) =>
   58:             Write($"[FAIL] {message}: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}");
   59:     }
   60: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\TestState.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.States;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Tests.Registry;
    6: 
    7: internal sealed class TestState :
    8:     WildsState
    9: {
   10:     public TestState(
   11:         IComponent component,
   12:         IContext context,
   13:         string layer)
   14:         : base(
   15:             component,
   16:             context,
   17:             layer)
   18:     {
   19:     }
   20: 
   21:     public override bool Process(
   22:         IStateInstruction instruction)
   23:     {
   24:         if (instruction == null)
   25:         {
   26:             throw new ArgumentNullException(
   27:                 nameof(instruction));
   28:         }
   29: 
   30:         if (TryProcessNeighborInstruction(
   31:                 instruction))
   32:         {
   33:             return true;
   34:         }
   35: 
   36:         if (instruction is
   37:             ISetContextVariableInstruction
   38:             setContextVariable)
   39:         {
   40:             Context.SetVariable(
   41:                 setContextVariable.Key,
   42:                 setContextVariable.Value);
   43: 
   44:             return true;
   45:         }
   46: 
   47:         return false;
   48:     }
   49: }
