    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Units;
    4: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    5: using Xunit;
    6: 
    7: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests
    8: {
    9:     /// <summary>
   10:     /// Certifies the Wilds unit and temporal foundation:
   11:     ///
   12:     /// BaseUnit
   13:     ///     -> Pulse
   14:     ///         -> WorkUnit(s)
   15:     ///             -> Moment
   16:     ///                 -> Timeline
   17:     ///                     -> Epoch
   18:     ///
   19:     /// A Pulse represents one complete request.
   20:     /// WorkUnits represent internal processing required
   21:     /// to resolve that Pulse.
   22:     /// A Moment records the completed outcome.
   23:     /// Timelines preserve ordered Moments.
   24:     /// Epochs preserve collections of Timelines.
   25:     /// </summary>
   26:     public sealed class UnitCertificationTests
   27:     {
   28:         [Fact]
   29:         public void PulseReceivesStableNonEmptyIdentity()
   30:         {
   31:             var request =
   32:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
   33:                     "request");
   34: 
   35:             var pulse =
   36:                 new Pulse(
   37:                     request);
   38: 
   39:             Assert.NotEqual(
   40:                 Guid.Empty,
   41:                 pulse.Id);
   42: 
   43:             Assert.Same(
   44:                 request,
   45:                 pulse.Request);
   46: 
   47:             Assert.Equal(
   48:                 PulseStatus.Created,
   49:                 pulse.Status);
   50: 
   51:             Assert.False(
   52:                 pulse.IsTerminal);
   53:         }
   54: 
   55:         [Fact]
   56:         public void PulseEnforcesLifecycleOrder()
   57:         {
   58:             var pulse =
   59:                 new Pulse(
   60:                     new TestRuntimeObject(
   61:                         "request"));
   62: 
   63:             Assert.False(
   64:                 pulse.TryMarkPending());
   65: 
   66:             Assert.False(
   67:                 pulse.TryMarkReady());
   68: 
   69:             Assert.False(
   70:                 pulse.TryBeginProcessing());
   71: 
   72:             Assert.False(
   73:                 pulse.TryComplete());
   74: 
   75:             Assert.True(
   76:                 pulse.TryBeginDecomposition());
   77: 
   78:             Assert.Equal(
   79:                 PulseStatus.Decomposing,
   80:                 pulse.Status);
   81: 
   82:             Assert.True(
   83:                 pulse.TryMarkPending());
   84: 
   85:             Assert.Equal(
   86:                 PulseStatus.Pending,
   87:                 pulse.Status);
   88: 
   89:             Assert.True(
   90:                 pulse.TryMarkReady());
   91: 
   92:             Assert.Equal(
   93:                 PulseStatus.Ready,
   94:                 pulse.Status);
   95: 
   96:             Assert.True(
   97:                 pulse.TryBeginProcessing());
   98: 
   99:             Assert.Equal(
  100:                 PulseStatus.Processing,
  101:                 pulse.Status);
  102: 
  103:             Assert.True(
  104:                 pulse.TryComplete());
  105: 
  106:             Assert.Equal(
  107:                 PulseStatus.Completed,
  108:                 pulse.Status);
  109: 
  110:             Assert.True(
  111:                 pulse.IsTerminal);
  112: 
  113:             Assert.False(
  114:                 pulse.TryComplete());
  115: 
  116:             Assert.False(
  117:                 pulse.TryFail());
  118: 
  119:             Assert.False(
  120:                 pulse.TryCancel());
  121:         }
  122:

  488:         [Fact]
  489:         
  490:         public void ArbitraryRuntimeObjectSurvivesCompleteUnitRoute()
  491:         {
  492:             TestLog.Section(
  493:                 "Wilds Temporal Unit Certification");
  494: 
  495:             var runtimeObject =
  496:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
  497:                     "consumer-runtime-object");
  498: 
  499:             var pulse =
  500:                 new Pulse(
  501:                     runtimeObject);
  502: 
  503:             TestLog.Test(
  504:                 "Complete Pulse -> WorkUnit -> Moment -> Timeline -> Epoch route");
  505: 
  506:             TestLog.State(
  507:                 $"Pulse created | Id={pulse.Id} | Status={pulse.Status}");
  508: 
  509:             Assert.Same(
  510:                 runtimeObject,
  511:                 pulse.Request);
  512: 
  513:             Assert.True(
  514:                 pulse.TryBeginDecomposition());
  515: 
  516:             TestLog.State(
  517:                 $"Pulse decomposing | Status={pulse.Status}");
  518: 
  519:             var work =
  520:                 new WorkUnit(
  521:                     pulse.Id,
  522:                     pulse.Request);
  523: 
  524:             TestLog.Task(
  525:                 $"WorkUnit created | Id={work.Id} | PulseId={work.PulseId}");
  526: 
  527:             Assert.Same(
  528:                 runtimeObject,
  529:                 work.Payload);
  530: 
  531:             TestLog.State(
  532:                 "Request reference preserved in WorkUnit payload");
  533: 
  534:             Assert.True(
  535:                 work.TryMarkPending());
  536: 
  537:             Assert.True(
  538:                 pulse.TryMarkPending());
  539: 
  540:             TestLog.State(
  541:                 $"Pulse pending | Status={pulse.Status}");
  542: 
  543:             Assert.True(
  544:                 pulse.TryMarkReady());
  545: 
  546:             TestLog.State(
  547:                 $"Pulse ready | Status={pulse.Status}");
  548: 
  549:             Assert.True(
  550:                 pulse.TryBeginProcessing());
  551: 
  552:             Assert.True(
  553:                 work.TryBeginProcessing());
  554: 
  555:             TestLog.Task(
  556:                 $"WorkUnit processing | Status={work.Status}");
  557: 
  558:             Assert.True(
  559:                 work.TryComplete(
  560:                     runtimeObject));
  561: 
  562:             Assert.Same(
  563:                 runtimeObject,
  564:                 work.Result);
  565: 
  566:             TestLog.Task(
  567:                 $"WorkUnit completed | Status={work.Status}");
  568: 
  569:             TestLog.State(
  570:                 "Runtime object reference preserved through WorkUnit result");
  571: 
  572:             Assert.True(
  573:                 pulse.TryComplete());
  574: 
  575:             TestLog.State(
  576:                 $"Pulse completed | Status={pulse.Status}");
  577: 
  578:             var moment =
  579:                 new Moment(
  580:                     pulse.Id,
  581:                     work.Result,
  582:                     new[]
  583:                     {
  584:                         work.Id
  585:                     });
  586: 
  587:             Assert.Equal(
  588:                 pulse.Id,
  589:                 moment.PulseId);
  590: 
  591:             Assert.Same(
  592:                 runtimeObject,
  593:                 moment.Result);
  594: 
  595:             Assert.Single(
  596:                 moment.WorkUnitIds);
  597: 
  598:             Assert.Equal(
  599:                 work.Id,
  600:                 moment.WorkUnitIds[0]);
  601: 
  602:             TestLog.State(
  603:                 $"Moment recorded | Id={moment.Id} | PulseId={moment.PulseId}");
  604: 
  605:             var timeline =
  606:                 new Timeline();
  607: 
  608:             Assert.True(
  609:                 timeline.AddMoment(
  610:                     moment));
  611: 
  612:             TestLog.State(
  613:                 $"Timeline recorded Moment | Moments={timeline.Count}");
  614: 
  615:             var epoch =
  616:                 new Epoch();
  617: 
  618:             Assert.True(
  619:                 epoch.AddTimeline(
  620:                     timeline));
  621: 
  622:             TestLog.State(
  623:                 $"Epoch recorded Timeline | Timelines={epoch.Count}");
  624: 
  625:             Assert.Same(
  626:                 runtimeObject,
  627:                 epoch.Timelines[0]
  628:                     .Moments[0]
  629:                     .Result);
  630: 
  631:             TestLog.Pass(
  632:                 "Exact runtime object survived " +
  633:                 "Pulse -> WorkUnit -> Moment -> Timeline -> Epoch");
  634:         }
  635: 
  636:         private sealed class TestRuntimeObject
  637:         {
  638:             public TestRuntimeObject(
  639:                 string name)
  640:             {
  641:                 Name = name;
  642:             }
  643: 
  644:             public string Name { get; }
  645:         }
  646:     }
  647: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\WildsEngineCertification.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using System.Threading;
    4: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core;
    6: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    7: using WildsEngine.Core.Tests;
    8: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    9: using WildsEngine.Tests.Registry;
   10: 
   11: namespace WildsEngine.Tests
   12: {
   13:     public static class WildsEngineCertification
   14:     {
   15:         private static readonly List<string> Passed =
   16:             new List<string>();
   17: 
   18:         public static async Task RunAllAsync()
   19:         {
   20:             Passed.Clear();
   21: 
   22:             TestLog.Write(
   23:                 "=== WildsEngine Composition Tests ===");
   24: 
   25:             Run(
   26:                 "Constructs isolated engine",
   27:                 ConstructsIsolatedEngine);
   28: 
   29:             Run(
   30:                 "Initialize is idempotent",
   31:                 InitializeIsIdempotent);
   32: 
   33:             Run(
   34:                 "Shutdown is idempotent",
   35:                 ShutdownIsIdempotent);
   36: 
   37:             await RunAsync(
   38:                 "Rejects processing before initialization",
   39:                 RejectsProcessingBeforeInitialization);
   40: 
   41:             await RunAsync(
   42:                 "Executes instruction lease",
   43:                 ExecutesInstructionLease);
   44: 
   45:             await RunAsync(
   46:                 "Reuses instruction processor",
   47:                 ReusesInstructionProcessor);
   48: 
   49:             await RunAsync(
   50:                 "Returns processor after operation failure",
   51:                 ReturnsProcessorAfterOperationFailure);
   52: 
   53:             await RunAsync(
   54:                 "Honors cancellation while waiting",
   55:                 HonorsCancellationWhileWaiting);
   56: 
   57:             Run(
   58:                 "Rejects use after disposal",
   59:                 RejectsUseAfterDisposal);
   60: 
   61:             await RunAsync(
   62:                 "Concurrent load respects limit",
   63:                 ConcurrentLoadRespectsLimit);
   64: 
   65:             TestLog.Write(
   66:                 $"WildsEngine certification passed: " +
   67:                 $"{Passed.Count}/{Passed.Count}");
   68:         }
   69: 
   70:         private static WildsEngineOptions Options(
   71:             int retainedInstructions = 4,
   72:             int concurrentInstructions = 4,
   73:             int retainedTasks = 8,
   74:             int concurrentTasks = 8)
   75:         {
   76:             return new WildsEngineOptions
   77:             {
   78:                 Layer =
   79:                     "certification",
   80: 
   81:                 RetainedInstructionsProcessors =
   82:                     retainedInstructions,
   83: 
   84:                 ConcurrentInstructionsProcessors =
   85:                     concurrentInstructions,
   86: 
   87:                 RetainedTaskProcessors =
   88:                     retainedTasks,
   89: 
   90:                 ConcurrentTaskProcessors =
   91:                     concurrentTasks
   92:             };
   93:         }
   94: 
   95:         private static IComponent CreateComponent(
   96:             string layer)
   97:         {
   98:             return new TestComponent(
   99:                 layer);
  100:         }
  101: 
  102:         private static Core.WildsEngine CreateEngine(
  103:             string layer,
  104:             int retainedInstructions = 4,
  105:             int concurrentInstructions = 4,
  106:             int retainedTasks = 8,
  107:             int concurrentTasks = 8)
  108:         {
  109:             var component =
  110:                 CreateComponent(
  111:                     layer);
  112: 
  113:             var options =
  114:                 Options(
  115:                     retainedInstructions,
  116:                     concurrentInstructions,
  117:                     retainedTasks,
  118:                     concurrentTasks);
  119: 
  120:             return new Core.WildsEngine(
  121:                 component,
  122:                 options);
  123:         }
  124: 
  125:         private static void ConstructsIsolatedEngine()
  126:         {
  127:             using var a =
  128:                 CreateEngine(
  129:                     "certification.engine.a");
  130: 
  131:             using var b =
  132:                 CreateEngine(
  133:                     "certification.engine.b");
  134: 
  135:             Assert(
  136:                 !ReferenceEquals(a, b),
  137:                 "Engine instances must be independent.");
  138: 
  139:             Assert(
  140:                 !ReferenceEquals(
  141:                     a.Instructions,
  142:                     b.Instructions),
  143:                 "Instruction pools must be instance-owned.");
  144:         }
  145: 
  146:         private static void InitializeIsIdempotent()
  147:         {
  148:             using var engine =
  149:                 CreateEngine(
  150:                     "certification.initialize");
  151: 
  152:             Assert(
  153:                 engine.Initialize(),
  154:                 "First Initialize() should succeed.");
  155: 
  156:             Assert(
  157:                 engine.IsInitialized,
  158:                 "Engine should report initialized.");
  159: 
  160:             Assert(
  161:                 !engine.Initialize(),
  162:                 "Second Initialize() should return false.");
  163:         }
  164: 
  165:         private static void ShutdownIsIdempotent()
  166:         {
  167:             using var engine =
  168:                 CreateEngine(
  169:                     "certification.shutdown");
  170: 
  171:             Assert(
  172:                 engine.Initialize(),
  173:                 "Initialize() should succeed.");
  174: 
  175:             Assert(
  176:                 engine.Shutdown(),
  177:                 "First Shutdown() should succeed.");
  178: 
  179:             Assert(
  180:                 !engine.IsInitialized,
  181:                 "Engine should report stopped.");
  182: 
  183:             Assert(
  184:                 !engine.Shutdown(),
  185:                 "Second Shutdown() should return false.");
  186:         }
  187: 
  188:         private static async Task
  189:             RejectsProcessingBeforeInitialization()
  190:         {
  191:             using var engine =
  192:                 CreateEngine(
  193:                     "certification.uninitialized");
  194: 
  195:             await AssertThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(
  196:                 async () =>
  197:                 {
  198:                     await engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  199:                         (_, __) =>
  200:                             new ValueTask<int>(1));
  201:                 });
  202:         }
  203: 
  204:         private static async Task
  205:             ExecutesInstructionLease()
  206:         {
  207:             using var engine =
  208:                 CreateEngine(
  209:                     "certification.execute");
  210: 
  211:             Assert(
  212:                 engine.Initialize(),
  213:                 "Initialize() should succeed.");
  214: 
  215:             var result =
  216:                 await engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  217:                     (processor, _) =>
  218:                     {
  219:                         Assert(
  220:                             processor != null,
  221:                             "Processor must be supplied.");
  222: 
  223:                         return new ValueTask<int>(
  224:                             42);
  225:                     });
  226: 
  227:             LogPool(
  228:                 engine,
  229:                 "After instruction execution");
  230: 
  231:             Assert(
  232:                 result == 42,
  233:                 "Instruction result should flow through the engine.");
  234: 
  235:             Assert(
  236:                 engine.Instructions.ActiveCount == 0,
  237:                 "Lease should be returned after execution.");
  238: 
  239:             Assert(
  240:                 engine.Instructions.AvailableCount == 1,
  241:                 "Returned processor should be retained.");
  242:         }
  243: 
  244:         private static async Task
  245:             ReusesInstructionProcessor()
  246:         {
  247:             using var engine =
  248:                 CreateEngine(
  249:                     "certification.reuse",
  250:                     retainedInstructions: 1,
  251:                     concurrentInstructions: 1);
  252: 
  253:             Assert(
  254:                 engine.Initialize(),
  255:                 "Initialize() should succeed.");
  256: 
  257:             IInstructionsProcessor? first =
  258:                 null;
  259: 
  260:             IInstructionsProcessor? second =
  261:                 null;
  262: 
  263:             await engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  264:                 (processor, _) =>
  265:                 {
  266:                     first =
  267:                         processor;
  268: 
  269:                     return new ValueTask<int>(
  270:                         1);
  271:                 });
  272: 
  273:             await engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  274:                 (processor, _) =>
  275:                 {
  276:                     second =
  277:                         processor;
  278: 
  279:                     return new ValueTask<int>(
  280:                         2);
  281:                 });
  282: 
  283:             Assert(
  284:                 first != null,
  285:                 "First instruction processor should be captured.");
  286: 
  287:             Assert(
  288:                 second != null,
  289:                 "Second instruction processor should be captured.");
  290: 
  291:             Assert(
  292:                 ReferenceEquals(
  293:                     first,
  294:                     second),
  295:                 "Sequential leases should reuse the retained processor.");
  296: 
  297:             Assert(
  298:                 engine.Instructions.CreatedCount == 1,
  299:                 "Only one instruction processor should have been created.");
  300:         }
  301: 
  302:         private static async Task
  303:             ReturnsProcessorAfterOperationFailure()
  304:         {
  305:             using var engine =
  306:                 CreateEngine(
  307:                     "certification.failure",
  308:                     retainedInstructions: 1,
  309:                     concurrentInstructions: 1);
  310: 
  311:             Assert(
  312:                 engine.Initialize(),
  313:                 "Initialize() should succeed.");
  314: 
  315:             await AssertThrowsAsync<ExpectedTestException>(
  316:                 async () =>
  317:                 {
  318:                     await engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  319:                         (_, __) =>
  320:                             throw new ExpectedTestException());
  321:                 });
  322: 
  323:             Assert(
  324:                 engine.Instructions.ActiveCount == 0,
  325:                 "Failed operation must not leak an active lease.");
  326: 
  327:             Assert(
  328:                 engine.Instructions.AvailableCount == 1,
  329:                 "Processor should return after operation failure.");
  330:         }
  331: 
  332:         private static async Task
  333:             HonorsCancellationWhileWaiting()
  334:         {
  335:             using var engine =
  336:                 CreateEngine(
  337:                     "certification.cancellation",
  338:                     retainedInstructions: 1,
  339:                     concurrentInstructions: 1);
  340: 
  341:             Assert(
  342:                 engine.Initialize(),
  343:                 "Initialize() should succeed.");
  344: 
  345:             using var held =
  346:                 engine.Instructions.Rent();
  347: 
  348:             using var cancellation =
  349:                 new CancellationTokenSource();
  350: 
  351:             cancellation.Cancel();
  352: 
  353:             await AssertThrowsAsync<OperationCanceledException>(
  354:                 async () =>
  355:                 {
  356:                     await engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  357:                         (_, __) =>
  358:                             new ValueTask<int>(1),
  359:                         cancellation.Token);
  360:                 });
  361: 
  362:             Assert(
  363:                 engine.Instructions.ActiveCount == 1,
  364:                 "Canceled waiter must not acquire a second lease.");
  365:         }
  366: 
  367:         private static void RejectsUseAfterDisposal()
  368:         {
  369:             var engine =
  370:                 CreateEngine(
  371:                     "certification.disposal");
  372: 
  373:             engine.Dispose();
  374: 
  375:             AssertThrows<ObjectDisposedException>(
  376:                 () =>
  377:                     engine.Initialize());
  378:         }
  379: 
  380:         private static async Task
  381:             ConcurrentLoadRespectsLimit()
  382:         {
  383:             const int limit = 3;
  384:             const int count = 24;
  385: 
  386:             using var engine =
  387:                 CreateEngine(
  388:                     "certification.concurrent",
  389:                     retainedInstructions: limit,
  390:                     concurrentInstructions: limit);
  391: 
  392:             Assert(
  393:                 engine.Initialize(),
  394:                 "Initialize() should succeed.");
  395: 
  396:             var current = 0;
  397:             var maximum = 0;
  398: 
  399:             var release =
  400:                 new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(
  401:                     TaskCreationOptions
  402:                         .RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
  403: 
  404:             var entered =
  405:                 new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(
  406:                     TaskCreationOptions
  407:                         .RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
  408: 
  409:             var tasks =
  410:                 new List<Task<int>>(
  411:                     count);
  412: 
  413:             for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
  414:             {
  415:                 tasks.Add(
  416:                     engine.ExecuteInstructionAsync<int>(
  417:                             async (_, token) =>
  418:                             {
  419:                                 var now =
  420:                                     Interlocked.Increment(
  421:                                         ref current);
  422: 
  423:                                 UpdateMaximum(
  424:                                     ref maximum,
  425:                                     now);
  426: 
  427:                                 if (now >= limit)
  428:                                 {
  429:                                     entered.TrySetResult(
  430:                                         true);
  431:                                 }
  432: 
  433:                                 try
  434:                                 {
  435:                                     await release.Task
  436:                                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  437: 
  438:                                     token.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  439: 
  440:                                     return 1;
  441:                                 }
  442:                                 finally
  443:                                 {
  444:                                     Interlocked.Decrement(
  445:                                         ref current);
  446:                                 }
  447:                             })
  448:                         .AsTask());
  449:             }
  450: 
  451:             await entered.Task
  452:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
  453: 
  454:             LogPool(
  455:                 engine,
  456:                 "At concurrency limit");
  457: 
  458:             Assert(
  459:                 engine.Instructions.ActiveCount <= limit,
  460:                 "Active leases exceeded MaximumConcurrent.");
  461: 
  462:             release.TrySetResult(
  463:                 true);
  464: 
  465:             await Task.WhenAll(
  466:                     tasks)
  467:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
  468: 
  469:             Assert(
  470:                 maximum == limit,
  471:                 "Concurrent load did not exercise the configured limit.");
  472: 
  473:             Assert(
  474:                 engine.Instructions.ActiveCount == 0,
  475:                 "All concurrent leases should be returned.");
  476: 
  477:             Assert(
  478:                 engine.Instructions.CreatedCount <= limit,
  479:                 "Pool created more processors than " +
  480:                 "concurrent leasing permits.");
  481:         }
  482: 
  483:         private static void UpdateMaximum(
  484:             ref int target,
  485:             int value)
  486:         {
  487:             while (true)
  488:             {
  489:                 var current =
  490:                     Volatile.Read(
  491:                         ref target);
  492: 
  493:                 if (value <= current)
  494:                     return;
  495: 
  496:                 if (Interlocked.CompareExchange(
  497:                         ref target,
  498:                         value,
  499:                         current) == current)
  500:                 {
  501:                     return;
  502:                 }
  503:             }
  504:         }
  505: 
  506:         private static void Run(
  507:             string name,
  508:             Action test)
  509:         {
  510:             TestLog.Test(
  511:                 name);
  512: 
  513:             try
  514:             {
  515:                 test();
  516: 
  517:                 Passed.Add(
  518:                     name);
  519: 
  520:                 TestLog.Pass(
  521:                     name);
  522:             }
  523:             catch (Exception exception)
  524:             {
  525:                 TestLog.Fail(
  526:                     name,
  527:                     exception);
  528: 
  529:                 throw;
  530:             }
  531:         }
  532: 
  533:         private static async Task RunAsync(
  534:             string name,
  535:             Func<Task> test)
  536:         {
  537:             TestLog.Test(
  538:                 name);
  539: 
  540:             try
  541:             {
  542:                 await test()
  543:                     .ConfigureAwait(false);
  544: 
  545:                 Passed.Add(
  546:                     name);
  547: 
  548:                 TestLog.Pass(
  549:                     name);
  550:             }
  551:             catch (Exception exception)
  552:             {
  553:                 TestLog.Fail(
  554:                     name,
  555:                     exception);
  556: 
  557:                 throw;
  558:             }
  559:         }
  560: 
  561:         private static void LogPool(
  562:             Core.WildsEngine engine,
  563:             string label)
  564:         {
  565:             TestLog.State(
  566:                 $"{label} | " +
  567:                 $"Active={engine.Instructions.ActiveCount}, " +
  568:                 $"Available={engine.Instructions.AvailableCount}, " +
  569:                 $"Created={engine.Instructions.CreatedCount}, " +
  570:                 $"Maximum={engine.Instructions.MaximumConcurrent}");
  571:         }
  572: 
  573:         private static void Assert(
  574:             bool condition,
  575:             string message)
  576:         {
  577:             if (!condition)
  578:             {
  579:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  580:                     message);
  581:             }
  582:         }
  583: 
  584:         private static void AssertThrows<TException>(
  585:             Action action)
  586:             where TException : Exception
  587:         {
  588:             try
  589:             {
  590:                 action();
  591:             }
  592:             catch (TException)
  593:             {
  594:                 TestLog.Expected(
  595:                     typeof(TException).Name);
  596: 
  597:                 return;
  598:             }
  599: 
  600:             throw new InvalidOperationException(
  601:                 $"Expected {typeof(TException).Name}.");
  602:         }
  603: 
  604:         private static async Task AssertThrowsAsync<TException>(
  605:             Func<Task> action)
  606:             where TException : Exception
  607:         {
  608:             try
  609:             {
  610:                 await action()
  611:                     .ConfigureAwait(false);
  612:             }
  613:             catch (TException)
  614:             {
  615:                 TestLog.Expected(
  616:                     typeof(TException).Name);
  617: 
  618:                 return;
  619:             }
  620: 
  621:             throw new InvalidOperationException(
  622:                 $"Expected {typeof(TException).Name}.");
  623:         }
  624: 
  625:         private sealed class ExpectedTestException :
  626:             Exception
  627:         {
  628:         }
  629:     }
  630: }

    1: using System;
    2: using System.Threading;
    3: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
    6: using WildsEngine.Tests.Registry;
    7: using Xunit;
    8: 
    9: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests
   10: {
   11:     /// <summary>
   12:     /// Certifies that WildsEngine.ExecuteAsync owns the
   13:     /// complete request lifecycle:
   14:     ///
   15:     /// request
   16:     ///     -> Pulse
   17:     ///     -> WorkUnit
   18:     ///     -> coordinator
   19:     ///     -> Moment
   20:     ///     -> Timeline
   21:     ///     -> Epoch
   22:     /// </summary>
   23:     public sealed class WildsEngineExecuteIntegrationTests
   24:     {
   25:         [Fact]
   26:         public async Task ExecuteAsyncRecordsSuccessfulMoment()
   27:         {
   28:             TestLog.Section(
   29:                 "WildsEngine ExecuteAsync Integration");
   30: 
   31:             TestLog.Test(
   32:                 "Successful request becomes completed Moment");
   33: 
   34:             var component =
   35:                 CreateComponent(
   36:                     "execute.success");
   37: 
   38:             using var engine =
   39:                 CreateEngine(
   40:                     component);
   41: 
   42:             Assert.True(
   43:                 engine.Initialize());
   44: 
   45:             var request =
   46:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
   47:                     "request");
   48: 
   49:             var result =
   50:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
   51:                     "result");
   52: 
   53:             var moment =
   54:                 await engine.ExecuteAsync(
   55:                         request,
   56:                         (
   57:                             observedRequest,
   58:                             cancellationToken) =>
   59:                         {
   60:                             cancellationToken
   61:                                 .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
   62: 
   63:                             Assert.Same(
   64:                                 request,
   65:                                 observedRequest);
   66: 
   67:                             return new ValueTask<object?>(
   68:                                 result);
   69:                         });
   70: 
   71:             Assert.Equal(
   72:                 MomentOutcome.Completed,
   73:                 moment.Outcome);
   74: 
   75:             Assert.Same(
   76:                 result,
   77:                 moment.Result);
   78: 
   79:             Assert.Null(
   80:                 moment.Exception);
   81: 
   82:             Assert.Single(
   83:                 moment.WorkUnitIds);
   84: 
   85:             Assert.Equal(
   86:                 1,
   87:                 engine.Timeline.Count);
   88: 
   89:             Assert.Same(
   90:                 moment,
   91:                 engine.Timeline.Moments[0]);
   92: 
   93:             Assert.Equal(
   94:                 1,
   95:                 engine.Epoch.Count);
   96: 
   97:             Assert.Same(
   98:                 engine.Timeline,
   99:                 engine.Epoch.Timelines[0]);
  100: 
  101:             TestLog.State(
  102:                 $"Moment recorded | " +
  103:                 $"Outcome={moment.Outcome} | " +
  104:                 $"TimelineMoments={engine.Timeline.Count}");
  105: 
  106:             TestLog.Pass(
  107:                 "ExecuteAsync completed and recorded request history");
  108:         }
  109: 
  110:         [Fact]
  111:         public async Task ExecuteAsyncPreservesArbitraryRuntimeIdentity()
  112:         {
  113:             TestLog.Test(
  114:                 "ExecuteAsync preserves arbitrary runtime identity");
  115: 
  116:             var component =
  117:                 CreateComponent(
  118:                     "execute.identity");
  119: 
  120:             using var engine =
  121:                 CreateEngine(
  122:                     component);
  123:

  125:                 engine.Initialize());

  128:                 new TestRuntimeObject(
  129:                     "consumer-owned-object");

  132:                 await engine.ExecuteAsync(

  134:                         (
  135:                             request,

  276:

  278:                 engine.Timeline.Moments);
  279: 
  280:             Assert.Same(
  281:                 moment,
  282:                 engine.Timeline.Moments[0]);
  283: 
  284:             TestLog.Pass(
  285:                 "Cancellation was retained as history");
  286:         }

  391:         private static Core.WildsEngine CreateEngine(
  392:             IComponent component)
  393:         {
  394:             return new Core.WildsEngine(
  395:                 component,
  396:                 new WildsEngineOptions
  397:                 {
  398:                     Layer =
  399:                         "certification.execute",
  400: 
  401:                     RetainedInstructionsProcessors =
  402:                         1,
  403: 
  404:                     ConcurrentInstructionsProcessors =
  405:                         1,
  406: 
  407:                     RetainedTaskProcessors =
  408:                         1,
  409: 
  410:                     ConcurrentTaskProcessors =
  411:                         1
  412:                 });
  413:         }
  414: 
  415:         private static IComponent CreateComponent(
  416:             string layer)
  417:         {
  418:             return new TestComponent(
  419:                 layer);
  420:         }
  421:

  431:         }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Abstractions\Base\NeighborDistance.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: 
    3: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Topology
    4: {
    5:     /// <summary>
    6:     /// Defines the canonical Wilds rules for relational distance.
    7:     ///
    8:     /// Distance is abstract and does not imply a physical
    9:     /// unit of measurement.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public static class NeighborDistance
   12:     {
   13:         public static bool IsValid(
   14:             double distance)
   15:         {
   16:             return
   17:                 !double.IsNaN(distance) &&
   18:                 !double.IsInfinity(distance) &&
   19:                 distance >= 0d;
   20:         }
   21: 
   22:         public static double Validate(
   23:             double distance,
   24:             string parameterName)
   25:         {
   26:             if (!IsValid(distance))
   27:             {
   28:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   29:                     parameterName,
   30:                     distance,
   31:                     "Neighbor distance must be finite and non-negative.");
   32:             }
   33: 
   34:             return distance;
   35:         }
   36: 
   37:         public static bool IsAdjacent(
   38:             double distance)
   39:         {
   40:             return
   41:                 Validate(
   42:                     distance,
   43:                     nameof(distance)) == 1d;
   44:         }
   45:     }
   46: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Abstractions\Pooling\IObjectPool.cs
############################################################
    1: using System.Threading;
    2: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Provides bounded synchronous and asynchronous leasing
    8:     /// of reusable objects.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public interface IObjectPool<T>
   11:         where T : class
   12:     {
   13:         int AvailableCount { get; }
   14: 
   15:         int CreatedCount { get; }
   16: 
   17:         int ActiveCount { get; }
   18: 
   19:         int MaximumRetained { get; }
   20: 
   21:         int MaximumConcurrent { get; }
   22: 
   23:         ObjectLease<T> Rent();
   24: 
   25:         ValueTask<ObjectLease<T>> RentAsync(
   26:             CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
   27: 
   28:         bool Return(T instance);
   29:     }
   30: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Abstractions\Pooling\ObjectLease.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Threading;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Represents temporary ownership of an object rented from a pool.
    8:     /// Disposing the lease returns the object to its originating pool.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public sealed class ObjectLease<T> : IDisposable
   11:         where T : class
   12:     {
   13:         private IObjectPool<T>? _pool;
   14:         private T? _instance;
   15: 
   16:         internal ObjectLease(
   17:             IObjectPool<T> pool,
   18:             T instance)
   19:         {
   20:             _pool = pool ??
   21:                     throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(pool));
   22: 
   23:             _instance = instance ??
   24:                         throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(instance));
   25:         }
   26: 
   27:         public T Instance
   28:         {
   29:             get
   30:             {
   31:                 return _instance ??
   32:                        throw new ObjectDisposedException(
   33:                            nameof(ObjectLease<T>));
   34:             }
   35:         }
   36: 
   37:         public void Dispose()
   38:         {
   39:             var instance =
   40:                 Interlocked.Exchange(
   41:                     ref _instance,
   42:                     null);
   43: 
   44:             var pool =
   45:                 Interlocked.Exchange(
   46:                     ref _pool,
   47:                     null);
   48: 
   49:             if (instance == null || pool == null)
   50:                 return;
   51: 
   52:             pool.Return(instance);
   53:         }
   54:     }
   55: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Abstractions\Pooling\ObjectPool.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Concurrent;
    3: using System.Threading;
    4: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    6: 
    7: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Pooling
    8: {
    9:     /// <summary>
   10:     /// Thread-safe object pool with bounded retention
   11:     /// and bounded concurrent leasing.
   12:     /// </summary>
   13:     public class ObjectPool<T> :
   14:         IObjectPool<T>,
   15:         IDisposable
   16:         where T : class
   17:     {
   18:         private readonly ConcurrentQueue<T> _available =
   19:             new ConcurrentQueue<T>();
   20: 
   21:         private readonly Func<T> _factory;
   22: 
   23:         private readonly SemaphoreSlim _concurrencyGate;
   24: 
   25:         private int _availableCount;
   26:         private int _createdCount;
   27:         private int _activeCount;
   28:         private int _disposed;
   29: 
   30:         public ObjectPool(
   31:             Func<T> factory,
   32:             int maximumRetained,
   33:             int maximumConcurrent)
   34:         {
   35:             _factory = factory ??
   36:                        throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(factory));
   37: 
   38:             if (maximumRetained < 0)
   39:             {
   40:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   41:                     nameof(maximumRetained));
   42:             }
   43: 
   44:             if (maximumConcurrent <= 0)
   45:             {
   46:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   47:                     nameof(maximumConcurrent));
   48:             }
   49: 
   50:             MaximumRetained = maximumRetained;
   51:             MaximumConcurrent = maximumConcurrent;
   52: 
   53:             _concurrencyGate = new SemaphoreSlim(
   54:                 maximumConcurrent,
   55:                 maximumConcurrent);
   56:         }
   57: 
   58:         public int AvailableCount =>
   59:             Volatile.Read(ref _availableCount);
   60: 
   61:         public int CreatedCount =>
   62:             Volatile.Read(ref _createdCount);
   63: 
   64:         public int ActiveCount =>
   65:             Volatile.Read(ref _activeCount);
   66: 
   67:         public int MaximumRetained { get; }
   68: 
   69:         public int MaximumConcurrent { get; }
   70: 
   71:         public ObjectLease<T> Rent()
   72:         {
   73:             ThrowIfDisposed();
   74: 
   75:             _concurrencyGate.Wait();
   76: 
   77:             try
   78:             {
   79:                 // The pool may have been disposed while waiting.
   80:                 ThrowIfDisposed();
   81: 
   82:                 return CreateLease();
   83:             }
   84:             catch
   85:             {
   86:                 _concurrencyGate.Release();
   87: 
   88:                 throw;
   89:             }
   90:         }
   91: 
   92:         public async ValueTask<ObjectLease<T>> RentAsync(
   93:             CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
   94:         {
   95:             ThrowIfDisposed();
   96: 
   97:             await _concurrencyGate
   98:                 .WaitAsync(cancellationToken)
   99:                 .ConfigureAwait(false);
  100: 
  101:             try
  102:             {
  103:                 // The pool may have been disposed while waiting.
  104:                 ThrowIfDisposed();
  105: 
  106:                 return CreateLease();
  107:             }
  108:             catch
  109:             {
  110:                 _concurrencyGate.Release();
  111: 
  112:                 throw;
  113:             }
  114:         }
  115: 
  116:         public bool Return(T instance)
  117:         {
  118:             if (instance == null)
  119:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(instance));
  120: 
  121:             var remainingActive =
  122:                 Interlocked.Decrement(ref _activeCount);
  123: 
  124:             if (remainingActive < 0)
  125:             {
  126:                 Interlocked.Increment(ref _activeCount);
  127: 
  128:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  129:                     "An object was returned without an active lease.");
  130:             }
  131: 
  132:             try
  133:             {
  134:                 if (Volatile.Read(ref _disposed) != 0)
  135:                 {
  136:                     DisposeInstance(instance);
  137: 
  138:                     return false;
  139:                 }
  140: 
  141:                 try
  142:                 {
  143:                     if (instance is
  144:                         IResettablePoolObject
  145:                         resettable)
  146:                     {
  147:                         if (!resettable.TryReset())
  148:                         {
  149:                             DisposeInstance(instance);
  150: 
  151:                             return false;
  152:                         }
  153:                     }
  154:                 }
  155:                 catch
  156:                 {
  157:                     DisposeInstance(instance);
  158: 
  159:                     throw;
  160:                 }
  161: 
  162:                 var retained =
  163:                     TryRetain(instance);
  164: 
  165:                 if (!retained)
  166:                     DisposeInstance(instance);
  167: 
  168:                 return retained;
  169:             }
  170:             finally
  171:             {
  172:                 _concurrencyGate.Release();
  173:             }
  174:         }
  175: 
  176:         public void Dispose()
  177:         {
  178:             if (Interlocked.Exchange(
  179:                     ref _disposed,
  180:                     1) != 0)
  181:             {
  182:                 return;
  183:             }
  184: 
  185:             while (_available.TryDequeue(out var instance))
  186:             {
  187:                 Interlocked.Decrement(
  188:                     ref _availableCount);
  189: 
  190:                 DisposeInstance(instance);
  191:             }
  192: 
  193:             // Intentionally do not dispose the semaphore here.
  194:             // Active leases and waiting renters may still need
  195:             // to interact with it while observing pool disposal.
  196:         }
  197: 
  198:         private ObjectLease<T>
  199:             CreateLease()
  200:         {
  201:             T instance;
  202: 
  203:             if (_available.TryDequeue(out var pooled))
  204:             {
  205:                 Interlocked.Decrement(
  206:                     ref _availableCount);
  207: 
  208:                 instance = pooled;
  209:             }
  210:             else
  211:             {
  212:                 instance = _factory();
  213: 
  214:                 if (instance == null)
  215:                 {
  216:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  217:                         "The object pool factory returned null.");
  218:                 }
  219: 
  220:                 Interlocked.Increment(
  221:                     ref _createdCount);
  222:             }
  223: 
  224:             Interlocked.Increment(
  225:                 ref _activeCount);
  226: 
  227:             return new ObjectLease<T>(
  228:                 this,
  229:                 instance);
  230:         }
  231: 
  232:         private bool TryRetain(T instance)
  233:         {
  234:             while (true)
  235:             {
  236:                 if (Volatile.Read(ref _disposed) != 0)
  237:                     return false;
  238: 
  239:                 var available =
  240:                     Volatile.Read(ref _availableCount);
  241: 
  242:                 if (available >= MaximumRetained)
  243:                     return false;
  244: 
  245:                 if (Interlocked.CompareExchange(
  246:                         ref _availableCount,
  247:                         available + 1,
  248:                         available) != available)
  249:                 {
  250:                     continue;
  251:                 }
  252: 
  253:                 if (Volatile.Read(ref _disposed) != 0)
  254:                 {
  255:                     Interlocked.Decrement(
  256:                         ref _availableCount);
  257: 
  258:                     return false;
  259:                 }
  260: 
  261:                 _available.Enqueue(instance);
  262: 
  263:                 return true;
  264:             }
  265:         }
  266: 
  267:         private static void DisposeInstance(
  268:             T instance)
  269:         {
  270:             if (instance is IDisposable disposable)
  271:                 disposable.Dispose();
  272:         }
  273: 
  274:         private void ThrowIfDisposed()
  275:         {
  276:             if (Volatile.Read(ref _disposed) != 0)
  277:             {
  278:                 throw new ObjectDisposedException(
  279:                     nameof(ObjectPool<T>));
  280:             }
  281:         }
  282:     }
  283: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Contexts\WildsContext.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Objects;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Contexts
    7: {
    8:     public abstract class WildsContext :
    9:         BaseObject,
   10:         IContext
   11:     {
   12:         private readonly Dictionary<string, object?> _variables =
   13:             new Dictionary<string, object?>();
   14: 
   15:         private readonly Dictionary<string, object?> _contextMetadata =
   16:             new Dictionary<string, object?>();
   17: 
   18:         private readonly IComponent _component;
   19: 
   20:         protected WildsContext(
   21:             IComponent component,
   22:             string layer)
   23:             : base(layer)
   24:         {
   25:             _component = component
   26:                 ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(
   27:                     nameof(component));
   28:         }
   29: 
   30:         protected WildsContext(
   31:             Guid id,
   32:             IComponent component,
   33:             string layer)
   34:             : base(id, layer)
   35:         {
   36:             _component = component
   37:                 ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(
   38:                     nameof(component));
   39:         }
   40: 
   41:         public sealed override IComponent Component =>
   42:             _component;
   43: 
   44:         public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?> Variables =>
   45:             _variables;
   46: 
   47:         IReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?> IContext.Metadata =>
   48:             _contextMetadata;
   49: 
   50:         public virtual void SetVariable(
   51:             string key,
   52:             object? value)
   53:         {
   54:             ValidateKey(key);
   55: 
   56:             _variables[key] = value;
   57:         }
   58: 
   59:         public virtual void SetMetadata(
   60:             string key,
   61:             object? value)
   62:         {
   63:             ValidateKey(key);
   64: 
   65:             _contextMetadata[key] = value;
   66:         }
   67: 
   68:         protected bool RemoveVariable(string key)
   69:         {
   70:             ValidateKey(key);
   71: 
   72:             return _variables.Remove(key);
   73:         }
   74: 
   75:         protected bool RemoveMetadata(string key)
   76:         {
   77:             ValidateKey(key);
   78: 
   79:             return _contextMetadata.Remove(key);
   80:         }
   81: 
   82:         protected bool TryGetVariable(
   83:             string key,
   84:             out object? value)
   85:         {
   86:             ValidateKey(key);
   87: 
   88:             return _variables.TryGetValue(
   89:                 key,
   90:                 out value);
   91:         }
   92: 
   93:         protected bool TryGetMetadata(
   94:             string key,
   95:             out object? value)
   96:         {
   97:             ValidateKey(key);
   98: 
   99:             return _contextMetadata.TryGetValue(
  100:                 key,
  101:                 out value);
  102:         }
  103: 
  104:         protected static void ValidateKey(string key)
  105:         {
  106:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(key))
  107:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  108:                     "Context keys cannot be empty.",
  109:                     nameof(key));
  110:         }
  111:     }
  112: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Diagnostics\WildsLog.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: 
    3: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Diagnostics
    4: {
    5:     public static class WildsLog
    6:     {
    7:         public static Action<string>? Writer { get; set; }
    8: 
    9:         public static bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;
   10: 
   11:         public static void Info(string message)
   12:         {
   13:             Write("INFO", message);
   14:         }
   15: 
   16:         public static void Process(string message)
   17:         {
   18:             Write("PROCESS", message);
   19:         }
   20: 
   21:         public static void State(string message)
   22:         {
   23:             Write("STATE", message);
   24:         }
   25: 
   26:         public static void Warning(string message)
   27:         {
   28:             Write("WARN", message);
   29:         }
   30: 
   31:         public static void Error(string message)
   32:         {
   33:             Write("ERROR", message);
   34:         }
   35: 
   36:         private static void Write(
   37:             string category,
   38:             string message)
   39:         {
   40:             if (!Enabled)
   41:                 return;
   42: 
   43:             var formatted =
   44:                 $"[Wilds:{category}] {message}";
   45: 
   46:             if (Writer != null)
   47:             {
   48:                 Writer(formatted);
   49:                 return;
   50:             }
   51: 
   52:             Console.WriteLine(formatted);
   53:         }
   54:     }
   55: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Engines\WildsEngine.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Threading;
    3: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Pooling;
    6: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Coordinator;
    7: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Processor;
    8: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Registry;
    9: using WildsEngine.Core.Units;
   10: 
   11: namespace WildsEngine.Core
   12: {
   13:     /// <summary>
   14:     /// Composition root for one isolated Wilds runtime instance.
   15:     ///
   16:     /// Wilds accepts complete requests as Pulses, owns their
   17:     /// internal WorkUnits, resolves them into Moments, and
   18:     /// records those Moments in its Timeline and Epoch.
   19:     /// </summary>
   20:     public sealed class WildsEngine :
   21:         IDisposable
   22:     {
   23:         private readonly IComponent _component;
   24: 
   25:         private readonly DefaultInstructionsRegistry
   26:             _instructionRegistry;
   27: 
   28:         private readonly DefaultTasksRegistry
   29:             _tasksRegistry;
   30: 
   31:         private readonly ObjectPool<ITaskProcessor>
   32:             _taskProcessors;
   33: 
   34:         private readonly ObjectPool<IInstructionsProcessor>
   35:             _instructionsProcessors;
   36: 
   37:         private readonly Timeline _timeline;
   38:         private readonly Epoch _epoch;
   39: 
   40:         private int _disposed;
   41: 
   42:         public WildsEngine(
   43:             IComponent component,
   44:             WildsEngineOptions options)
   45:         {
   46:             _component = component ??
   47:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   48:                     nameof(component));
   49: 
   50:             if (options == null)
   51:             {
   52:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   53:                     nameof(options));
   54:             }
   55: 
   56:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
   57:                     options.Layer))
   58:             {
   59:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   60:                     "The engine requires a layer.",
   61:                     nameof(options));
   62:             }
   63: 
   64:             Layer =
   65:                 options.Layer.Trim();
   66: 
   67:             _instructionRegistry =
   68:                 new DefaultInstructionsRegistry(
   69:                     _component,
   70:                     $"{Layer}.instruction-registry");
   71: 
   72:             _tasksRegistry =
   73:                 new DefaultTasksRegistry(
   74:                     _component,
   75:                     $"{Layer}.task-registry");
   76: 
   77:             _taskProcessors =
   78:                 new ObjectPool<ITaskProcessor>(
   79:                     factory: () =>
   80:                         new TaskProcessor(
   81:                             _component,
   82:                             $"{Layer}.task-processor",
   83:                             _tasksRegistry),
   84:                     maximumRetained:
   85:                         options.RetainedTaskProcessors,
   86:                     maximumConcurrent:
   87:                         options.ConcurrentTaskProcessors);
   88: 
   89:             _instructionsProcessors =
   90:                 new ObjectPool<IInstructionsProcessor>(
   91:                     factory: () =>
   92:                         new InstructionsProcessor(
   93:                             _component,
   94:                             $"{Layer}.instructions-processor",
   95:                             _instructionRegistry,
   96:                             _taskProcessors),
   97:                     maximumRetained:
   98:                         options.RetainedInstructionsProcessors,
   99:                     maximumConcurrent:
  100:                         options.ConcurrentInstructionsProcessors);
  101: 
  102:             _timeline =
  103:                 new Timeline();
  104: 
  105:             _epoch =
  106:                 new Epoch();
  107: 
  108:             if (!_epoch.AddTimeline(
  109:                     _timeline))
  110:             {
  111:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  112:                     "The engine could not initialize " +
  113:                     "its primary Timeline.");
  114:             }
  115:         }
  116: 
  117:         public string Layer { get; }
  118: 
  119:         public IComponent Component =>
  120:             _component;
  121: 
  122:         public bool IsInitialized { get; private set; }
  123: 
  124:         public IObjectPool<IInstructionsProcessor>
  125:             Instructions =>
  126:                 _instructionsProcessors;
  127: 
  128:         /// <summary>
  129:         /// Contains the ordered Moment history produced by
  130:         /// this engine instance.
  131:         /// </summary>
  132:         public ITimeline Timeline =>
  133:             _timeline;
  134: 
  135:         /// <summary>
  136:         /// Contains the Timeline history owned by this
  137:         /// engine instance.
  138:         /// </summary>
  139:         public IEpoch Epoch =>
  140:             _epoch;
  141: 
  142:         public bool Initialize()
  143:         {
  144:             ThrowIfDisposed();
  145: 
  146:             if (IsInitialized)
  147:                 return false;
  148: 
  149:             _instructionRegistry.Initialize();
  150:             _tasksRegistry.Initialize();
  151: 
  152:             IsInitialized = true;
  153: 
  154:             return true;
  155:         }
  156: 
  157:         /// <summary>
  158:         /// Executes one complete consumer request through the
  159:         /// certified Pulse lifecycle.
  160:         ///
  161:         /// Wilds creates and correlates the Pulse and WorkUnit,
  162:         /// owns their lifecycle transitions, finalizes the
  163:         /// terminal Moment, and records that Moment in the
  164:         /// engine Timeline.
  165:         ///
  166:         /// The supplied operation represents internal work
  167:         /// performed by Wilds or an integration adapter.
  168:         /// </summary>
  169:         public async ValueTask<IMoment> ExecuteAsync(
  170:             object? request,
  171:             Func<
  172:                 object?,
  173:                 CancellationToken,
  174:                 ValueTask<object?>> operation,
  175:             CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
  176:         {
  177:             ThrowIfDisposed();
  178:             ThrowIfNotInitialized();
  179: 
  180:             if (operation == null)
  181:             {
  182:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  183:                     nameof(operation));
  184:             }
  185: 
  186:             var pulse =
  187:                 new Pulse(
  188:                     request);
  189: 
  190:             var work =
  191:                 new WorkUnit(
  192:                     pulse.Id,
  193:                     request);
  194: 
  195:             var coordinator =
  196:                 new PulseCoordinator(
  197:                     pulse);
  198: 
  199:             BeginPulse(
  200:                 pulse,
  201:                 work,
  202:                 coordinator);
  203: 
  204:             object? result;
  205: 
  206:             try
  207:             {
  208:                 cancellationToken
  209:                     .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  210: 
  211:                 result =
  212:                     await operation(
  213:                             request,
  214:                             cancellationToken)
  215:                         .ConfigureAwait(false);
  216: 
  217:                 cancellationToken
  218:                     .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
  219: 
  220:                 if (!work.TryComplete(
  221:                         result))
  222:                 {
  223:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  224:                         "The WorkUnit could not complete.");
  225:                 }
  226:             }
  227:             catch (OperationCanceledException)
  228:             {
  229:                 if (!work.IsTerminal &&
  230:                     !work.TryCancel())
  231:                 {
  232:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  233:                         "The WorkUnit could not be cancelled.");
  234:                 }
  235: 
  236:                 var cancelledMoment =
  237:                     FinalizePulse(
  238:                         coordinator,
  239:                         null);
  240: 
  241:                 RecordMoment(
  242:                     cancelledMoment);
  243: 
  244:                 return cancelledMoment;
  245:             }
  246:             catch (Exception exception)
  247:             {
  248:                 if (!work.IsTerminal &&
  249:                     !work.TryFail(
  250:                         exception))
  251:                 {
  252:                     throw new InvalidOperationException(
  253:                         "The WorkUnit could not record failure.",
  254:                         exception);
  255:                 }
  256: 
  257:                 var failedMoment =
  258:                     FinalizePulse(
  259:                         coordinator,
  260:                         null);
  261: 
  262:                 RecordMoment(
  263:                     failedMoment);
  264: 
  265:                 return failedMoment;
  266:             }
  267: 
  268:             var completedMoment =
  269:                 FinalizePulse(
  270:                     coordinator,
  271:                     result);
  272: 
  273:             RecordMoment(
  274:                 completedMoment);
  275: 
  276:             return completedMoment;
  277:         }
  278: 
  279:         /// <summary>
  280:         /// Preserves the existing certified instruction
  281:         /// processor lease boundary.
  282:         /// </summary>
  283:         public async ValueTask<TResult>
  284:             ExecuteInstructionAsync<TResult>(
  285:                 Func<
  286:                     IInstructionsProcessor,
  287:                     CancellationToken,
  288:                     ValueTask<TResult>> operation,
  289:                 CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
  290:         {
  291:             ThrowIfDisposed();
  292:             ThrowIfNotInitialized();
  293: 
  294:             if (operation == null)
  295:             {
  296:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  297:                     nameof(operation));
  298:             }
  299: 
  300:             using (var lease =
  301:                 await _instructionsProcessors
  302:                     .RentAsync(
  303:                         cancellationToken)
  304:                     .ConfigureAwait(false))
  305:             {
  306:                 return await operation(
  307:                         lease.Instance,
  308:                         cancellationToken)
  309:                     .ConfigureAwait(false);
  310:             }
  311:         }
  312: 
  313:         public bool Shutdown()
  314:         {
  315:             ThrowIfDisposed();
  316: 
  317:             if (!IsInitialized)
  318:                 return false;
  319: 
  320:             _tasksRegistry.Shutdown();
  321:             _instructionRegistry.Shutdown();
  322: 
  323:             IsInitialized = false;
  324: 
  325:             return true;
  326:         }
  327: 
  328:         public void Dispose()
  329:         {
  330:             if (Interlocked.Exchange(
  331:                     ref _disposed,
  332:                     1) != 0)
  333:             {
  334:                 return;
  335:             }
  336: 
  337:             if (IsInitialized)
  338:             {
  339:                 _tasksRegistry.Shutdown();
  340:                 _instructionRegistry.Shutdown();
  341: 
  342:                 IsInitialized = false;
  343:             }
  344: 
  345:             _instructionsProcessors.Dispose();
  346:             _taskProcessors.Dispose();
  347:         }
  348: 
  349:         private static void BeginPulse(
  350:             Pulse pulse,
  351:             WorkUnit work,
  352:             IPulseCoordinator coordinator)
  353:         {
  354:             if (!pulse.TryBeginDecomposition())
  355:             {
  356:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  357:                     "The Pulse could not begin decomposition.");
  358:             }
  359: 
  360:             if (!coordinator.AddWorkUnit(
  361:                     work))
  362:             {
  363:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  364:                     "The WorkUnit could not be correlated " +
  365:                     "with its Pulse.");
  366:             }
  367: 
  368:             if (!work.TryMarkPending())
  369:             {
  370:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  371:                     "The WorkUnit could not become pending.");
  372:             }
  373: 
  374:             if (!pulse.TryMarkPending())
  375:             {
  376:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  377:                     "The Pulse could not become pending.");
  378:             }
  379: 
  380:             if (!pulse.TryMarkReady())
  381:             {
  382:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  383:                     "The Pulse could not become ready.");
  384:             }
  385: 
  386:             if (!pulse.TryBeginProcessing())
  387:             {
  388:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  389:                     "The Pulse could not begin processing.");
  390:             }
  391: 
  392:             if (!work.TryBeginProcessing())
  393:             {
  394:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  395:                     "The WorkUnit could not begin processing.");
  396:             }
  397:         }
  398: 
  399:         private static IMoment FinalizePulse(
  400:             IPulseCoordinator coordinator,
  401:             object? result)
  402:         {
  403:             if (!coordinator.CanFinalize)
  404:             {
  405:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  406:                     "The Pulse cannot finalize while required " +
  407:                     "WorkUnits remain unresolved.");
  408:             }
  409: 
  410:             if (!coordinator.TryFinalize(
  411:                     result,
  412:                     out var moment))
  413:             {
  414:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  415:                     "The Pulse coordinator could not create " +
  416:                     "a terminal Moment.");
  417:             }
  418: 
  419:             return moment ??
  420:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  421:                     "Pulse finalization returned no Moment.");
  422:         }
  423: 
  424:         private void RecordMoment(
  425:             IMoment moment)
  426:         {
  427:             if (!_timeline.AddMoment(
  428:                     moment))
  429:             {
  430:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  431:                     "The terminal Moment could not be " +
  432:                     "recorded in the engine Timeline.");
  433:             }
  434:         }
  435: 
  436:         private void ThrowIfNotInitialized()
  437:         {
  438:             if (!IsInitialized)
  439:             {
  440:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
  441:                     "The WildsEngine must be initialized " +
  442:                     "before processing requests.");
  443:             }
  444:         }
  445: 
  446:         private void ThrowIfDisposed()
  447:         {
  448:             if (Volatile.Read(
  449:                     ref _disposed) != 0)
  450:             {
  451:                 throw new ObjectDisposedException(
  452:                     nameof(WildsEngine));
  453:             }
  454:         }
  455:     }
  456: }

    1: using System;
    2: 
    3: namespace WildsEngine.Core
    4: {
    5:     /// <summary>
    6:     /// Configures processor retention and concurrency
    7:     /// for one WildsEngine instance.
    8:     ///
    9:     /// Temporal units are semantic request and history
   10:     /// boundaries. They are intentionally not configured
   11:     /// as frame, duration, or scheduling budgets here.
   12:     /// </summary>
   13:     public sealed class WildsEngineOptions
   14:     {
   15:         public string Layer { get; set; } =
   16:             "runtime";
   17: 
   18:         public int RetainedInstructionsProcessors { get; set; } =
   19:             8;
   20: 
   21:         public int ConcurrentInstructionsProcessors { get; set; } =
   22:             Math.Max(
   23:                 1,
   24:                 Environment.ProcessorCount);
   25: 
   26:         public int RetainedTaskProcessors { get; set; } =
   27:             16;
   28: 
   29:         public int ConcurrentTaskProcessors { get; set; } =
   30:             Math.Max(
   31:                 1,
   32:                 Environment.ProcessorCount);
   33:     }
   34: }
