FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\AverageNeighborDistance.cs
############################################################
    1: using System.Collections.Generic;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    5: {
    6:     public sealed class AverageNeighborDistance : NeighborOperatorBase<double?>, INeighborScalarOperator<double?>
    7:     {
    8:         protected override double? ApplyCore(IBaseObject subject, IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
    9:         {
   10:             if (relationships.Count == 0) return null;
   11:             var sum = 0d;
   12:             for (var i = 0; i < relationships.Count; i++) sum += relationships[i].Distance;
   13:             return sum / relationships.Count;
   14:         }
   15:     }
   16: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborComparerBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Supplies validation and deterministic tie handling for
    8:     /// Neighbor relationship comparers.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public abstract class NeighborComparerBase :
   11:         INeighborComparer
   12:     {
   13:         public int Compare(
   14:             IBaseObject subject,
   15:             INeighborRelationship left,
   16:             INeighborRelationship right)
   17:         {
   18:             if (subject == null)
   19:             {
   20:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   21:                     nameof(subject));
   22:             }
   23: 
   24:             if (left == null)
   25:             {
   26:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   27:                     nameof(left));
   28:             }
   29: 
   30:             if (right == null)
   31:             {
   32:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   33:                     nameof(right));
   34:             }
   35: 
   36:             var compared =
   37:                 CompareCore(
   38:                     subject,
   39:                     left,
   40:                     right);
   41: 
   42:             if (compared != 0)
   43:             {
   44:                 return compared;
   45:             }
   46: 
   47:             compared = string.Compare(
   48:                 left.Membership,
   49:                 right.Membership,
   50:                 StringComparison.Ordinal);
   51: 
   52:             if (compared != 0)
   53:             {
   54:                 return compared;
   55:             }
   56: 
   57:             return left.Neighbor.Id.CompareTo(
   58:                 right.Neighbor.Id);
   59:         }
   60: 
   61:         protected abstract int CompareCore(
   62:             IBaseObject subject,
   63:             INeighborRelationship left,
   64:             INeighborRelationship right);
   65:     }
   66: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborCompositeOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Applies a deterministic sequence of Neighbor sequence
    9:     /// operators. Each stage receives the prior stage snapshot.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public abstract class NeighborCompositeOperatorBase :
   12:         NeighborSequenceOperatorBase
   13:     {
   14:         private readonly IReadOnlyList<INeighborSequenceOperator>
   15:             _operators;
   16: 
   17:         protected NeighborCompositeOperatorBase(
   18:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborSequenceOperator> operators)
   19:         {
   20:             if (operators == null)
   21:             {
   22:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   23:                     nameof(operators));
   24:             }
   25: 
   26:             var snapshot =
   27:                 new INeighborSequenceOperator[operators.Count];
   28: 
   29:             for (var index = 0;
   30:                  index < operators.Count;
   31:                  index++)
   32:             {
   33:                 snapshot[index] =
   34:                     operators[index] ??
   35:                     throw new ArgumentException(
   36:                         "Neighbor operator pipelines cannot contain null stages.",
   37:                         nameof(operators));
   38:             }
   39: 
   40:             _operators = snapshot;
   41:         }
   42: 
   43:         protected sealed override
   44:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> ApplyCore(
   45:                 IBaseObject subject,
   46:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   47:         {
   48:             var current =
   49:                 relationships;
   50: 
   51:             for (var index = 0;
   52:                  index < _operators.Count;
   53:                  index++)
   54:             {
   55:                 current =
   56:                     _operators[index].Apply(
   57:                         subject,
   58:                         current);
   59:             }
   60: 
   61:             return current;
   62:         }
   63:     }
   64: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborFilterOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Filters a relationship snapshot through one reusable
    9:     /// predicate while preserving source order.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public abstract class NeighborFilterOperatorBase :
   12:         NeighborSequenceOperatorBase
   13:     {
   14:         private readonly INeighborPredicate _predicate;
   15: 
   16:         protected NeighborFilterOperatorBase(
   17:             INeighborPredicate predicate)
   18:         {
   19:             _predicate =
   20:                 predicate ??
   21:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   22:                     nameof(predicate));
   23:         }
   24: 
   25:         protected sealed override
   26:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> ApplyCore(
   27:                 IBaseObject subject,
   28:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   29:         {
   30:             var result =
   31:                 new List<INeighborRelationship>();
   32: 
   33:             for (var index = 0;
   34:                  index < relationships.Count;
   35:                  index++)
   36:             {
   37:                 var relationship =
   38:                     relationships[index];
   39: 
   40:                 if (_predicate.Includes(
   41:                         subject,
   42:                         relationship))
   43:                 {
   44:                     result.Add(
   45:                         relationship);
   46:                 }
   47:             }
   48: 
   49:             return Snapshot(
   50:                 result);
   51:         }
   52:     }
   53: }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborGroupingOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System.Collections.Generic;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Base class for operators that group relationships under
    8:     /// deterministic string keys.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public abstract class NeighborGroupingOperatorBase :
   11:         NeighborOperatorBase<
   12:             IReadOnlyDictionary<
   13:                 string,
   14:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>>>,
   15:         INeighborGroupingOperator
   16:     {
   17:         protected static IReadOnlyDictionary<
   18:             string,
   19:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>> Freeze(
   20:                 Dictionary<
   21:                     string,
   22:                     List<INeighborRelationship>> groups)
   23:         {
   24:             var frozen =
   25:                 new Dictionary<
   26:                     string,
   27:                     IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>>(
   28:                         groups.Count,
   29:                         System.StringComparer.Ordinal);
   30: 
   31:             foreach (var pair in groups)
   32:             {
   33:                 frozen.Add(
   34:                     pair.Key,
   35:                     pair.Value.ToArray());
   36:             }
   37: 
   38:             return frozen;
   39:         }
   40:     }
   41: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Supplies argument validation and a stable execution
    9:     /// boundary for Neighbor operators.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public abstract class NeighborOperatorBase<TResult> :
   12:         INeighborOperator<TResult>
   13:     {
   14:         public TResult Apply(
   15:             IBaseObject subject,
   16:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   17:         {
   18:             if (subject == null)
   19:             {
   20:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   21:                     nameof(subject));
   22:             }
   23: 
   24:             if (relationships == null)
   25:             {
   26:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   27:                     nameof(relationships));
   28:             }
   29: 
   30:             return ApplyCore(
   31:                 subject,
   32:                 relationships);
   33:         }
   34: 
   35:         protected abstract TResult ApplyCore(
   36:             IBaseObject subject,
   37:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships);
   38:     }
   39: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborOrderOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Orders a copied relationship snapshot through a reusable
    9:     /// comparer and never mutates the source sequence.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public abstract class NeighborOrderOperatorBase :
   12:         NeighborSequenceOperatorBase
   13:     {
   14:         private readonly INeighborComparer _comparer;
   15: 
   16:         protected NeighborOrderOperatorBase(
   17:             INeighborComparer comparer)
   18:         {
   19:             _comparer =
   20:                 comparer ??
   21:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   22:                     nameof(comparer));
   23:         }
   24: 
   25:         protected sealed override
   26:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> ApplyCore(
   27:                 IBaseObject subject,
   28:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   29:         {
   30:             var result =
   31:                 new List<INeighborRelationship>(
   32:                     relationships.Count);
   33: 
   34:             for (var index = 0;
   35:                  index < relationships.Count;
   36:                  index++)
   37:             {
   38:                 result.Add(
   39:                     relationships[index]);
   40:             }
   41: 
   42:             result.Sort(
   43:                 (left, right) =>
   44:                     _comparer.Compare(
   45:                         subject,
   46:                         left,
   47:                         right));
   48: 
   49:             return Snapshot(
   50:                 result);
   51:         }
   52:     }
   53: }

   15:         {
   16:             if (subject == null)
   17:             {
   18:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   19:                     nameof(subject));
   20:             }
   21: 
   22:             if (relationship == null)
   23:             {
   24:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   25:                     nameof(relationship));
   26:             }
   27: 
   28:             return IncludesCore(
   29:                 subject,
   30:                 relationship);
   31:         }
   32: 
   33:         protected abstract bool IncludesCore(

   37: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborSamplerBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Base class for bounded, snapshot-safe Neighbor sampling.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public abstract class NeighborSamplerBase :
   11:         NeighborSequenceOperatorBase,
   12:         INeighborSampler
   13:     {
   14:         protected NeighborSamplerBase(
   15:             int count)
   16:         {
   17:             if (count < 0)
   18:             {
   19:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   20:                     nameof(count),
   21:                     count,
   22:                     "Neighbor sample count cannot be negative.");
   23:             }
   24: 
   25:             Count = count;
   26:         }
   27: 
   28:         public int Count { get; }
   29: 
   30:         protected sealed override
   31:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> ApplyCore(
   32:                 IBaseObject subject,
   33:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   34:         {
   35:             if (Count == 0 ||
   36:                 relationships.Count == 0)
   37:             {
   38:                 return Empty();
   39:             }
   40: 
   41:             var boundedCount =
   42:                 Math.Min(
   43:                     Count,
   44:                     relationships.Count);
   45: 
   46:             return SampleCore(
   47:                 subject,
   48:                 relationships,
   49:                 boundedCount);
   50:         }
   51: 
   52:         protected abstract IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>
   53:             SampleCore(
   54:                 IBaseObject subject,
   55:                 IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships,
   56:                 int count);
   57:     }
   58: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\Base\NeighborSequenceOperatorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System.Collections.Generic;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Base class for operators that return relationship
    8:     /// sequences without mutating the source snapshot.
    9:     /// </summary>
   10:     public abstract class NeighborSequenceOperatorBase :
   11:         NeighborOperatorBase<
   12:             IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>>,
   13:         INeighborSequenceOperator
   14:     {
   15:         protected static IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>
   16:             Empty()
   17:         {
   18:             return new INeighborRelationship[0];
   19:         }
   20: 
   21:         protected static IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>
   22:             Snapshot(
   23:                 List<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   24:         {
   25:             return relationships.ToArray();
   26:         }
   27:     }
   28: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\DeterministicNeighborSample.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     public sealed class DeterministicNeighborSample : NeighborSamplerBase
    8:     {
    9:         public DeterministicNeighborSample(int count, int seed) : base(count) => Seed = seed;
   10:         public int Seed { get; }
   11: 
   12:         protected override IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> SampleCore(IBaseObject subject, IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships, int count)
   13:         {
   14:             var copy = new List<INeighborRelationship>(relationships.Count);
   15:             for (var i = 0; i < relationships.Count; i++) copy.Add(relationships[i]);
   16:             var random = new Random(Seed);
   17:             for (var i = copy.Count - 1; i > 0; i--)
   18:             {
   19:                 var j = random.Next(i + 1);
   20:                 var temp = copy[i]; copy[i] = copy[j]; copy[j] = temp;
   21:             }
   22:             if (copy.Count > count) copy.RemoveRange(count, copy.Count - count);
   23:             return copy.ToArray();
   24:         }
   25:     }
   26: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\DistanceRangeNeighborPredicate.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Topology;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     public sealed class DistanceRangeNeighborPredicate : NeighborPredicateBase
    8:     {
    9:         public DistanceRangeNeighborPredicate(double minimumDistance, double maximumDistance)
   10:         {
   11:             MinimumDistance = NeighborDistance.Validate(minimumDistance, nameof(minimumDistance));
   12:             MaximumDistance = NeighborDistance.Validate(maximumDistance, nameof(maximumDistance));
   13:             if (MaximumDistance < MinimumDistance)
   14:                 throw new ArgumentException("Maximum distance cannot be less than minimum distance.", nameof(maximumDistance));
   15:         }
   16: 
   17:         public double MinimumDistance { get; }
   18:         public double MaximumDistance { get; }
   19: 
   20:         protected override bool IncludesCore(IBaseObject subject, INeighborRelationship relationship) =>
   21:             relationship.Distance >= MinimumDistance && relationship.Distance <= MaximumDistance;
   22:     }
   23: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\FarthestNeighbor.cs
############################################################
    1: using System.Collections.Generic;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    5: {
    6:     public sealed class FarthestNeighbor : NeighborOperatorBase<INeighborRelationship?>, INeighborScalarOperator<INeighborRelationship?>
    7:     {
    8:         protected override INeighborRelationship? ApplyCore(IBaseObject subject, IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
    9:         {
   10:             INeighborRelationship? best = null;
   11:             var bestDistance = double.MinValue;
   12:             for (var i = 0; i < relationships.Count; i++)
   13:             {
   14:                 var r = relationships[i];
   15:                 if (best == null || r.Distance > bestDistance ||
   16:                     (r.Distance == bestDistance && r.Neighbor.Id.CompareTo(best.Neighbor.Id) < 0))
   17:                 {
   18:                     best = r; bestDistance = r.Distance;
   19:                 }
   20:             }
   21:             return best;
   22:         }
   23:     }
   24: }

    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3:

    5: {

    7:     {
    8:         protected override double? ApplyCore(IBaseObject subject, IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
    9:         {

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\NeighborDistanceBandGroups.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using System.Globalization;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Topology;
    6: 
    7: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    8: {
    9:     public sealed class NeighborDistanceBandGroups : NeighborGroupingOperatorBase
   10:     {
   11:         public NeighborDistanceBandGroups(double bandSize)
   12:         {
   13:             BandSize = NeighborDistance.Validate(bandSize, nameof(bandSize));
   14:             if (BandSize == 0d) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(bandSize), "Distance band size must be greater than zero.");
   15:         }
   16:         public double BandSize { get; }
   17: 
   18:         protected override IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>> ApplyCore(IBaseObject subject, IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
   19:         {
   20:             var groups = new Dictionary<string, List<INeighborRelationship>>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
   21:             for (var i = 0; i < relationships.Count; i++)
   22:             {
   23:                 var r = relationships[i];
   24:                 var band = Math.Floor(r.Distance / BandSize);
   25:                 var min = band * BandSize;
   26:                 var max = min + BandSize;
   27:                 var key = min.ToString("R", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + ".." + max.ToString("R", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
   28:                 List<INeighborRelationship> group;
   29:                 if (!groups.TryGetValue(key, out group!)) { group = new List<INeighborRelationship>(); groups.Add(key, group); }
   30:                 group.Add(r);
   31:             }
   32:             return Freeze(groups);
   33:         }
   34:     }
   35: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\NeighborDistanceDescendingComparer.cs
############################################################

    6:     {
    7:         protected override int CompareCore(IBaseObject subject, INeighborRelationship left, INeighborRelationship right) =>
    8:             right.Distance.CompareTo(left.Distance);
    9:     }
   10: }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\NeighborMembershipGroups.cs
############################################################
    1: using System.Collections.Generic;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    5: {
    6:     public sealed class NeighborMembershipGroups : NeighborGroupingOperatorBase
    7:     {
    8:         protected override IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship>> ApplyCore(IBaseObject subject, IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships)
    9:         {
   10:             var groups = new Dictionary<string, List<INeighborRelationship>>(System.StringComparer.Ordinal);
   11:             for (var i = 0; i < relationships.Count; i++)
   12:             {
   13:                 var r = relationships[i];
   14:                 List<INeighborRelationship> group;
   15:                 if (!groups.TryGetValue(r.Membership, out group!))
   16:                 {
   17:                     group = new List<INeighborRelationship>();
   18:                     groups.Add(r.Membership, group);
   19:                 }
   20:                 group.Add(r);
   21:             }
   22:             return Freeze(groups);
   23:         }
   24:     }
   25: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Operators\RandomNeighborSample.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Operators
    6: {
    7:     public sealed class RandomNeighborSample : NeighborSamplerBase
    8:     {
    9:         public RandomNeighborSample(int count) : base(count) { }
   10: 
   11:         protected override IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> SampleCore(IBaseObject subject, IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> relationships, int count)
   12:         {
   13:             var copy = new List<INeighborRelationship>(relationships.Count);
   14:             for (var i = 0; i < relationships.Count; i++) copy.Add(relationships[i]);
   15:             var random = new Random();
   16:             for (var i = copy.Count - 1; i > 0; i--)
   17:             {
   18:                 var j = random.Next(i + 1);
   19:                 var temp = copy[i]; copy[i] = copy[j]; copy[j] = temp;
   20:             }
   21:             if (copy.Count > count) copy.RemoveRange(count, copy.Count - count);
   22:             return copy.ToArray();
   23:         }
   24:     }
   25: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Neighbors\Propagation\ExponentialDistancePropagation.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions.Neighbors.Propagation;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Neighbors.Propagation
    6: {
    7:     public sealed class ExponentialDistancePropagation :
    8:         INeighborPropagation
    9:     {
   10:         private readonly double _decay;
   11: 
   12:         public ExponentialDistancePropagation(
   13:             double decay)
   14:         {
   15:             if (decay < 0.0)
   16:             {
   17:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   18:                     nameof(decay));
   19:             }
   20: 
   21:             _decay =
   22:                 decay;
   23:         }
   24: 
   25:         public double Propagate(
   26:             double intensity,
   27:             INeighborRelationship relationship)
   28:         {
   29:             if (relationship == null)
   30:             {
   31:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   32:                     nameof(relationship));
   33:             }
   34: 
   35:             if (intensity < 0.0)
   36:             {
   37:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   38:                     nameof(intensity));
   39:             }
   40: 
   41:             return intensity *
   42:                    Math.Exp(
   43:                        -_decay *
   44:                        relationship.Distance);
   45:         }
   46:     }
   47: }

   34:                    MinimumIntensity;

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Objects\BaseObject.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Objects
    6: {
    7:     public abstract class BaseObject :
    8:         IBaseObject
    9:     {
   10:         private readonly NeighborCollection _neighbors;
   11: 
   12:         protected BaseObject(
   13:             string layer)
   14:             : this(
   15:                 Guid.NewGuid(),
   16:                 layer)
   17:         {
   18:         }
   19: 
   20:         protected BaseObject(
   21:             Guid id,
   22:             string layer)
   23:         {
   24:             if (id == Guid.Empty)
   25:             {
   26:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   27:                     "Wilds objects require a non-empty GUID.",
   28:                     nameof(id));
   29:             }
   30: 
   31:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(layer))
   32:             {
   33:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   34:                     "Wilds objects require a layer.",
   35:                     nameof(layer));
   36:             }
   37: 
   38:             Id = id;
   39: 
   40:             Slug =
   41:                 BuildSlug(
   42:                     id,
   43:                     layer);
   44: 
   45:             _neighbors =
   46:                 new NeighborCollection(
   47:                     this);
   48:         }
   49: 
   50:         public Guid Id { get; }
   51: 
   52:         public string Slug { get; private set; }
   53: 
   54:         public INeighborCollection Neighbors =>
   55:             _neighbors;
   56: 
   57:         public abstract IComponent Component { get; }
   58: 
   59:         // Component shortcuts.
   60:         // These intentionally do NOT belong to IBaseObject.
   61: 
   62:         public virtual IState State =>
   63:             Component.State;
   64: 
   65:         public virtual ISignal Signal =>
   66:             Component.Signal;
   67: 
   68:         public virtual IDictionary<string, object?> Metadata =>
   69:             Component.Metadata;
   70: 
   71:         public string AppendSlug(
   72:             string value)
   73:         {
   74:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
   75:             {
   76:                 return Slug;
   77:             }
   78: 
   79:             var segment =
   80:                 value
   81:                     .Trim()
   82:                     .Trim('.');
   83: 
   84:             if (segment.Length == 0)
   85:             {
   86:                 return Slug;
   87:             }
   88: 
   89:             Slug =
   90:                 $"{Slug}.{segment}";
   91: 
   92:             return Slug;
   93:         }
   94: 
   95:         public TResult ApplyNeighborOperator<TResult>(
   96:             INeighborOperator<TResult> neighborOperator)
   97:         {
   98:             if (neighborOperator == null)
   99:             {
  100:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  101:                     nameof(neighborOperator));
  102:             }
  103: 
  104:             return neighborOperator.Apply(
  105:                 this,
  106:                 _neighbors.Relationships);
  107:         }
  108: 
  109:         private static string BuildSlug(
  110:             Guid id,
  111:             string layer)
  112:         {
  113:             var raw =
  114:                 id.ToString(
  115:                     "N");
  116: 
  117:             var first =
  118:                 raw.Substring(
  119:                     0,
  120:                     3);
  121: 
  122:             var last =
  123:                 raw.Substring(
  124:                     raw.Length - 3,
  125:                     3);
  126: 
  127:             return
  128:                 $"{first}{last}-{layer.Trim()}";
  129:         }
  130:     }
  131: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Objects\NeighborCollection.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Topology;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Objects
    7: {
    8:     /// <summary>
    9:     /// Owns the directional Neighbor relationships belonging
   10:     /// to one Wilds object.
   11:     ///
   12:     /// Relationship identity is defined by:
   13:     ///
   14:     ///     Neighbor.Id + Membership
   15:     ///
   16:     /// Distance describes relationship state and may therefore
   17:     /// change without changing relationship identity.
   18:     /// </summary>
   19:     public sealed class NeighborCollection :
   20:         INeighborCollection
   21:     {
   22:         private readonly IBaseObject _owner;
   23: 
   24:         private readonly object _sync =
   25:             new object();
   26: 
   27:         private readonly List<INeighborRelationship> _relationships =
   28:             new List<INeighborRelationship>();
   29: 
   30:         public NeighborCollection(
   31:             IBaseObject owner)
   32:         {
   33:             _owner =
   34:                 owner ??
   35:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   36:                     nameof(owner));
   37:         }
   38: 
   39:         public int Count
   40:         {
   41:             get
   42:             {
   43:                 lock (_sync)
   44:                 {
   45:                     return _relationships.Count;
   46:                 }
   47:             }
   48:         }
   49: 
   50:         public IReadOnlyList<INeighborRelationship> Relationships
   51:         {
   52:             get
   53:             {
   54:                 lock (_sync)
   55:                 {
   56:                     return _relationships.ToArray();
   57:                 }
   58:             }
   59:         }
   60: 
   61:         public bool Add(
   62:             IBaseObject neighbor,
   63:             string membership,
   64:             double distance)
   65:         {
   66:             if (neighbor == null)
   67:             {
   68:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   69:                     nameof(neighbor));
   70:             }
   71: 
   72:             var normalizedMembership =
   73:                 ValidateMembership(
   74:                     membership);
   75: 
   76:             NeighborDistance.Validate(
   77:                 distance,
   78:                 nameof(distance));
   79: 
   80:             if (neighbor.Id == _owner.Id)
   81:             {
   82:                 return false;
   83:             }
   84: 
   85:             lock (_sync)
   86:             {
   87:                 if (FindIndex(
   88:                         neighbor.Id,
   89:                         normalizedMembership) >= 0)
   90:                 {
   91:                     return false;
   92:                 }
   93: 
   94:                 _relationships.Add(
   95:                     new NeighborRelationship(
   96:                         neighbor,
   97:                         normalizedMembership,
   98:                         distance));
   99: 
  100:                 return true;
  101:             }
  102:         }
  103: 
  104:         public bool Contains(
  105:             Guid neighborId,
  106:             string membership)
  107:         {
  108:             if (neighborId == Guid.Empty)
  109:             {
  110:                 return false;
  111:             }
  112: 
  113:             var normalizedMembership =
  114:                 ValidateMembership(
  115:                     membership);
  116: 
  117:             lock (_sync)
  118:             {
  119:                 return FindIndex(
  120:                            neighborId,
  121:                            normalizedMembership) >= 0;
  122:             }
  123:         }
  124: 
  125:         public bool TryGet(
  126:             Guid neighborId,
  127:             string membership,
  128:             out INeighborRelationship? relationship)
  129:         {
  130:             relationship = null;
  131: 
  132:             if (neighborId == Guid.Empty)
  133:             {
  134:                 return false;
  135:             }
  136: 
  137:             var normalizedMembership =
  138:                 ValidateMembership(
  139:                     membership);
  140: 
  141:             lock (_sync)
  142:             {
  143:                 var index =
  144:                     FindIndex(
  145:                         neighborId,
  146:                         normalizedMembership);
  147: 
  148:                 if (index < 0)
  149:                 {
  150:                     return false;
  151:                 }
  152: 
  153:                 relationship =
  154:                     _relationships[index];
  155: 
  156:                 return true;
  157:             }
  158:         }
  159: 
  160:         public bool SetDistance(
  161:             Guid neighborId,
  162:             string membership,
  163:             double distance)
  164:         {
  165:             if (neighborId == Guid.Empty)
  166:             {
  167:                 return false;
  168:             }
  169: 
  170:             var normalizedMembership =
  171:                 ValidateMembership(
  172:                     membership);
  173: 
  174:             NeighborDistance.Validate(
  175:                 distance,
  176:                 nameof(distance));
  177: 
  178:             lock (_sync)
  179:             {
  180:                 var index =
  181:                     FindIndex(
  182:                         neighborId,
  183:                         normalizedMembership);
  184: 
  185:                 if (index < 0)
  186:                 {
  187:                     return false;
  188:                 }
  189: 
  190:                 var existing =
  191:                     _relationships[index];
  192: 
  193:                 if (existing.Distance == distance)
  194:                 {
  195:                     return false;
  196:                 }
  197: 
  198:                 _relationships[index] =
  199:                     new NeighborRelationship(
  200:                         existing.Neighbor,
  201:                         existing.Membership,
  202:                         distance);
  203: 
  204:                 return true;
  205:             }
  206:         }
  207: 
  208:         public bool UpdateDistance(
  209:             Guid neighborId,
  210:             string membership,
  211:             Func<double, double> update)
  212:         {
  213:             if (neighborId == Guid.Empty)
  214:             {
  215:                 return false;
  216:             }
  217: 
  218:             if (update == null)
  219:             {
  220:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  221:                     nameof(update));
  222:             }
  223: 
  224:             var normalizedMembership =
  225:                 ValidateMembership(
  226:                     membership);
  227: 
  228:             lock (_sync)
  229:             {
  230:                 var index =
  231:                     FindIndex(
  232:                         neighborId,
  233:                         normalizedMembership);
  234: 
  235:                 if (index < 0)
  236:                 {
  237:                     return false;
  238:                 }
  239: 
  240:                 var existing =
  241:                     _relationships[index];
  242: 
  243:                 var updatedDistance =
  244:                     update(
  245:                         existing.Distance);
  246: 
  247:                 NeighborDistance.Validate(
  248:                     updatedDistance,
  249:                     nameof(updatedDistance));
  250: 
  251:                 if (existing.Distance ==
  252:                     updatedDistance)
  253:                 {
  254:                     return false;
  255:                 }
  256: 
  257:                 _relationships[index] =
  258:                     new NeighborRelationship(
  259:                         existing.Neighbor,
  260:                         existing.Membership,
  261:                         updatedDistance);
  262: 
  263:                 return true;
  264:             }
  265:         }
  266: 
  267:         public bool Remove(
  268:             Guid neighborId,
  269:             string membership)
  270:         {
  271:             if (neighborId == Guid.Empty)
  272:             {
  273:                 return false;
  274:             }
  275: 
  276:             var normalizedMembership =
  277:                 ValidateMembership(
  278:                     membership);
  279: 
  280:             lock (_sync)
  281:             {
  282:                 var index =
  283:                     FindIndex(
  284:                         neighborId,
  285:                         normalizedMembership);
  286: 
  287:                 if (index < 0)
  288:                 {
  289:                     return false;
  290:                 }
  291: 
  292:                 _relationships.RemoveAt(
  293:                     index);
  294: 
  295:                 return true;
  296:             }
  297:         }
  298: 
  299:         public int Remove(
  300:             Guid neighborId)
  301:         {
  302:             if (neighborId == Guid.Empty)
  303:             {
  304:                 return 0;
  305:             }
  306: 
  307:             lock (_sync)
  308:             {
  309:                 var removed = 0;
  310: 
  311:                 for (var index =
  312:                          _relationships.Count - 1;
  313:                      index >= 0;
  314:                      index--)
  315:                 {
  316:                     if (_relationships[index]
  317:                             .Neighbor
  318:                             .Id != neighborId)
  319:                     {
  320:                         continue;
  321:                     }
  322: 
  323:                     _relationships.RemoveAt(
  324:                         index);
  325: 
  326:                     removed++;
  327:                 }
  328: 
  329:                 return removed;
  330:             }
  331:         }
  332: 
  333:         public void Clear()
  334:         {
  335:             lock (_sync)
  336:             {
  337:                 _relationships.Clear();
  338:             }
  339:         }
  340: 
  341:         private int FindIndex(
  342:             Guid neighborId,
  343:             string membership)
  344:         {
  345:             for (var index = 0;
  346:                  index < _relationships.Count;
  347:                  index++)
  348:             {
  349:                 var relationship =
  350:                     _relationships[index];
  351: 
  352:                 if (relationship.Neighbor.Id !=
  353:                     neighborId)
  354:                 {
  355:                     continue;
  356:                 }
  357: 
  358:                 if (!string.Equals(
  359:                         relationship.Membership,
  360:                         membership,
  361:                         StringComparison.Ordinal))
  362:                 {
  363:                     continue;
  364:                 }
  365: 
  366:                 return index;
  367:             }
  368: 
  369:             return -1;
  370:         }
  371: 
  372:         private static string ValidateMembership(
  373:             string membership)
  374:         {
  375:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
  376:                     membership))
  377:             {
  378:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  379:                     "Neighbor relationships require a membership.",
  380:                     nameof(membership));
  381:             }
  382: 
  383:             return membership.Trim();
  384:         }
  385:     }
  386: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Objects\NeighborRelationship.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Topology;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Objects
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Immutable description of a directional Neighbor
    9:     /// relationship between Wilds objects.
   10:     ///
   11:     /// Relationship identity is defined by the owning object,
   12:     /// neighboring object, and membership. Distance describes
   13:     /// the current proximity within that relationship and is
   14:     /// not itself relationship identity.
   15:     /// </summary>
   16:     public sealed class NeighborRelationship :
   17:         INeighborRelationship
   18:     {
   19:         public NeighborRelationship(
   20:             IBaseObject neighbor,
   21:             string membership,
   22:             double distance)
   23:         {
   24:             Neighbor =
   25:                 neighbor ??
   26:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   27:                     nameof(neighbor));
   28: 
   29:             Membership =
   30:                 ValidateMembership(
   31:                     membership);
   32: 
   33:             Distance =
   34:                 NeighborDistance.Validate(
   35:                     distance,
   36:                     nameof(distance));
   37:         }
   38: 
   39:         public IBaseObject Neighbor { get; }
   40: 
   41:         public string Membership { get; }
   42: 
   43:         public double Distance { get; }
   44: 
   45:         private static string ValidateMembership(
   46:             string membership)
   47:         {
   48:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
   49:                     membership))
   50:             {
   51:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   52:                     "Neighbor relationships require a membership.",
   53:                     nameof(membership));
   54:             }
   55: 
   56:             return membership.Trim();
   57:         }
   58:     }
   59: }

    1: using System;

    9:     {

   12:             string key,
   13:             object? value)
   14:             : base(
   15:                 intent,
   16:                 value)
   17:         {

   19:             {
   20:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   21:                     "Context variable key cannot be empty.",
   22:                     nameof(key));
   23:             }
   24: 
   25:             Key = key.Trim();
   26:             Value = value;
   27: 
   28:             SetArgument(
   29:                 "key",

   35:         }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Base\ProcessorBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Objects;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Processing
    6: {
    7:     public abstract class ProcessorBase :
    8:         BaseObject,
    9:         IResettablePoolObject
   10:     {
   11:         private readonly IComponent _component;
   12: 
   13:         protected ProcessorBase(
   14:             IComponent component,
   15:             string layer)
   16:             : base(layer)
   17:         {
   18:             _component = component ??
   19:                          throw new ArgumentNullException(
   20:                              nameof(component));
   21:         }
   22: 
   23:         public sealed override IComponent Component =>
   24:             _component;
   25: 
   26:         public bool TryReset()
   27:         {
   28:             return OnReset();
   29:         }
   30: 
   31:         protected virtual bool OnReset()
   32:         {
   33:             return true;
   34:         }
   35:     }
   36: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Base\RegistryBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Concurrent;
    3: using System.Collections.Generic;
    4: using System.Linq;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    6: using WildsEngine.Core.Objects;
    7: 
    8: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Processing
    9: {
   10:     public abstract class RegistryBase<T> :
   11:         BaseObject,
   12:         IRegistry<T>
   13:         where T : class
   14:     {
   15:         private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, T> _items =
   16:             new ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, T>();
   17: 
   18:         private readonly IComponent _component;
   19:         private readonly Func<T, Guid> _identity;
   20: 
   21:         protected RegistryBase(
   22:             IComponent component,
   23:             string layer,
   24:             Func<T, Guid> identity)
   25:             : base(layer)
   26:         {
   27:             _component = component ??
   28:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   29:                     nameof(component));
   30: 
   31:             _identity = identity ??
   32:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   33:                     nameof(identity));
   34:         }
   35: 
   36:         public sealed override IComponent Component =>
   37:             _component;
   38: 
   39:         public int Count =>
   40:             _items.Count;
   41: 
   42:         public IReadOnlyCollection<T> Items =>
   43:             _items.Values.ToArray();
   44: 
   45:         public bool Register(T item)
   46:         {
   47:             if (item == null)
   48:             {
   49:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   50:                     nameof(item));
   51:             }
   52: 
   53:             var id =
   54:                 _identity(item);
   55: 
   56:             if (id == Guid.Empty)
   57:                 return false;
   58: 
   59:             return _items.TryAdd(
   60:                 id,
   61:                 item);
   62:         }
   63: 
   64:         public bool Contains(Guid id)
   65:         {
   66:             if (id == Guid.Empty)
   67:                 return false;
   68: 
   69:             return _items.ContainsKey(
   70:                 id);
   71:         }
   72: 
   73:         public T? Get(Guid id)
   74:         {
   75:             if (id == Guid.Empty)
   76:                 return null;
   77: 
   78:             return _items.TryGetValue(
   79:                 id,
   80:                 out var item)
   81:                     ? item
   82:                     : null;
   83:         }
   84: 
   85:         public bool TryGet(
   86:             Guid id,
   87:             out T? item)
   88:         {
   89:             if (id == Guid.Empty)
   90:             {
   91:                 item = null;
   92: 
   93:                 return false;
   94:             }
   95: 
   96:             return _items.TryGetValue(
   97:                 id,
   98:                 out item);
   99:         }
  100: 
  101:         public bool Remove(Guid id)
  102:         {
  103:             if (id == Guid.Empty)
  104:                 return false;
  105: 
  106:             return _items.TryRemove(
  107:                 id,
  108:                 out _);
  109:         }
  110: 
  111:         public void Clear()
  112:         {
  113:             _items.Clear();
  114:         }
  115: 
  116:         public bool IsInitialized { get; private set; }
  117: 
  118:         public bool Initialize()
  119:         {
  120:             if (IsInitialized)
  121:                 return false;
  122: 
  123:             OnInitialize();
  124: 
  125:             IsInitialized = true;
  126: 
  127:             return true;
  128:         }
  129: 
  130:         public bool Shutdown()
  131:         {
  132:             if (!IsInitialized)
  133:                 return false;
  134: 
  135:             OnShutdown();
  136: 
  137:             IsInitialized = false;
  138: 
  139:             return true;
  140:         }
  141: 
  142:         protected abstract void OnInitialize();
  143: 
  144:         protected abstract void OnShutdown();
  145:     }
  146: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Base\StateInstructionBase.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Processing
    6: {
    7:     public abstract class StateInstructionBase :
    8:         IStateInstruction
    9:     {
   10:         private readonly Dictionary<string, object?> _arguments;
   11:         private readonly Dictionary<string, object?> _metadata;
   12: 
   13:         protected StateInstructionBase(
   14:             string intent,
   15:             object? payload = null,
   16:             IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, object?>>? arguments = null,
   17:             IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, object?>>? metadata = null)
   18:         {
   19:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(intent))
   20:             {
   21:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   22:                     "Instruction intent cannot be empty.",
   23:                     nameof(intent));
   24:             }
   25: 
   26:             Id = Guid.NewGuid();
   27:             Intent = intent;
   28:             Payload = payload;
   29: 
   30:             _arguments = CreateDictionary(arguments);
   31:             _metadata = CreateDictionary(metadata);
   32:         }
   33: 
   34:         public Guid Id { get; }
   35: 
   36:         public string Intent { get; }
   37: 
   38:         public object? Payload { get; }
   39: 
   40:         public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?> Arguments =>
   41:             _arguments;
   42: 
   43:         public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?> Metadata =>
   44:             _metadata;
   45: 
   46:         protected void SetArgument(
   47:             string key,
   48:             object? value)
   49:         {
   50:             ValidateKey(key, nameof(key));
   51: 
   52:             _arguments[key] = value;
   53:         }
   54: 
   55:         protected void SetMetadata(
   56:             string key,
   57:             object? value)
   58:         {
   59:             ValidateKey(key, nameof(key));
   60: 
   61:             _metadata[key] = value;
   62:         }
   63: 
   64:         protected bool RemoveArgument(string key)
   65:         {
   66:             ValidateKey(key, nameof(key));
   67: 
   68:             return _arguments.Remove(key);
   69:         }
   70: 
   71:         protected bool RemoveMetadata(string key)
   72:         {
   73:             ValidateKey(key, nameof(key));
   74: 
   75:             return _metadata.Remove(key);
   76:         }
   77: 
   78:         private static Dictionary<string, object?> CreateDictionary(
   79:             IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, object?>>? source)
   80:         {
   81:             var result =
   82:                 new Dictionary<string, object?>();
   83: 
   84:             if (source == null)
   85:             {
   86:                 return result;
   87:             }
   88: 
   89:             foreach (var pair in source)
   90:             {
   91:                 ValidateKey(pair.Key, nameof(source));
   92: 
   93:                 result[pair.Key] = pair.Value;
   94:             }
   95: 
   96:             return result;
   97:         }
   98: 
   99:         private static void ValidateKey(
  100:             string key,
  101:             string parameterName)
  102:         {
  103:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(key))
  104:             {
  105:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  106:                     "Instruction keys cannot be empty.",
  107:                     parameterName);
  108:             }
  109:         }
  110:     }
  111: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Benchmarks\ProcessorPoolingBenchmark.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Diagnostics;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Pooling;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Processor;
    6: 
    7: namespace WildsEngine.Benchmarks
    8: {
    9:     /// <summary>
   10:     /// Compares direct TaskProcessor allocation against
   11:     /// pooled TaskProcessor leasing.
   12:     /// </summary>
   13:     public sealed class ProcessorPoolingBenchmark
   14:     {
   15:         private readonly IComponent _component;
   16:         private readonly ITaskRegistry _registry;
   17: 
   18:         public ProcessorPoolingBenchmark(
   19:             IComponent component,
   20:             ITaskRegistry registry)
   21:         {
   22:             _component = component ??
   23:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   24:                     nameof(component));
   25: 
   26:             _registry = registry ??
   27:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   28:                     nameof(registry));
   29:         }
   30: 
   31:         public ProcessorPoolingBenchmarkResult Run(
   32:             int iterations,
   33:             int maximumRetained = 64,
   34:             int maximumConcurrent = 64)
   35:         {
   36:             if (iterations <= 0)
   37:             {
   38:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   39:                     nameof(iterations));
   40:             }
   41: 
   42:             if (maximumRetained < 0)
   43:             {
   44:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   45:                     nameof(maximumRetained));
   46:             }
   47: 
   48:             if (maximumConcurrent <= 0)
   49:             {
   50:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
   51:                     nameof(maximumConcurrent));
   52:             }
   53: 
   54:             WarmUp();
   55: 
   56:             var allocationMeasurementAvailable =
   57:                 IsAllocationMeasurementAvailable();
   58: 
   59:             var directAllocatedBytesBefore =
   60:                 GetAllocatedBytes();
   61: 
   62:             var directWatch =
   63:                 Stopwatch.StartNew();
   64: 
   65:             for (var i = 0; i < iterations; i++)
   66:             {
   67:                 var processor =
   68:                     new TaskProcessor(
   69:                         _component,
   70:                         "benchmark.direct",
   71:                         _registry);
   72: 
   73:                 Consume(
   74:                     processor);
   75:             }
   76: 
   77:             directWatch.Stop();
   78: 
   79:             var directAllocatedBytes =
   80:                 allocationMeasurementAvailable
   81:                     ? GetAllocatedBytes() -
   82:                       directAllocatedBytesBefore
   83:                     : 0;
   84: 
   85:             using (var pool =
   86:                 new ObjectPool<ITaskProcessor>(
   87:                     factory: () =>
   88:                         new TaskProcessor(
   89:                             _component,
   90:                             "benchmark.pooled",
   91:                             _registry),
   92:                     maximumRetained:
   93:                         maximumRetained,
   94:                     maximumConcurrent:
   95:                         maximumConcurrent))
   96:             {
   97:                 var pooledAllocatedBytesBefore =
   98:                     GetAllocatedBytes();
   99: 
  100:                 var pooledWatch =
  101:                     Stopwatch.StartNew();
  102: 
  103:                 for (var i = 0; i < iterations; i++)
  104:                 {
  105:                     using (var lease = pool.Rent())
  106:                     {
  107:                         Consume(
  108:                             lease.Instance);
  109:                     }
  110:                 }
  111: 
  112:                 pooledWatch.Stop();
  113: 
  114:                 var pooledAllocatedBytes =
  115:                     allocationMeasurementAvailable
  116:                         ? GetAllocatedBytes() -
  117:                           pooledAllocatedBytesBefore
  118:                         : 0;
  119: 
  120:                 return new ProcessorPoolingBenchmarkResult(
  121:                     iterations,
  122:                     directWatch.Elapsed,
  123:                     pooledWatch.Elapsed,
  124:                     directAllocatedBytes,
  125:                     pooledAllocatedBytes,
  126:                     pool.CreatedCount,
  127:                     allocationMeasurementAvailable);
  128:             }
  129:         }
  130: 
  131:         private void WarmUp()
  132:         {
  133:             for (var i = 0; i < 128; i++)
  134:             {
  135:                 var processor =
  136:                     new TaskProcessor(
  137:                         _component,
  138:                         "benchmark.warmup",
  139:                         _registry);
  140: 
  141:                 Consume(
  142:                     processor);
  143:             }
  144:         }
  145: 
  146:         private static bool IsAllocationMeasurementAvailable()
  147:         {
  148: #if NETSTANDARD2_1
  149:             return true;
  150: #else
  151:             return false;
  152: #endif
  153:         }
  154: 
  155:         private static long GetAllocatedBytes()
  156:         {
  157: #if NETSTANDARD2_1
  158:             return GC.GetAllocatedBytesForCurrentThread();
  159: #else
  160:             return 0;
  161: #endif
  162:         }
  163: 
  164:         private static void Consume(
  165:             ITaskProcessor processor)
  166:         {
  167:             if (processor == null)
  168:             {
  169:                 throw new InvalidOperationException();
  170:             }
  171:         }
  172:     }
  173: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Benchmarks\ProcessorPoolingBenchmarkResult.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: 
    3: namespace WildsEngine.Benchmarks
    4: {
    5:     /// <summary>
    6:     /// Contains the results of a processor pooling benchmark.
    7:     /// </summary>
    8:     public sealed class ProcessorPoolingBenchmarkResult
    9:     {
   10:         public ProcessorPoolingBenchmarkResult(
   11:             int iterations,
   12:             TimeSpan directDuration,
   13:             TimeSpan pooledDuration,
   14:             long directAllocatedBytes,
   15:             long pooledAllocatedBytes,
   16:             int pooledInstancesCreated,
   17:             bool allocationMeasurementAvailable)
   18:         {
   19:             Iterations = iterations;
   20:             DirectDuration = directDuration;
   21:             PooledDuration = pooledDuration;
   22:             DirectAllocatedBytes =
   23:                 directAllocatedBytes;
   24:             PooledAllocatedBytes =
   25:                 pooledAllocatedBytes;
   26:             PooledInstancesCreated =
   27:                 pooledInstancesCreated;
   28:             AllocationMeasurementAvailable =
   29:                 allocationMeasurementAvailable;
   30:         }
   31: 
   32:         public int Iterations { get; }
   33: 
   34:         public TimeSpan DirectDuration { get; }
   35: 
   36:         public TimeSpan PooledDuration { get; }
   37: 
   38:         public long DirectAllocatedBytes { get; }
   39: 
   40:         public long PooledAllocatedBytes { get; }
   41: 
   42:         public int PooledInstancesCreated { get; }
   43: 
   44:         public bool AllocationMeasurementAvailable { get; }
   45: 
   46:         public override string ToString()
   47:         {
   48:             var allocations =
   49:                 AllocationMeasurementAvailable
   50:                     ? $"Direct allocation: {DirectAllocatedBytes} bytes{Environment.NewLine}" +
   51:                       $"Pooled allocation: {PooledAllocatedBytes} bytes{Environment.NewLine}"
   52:                     : $"Allocation measurement: unavailable{Environment.NewLine}";
   53: 
   54:             return
   55:                 $"Iterations: {Iterations}{Environment.NewLine}" +
   56:                 $"Direct duration: {DirectDuration.TotalMilliseconds:F3} ms{Environment.NewLine}" +
   57:                 $"Pooled duration: {PooledDuration.TotalMilliseconds:F3} ms{Environment.NewLine}" +
   58:                 allocations +
   59:                 $"Pooled processors created: {PooledInstancesCreated}";
   60:         }
   61:     }
   62: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Coordinator\PulseCoordinator.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using System.Linq;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Units;
    6: 
    7: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Coordinator
    8: {
    9:     /// <summary>
   10:     /// Coordinates all required WorkUnits for one Pulse.
   11:     ///
   12:     /// The coordinator prevents early completion and records
   13:     /// successful, failed, and cancelled Pulse outcomes.
   14:     /// </summary>
   15:     public sealed class PulseCoordinator :
   16:         IPulseCoordinator
   17:     {
   18:         private readonly object _sync =
   19:             new object();
   20: 
   21:         private readonly List<IWorkUnit> _workUnits =
   22:             new List<IWorkUnit>();
   23: 
   24:         private readonly HashSet<Guid> _workUnitIds =
   25:             new HashSet<Guid>();
   26: 
   27:         public PulseCoordinator(
   28:             IPulse pulse)
   29:         {
   30:             Pulse = pulse ??
   31:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   32:                     nameof(pulse));
   33:         }
   34: 
   35:         public IPulse Pulse { get; }
   36: 
   37:         public int WorkUnitCount
   38:         {
   39:             get
   40:             {
   41:                 lock (_sync)
   42:                 {
   43:                     return _workUnits.Count;
   44:                 }
   45:             }
   46:         }
   47: 
   48:         public IReadOnlyList<IWorkUnit> WorkUnits
   49:         {
   50:             get
   51:             {
   52:                 lock (_sync)
   53:                 {
   54:                     return _workUnits.ToArray();
   55:                 }
   56:             }
   57:         }
   58: 
   59:         public bool CanResolve
   60:         {
   61:             get
   62:             {
   63:                 lock (_sync)
   64:                 {
   65:                     return CanResolveCore();
   66:                 }
   67:             }
   68:         }
   69: 
   70:         public bool CanFinalize
   71:         {
   72:             get
   73:             {
   74:                 lock (_sync)
   75:                 {
   76:                     return CanFinalizeCore();
   77:                 }
   78:             }
   79:         }
   80: 
   81:         public bool AddWorkUnit(
   82:             IWorkUnit workUnit)
   83:         {
   84:             if (workUnit == null)
   85:             {
   86:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   87:                     nameof(workUnit));
   88:             }
   89: 
   90:             if (workUnit.PulseId != Pulse.Id)
   91:             {
   92:                 return false;
   93:             }
   94: 
   95:             lock (_sync)
   96:             {
   97:                 if (Pulse.IsTerminal)
   98:                 {
   99:                     return false;
  100:                 }
  101: 
  102:                 if (!_workUnitIds.Add(
  103:                         workUnit.Id))
  104:                 {
  105:                     return false;
  106:                 }
  107: 
  108:                 _workUnits.Add(
  109:                     workUnit);
  110: 
  111:                 return true;
  112:             }
  113:         }
  114: 
  115:         public bool TryResolve(
  116:             object? result,
  117:             out IMoment? moment)
  118:         {
  119:             lock (_sync)
  120:             {
  121:                 moment = null;
  122: 
  123:                 if (!CanResolveCore())
  124:                 {
  125:                     return false;
  126:                 }
  127: 
  128:                 return TryCreateCompletedMoment(
  129:                     result,
  130:                     out moment);
  131:             }
  132:         }
  133: 
  134:         public bool TryFinalize(
  135:             object? result,
  136:             out IMoment? moment)
  137:         {
  138:             lock (_sync)
  139:             {
  140:                 moment = null;
  141: 
  142:                 if (!CanFinalizeCore())
  143:                 {
  144:                     return false;
  145:                 }
  146: 
  147:                 var failedWork =
  148:                     GetFirstFailedWorkUnit();
  149: 
  150:                 if (failedWork != null)
  151:                 {
  152:                     return TryCreateFailedMoment(
  153:                         failedWork,
  154:                         out moment);
  155:                 }
  156: 
  157:                 if (ContainsCancelledWorkUnit())
  158:                 {
  159:                     return TryCreateCancelledMoment(
  160:                         out moment);
  161:                 }
  162: 
  163:                 return TryCreateCompletedMoment(
  164:                     result,
  165:                     out moment);
  166:             }
  167:         }
  168: 
  169:         private bool TryCreateCompletedMoment(
  170:             object? result,
  171:             out IMoment? moment)
  172:         {
  173:             moment = null;
  174: 
  175:             if (!CanResolveCore())
  176:             {
  177:                 return false;
  178:             }
  179: 
  180:             if (!Pulse.TryComplete())
  181:             {
  182:                 return false;
  183:             }
  184: 
  185:             moment =
  186:                 new Moment(
  187:                     Pulse.Id,
  188:                     MomentOutcome.Completed,
  189:                     result,
  190:                     null,
  191:                     GetWorkUnitIds());
  192: 
  193:             return true;
  194:         }
  195: 
  196:         private bool TryCreateFailedMoment(
  197:             IWorkUnit failedWork,
  198:             out IMoment? moment)
  199:         {
  200:             moment = null;
  201: 
  202:             var exception =
  203:                 failedWork.Exception;
  204: 
  205:             if (exception == null)
  206:             {
  207:                 return false;
  208:             }
  209: 
  210:             if (!Pulse.TryFail())
  211:             {
  212:                 return false;
  213:             }
  214: 
  215:             moment =
  216:                 new Moment(
  217:                     Pulse.Id,
  218:                     MomentOutcome.Failed,
  219:                     null,
  220:                     exception,
  221:                     GetWorkUnitIds());
  222: 
  223:             return true;
  224:         }
  225: 
  226:         private bool TryCreateCancelledMoment(
  227:             out IMoment? moment)
  228:         {
  229:             moment = null;
  230: 
  231:             if (!Pulse.TryCancel())
  232:             {
  233:                 return false;
  234:             }
  235: 
  236:             moment =
  237:                 new Moment(
  238:                     Pulse.Id,
  239:                     MomentOutcome.Cancelled,
  240:                     null,
  241:                     null,
  242:                     GetWorkUnitIds());
  243: 
  244:             return true;
  245:         }
  246: 
  247:         private bool CanResolveCore()
  248:         {
  249:             if (Pulse.Status !=
  250:                 PulseStatus.Processing)
  251:             {
  252:                 return false;
  253:             }
  254: 
  255:             if (_workUnits.Count == 0)
  256:             {
  257:                 return false;
  258:             }
  259: 
  260:             for (
  261:                 var index = 0;
  262:                 index < _workUnits.Count;
  263:                 index++)
  264:             {
  265:                 if (_workUnits[index].Status !=
  266:                     WorkUnitStatus.Completed)
  267:                 {
  268:                     return false;
  269:                 }
  270:             }
  271: 
  272:             return true;
  273:         }
  274: 
  275:         private bool CanFinalizeCore()
  276:         {
  277:             if (Pulse.Status !=
  278:                 PulseStatus.Processing)
  279:             {
  280:                 return false;
  281:             }
  282: 
  283:             if (_workUnits.Count == 0)
  284:             {
  285:                 return false;
  286:             }
  287: 
  288:             for (
  289:                 var index = 0;
  290:                 index < _workUnits.Count;
  291:                 index++)
  292:             {
  293:                 if (!_workUnits[index].IsTerminal)
  294:                 {
  295:                     return false;
  296:                 }
  297:             }
  298: 
  299:             return true;
  300:         }
  301: 
  302:         private IWorkUnit? GetFirstFailedWorkUnit()
  303:         {
  304:             for (
  305:                 var index = 0;
  306:                 index < _workUnits.Count;
  307:                 index++)
  308:             {
  309:                 var workUnit =
  310:                     _workUnits[index];
  311: 
  312:                 if (workUnit.Status ==
  313:                     WorkUnitStatus.Failed)
  314:                 {
  315:                     return workUnit;
  316:                 }
  317:             }
  318: 
  319:             return null;
  320:         }
  321: 
  322:         private bool ContainsCancelledWorkUnit()
  323:         {
  324:             for (
  325:                 var index = 0;
  326:                 index < _workUnits.Count;
  327:                 index++)
  328:             {
  329:                 if (_workUnits[index].Status ==
  330:                     WorkUnitStatus.Cancelled)
  331:                 {
  332:                     return true;
  333:                 }
  334:             }
  335: 
  336:             return false;
  337:         }
  338: 
  339:         private Guid[] GetWorkUnitIds()
  340:         {
  341:             return _workUnits
  342:                 .Select(
  343:                     workUnit =>
  344:                         workUnit.Id)
  345:                 .ToArray();
  346:         }
  347:     }
  348: }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Instructions\AddNeighborInstruction.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Topology;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Processing
    7: {
    8:     public sealed class AddNeighborInstruction :
    9:         StateInstructionBase
   10:     {
   11:         public const string IntentName =
   12:             "topology.neighbor.add";
   13: 
   14:         public AddNeighborInstruction(
   15:             IBaseObject neighbor,
   16:             string membership,
   17:             double distance,
   18:             IDictionary<string, object?>? metadata = null)
   19:             : base(
   20:                 IntentName,
   21:                 ValidateNeighbor(
   22:                     neighbor),
   23:                 CreateArguments(
   24:                     membership,
   25:                     distance),
   26:                 metadata)
   27:         {
   28:             Neighbor =
   29:                 neighbor;
   30: 
   31:             Membership =
   32:                 ValidateMembership(
   33:                     membership);
   34: 
   35:             Distance =
   36:                 NeighborDistance.Validate(
   37:                     distance,
   38:                     nameof(distance));
   39:         }
   40: 
   41:         public IBaseObject Neighbor { get; }
   42: 
   43:         public string Membership { get; }
   44: 
   45:         public double Distance { get; }
   46: 
   47:         private static IBaseObject ValidateNeighbor(
   48:             IBaseObject neighbor)
   49:         {
   50:             return neighbor ??
   51:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   52:                     nameof(neighbor));
   53:         }
   54: 
   55:         private static IDictionary<string, object?>
   56:             CreateArguments(
   57:                 string membership,
   58:                 double distance)
   59:         {
   60:             return new Dictionary<string, object?>
   61:             {
   62:                 ["membership"] =
   63:                     ValidateMembership(
   64:                         membership),
   65: 
   66:                 ["distance"] =
   67:                     NeighborDistance.Validate(
   68:                         distance,
   69:                         nameof(distance))
   70:             };
   71:         }
   72: 
   73:         private static string ValidateMembership(
   74:             string membership)
   75:         {
   76:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
   77:                     membership))
   78:             {
   79:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   80:                     "Neighbor membership cannot be empty.",
   81:                     nameof(membership));
   82:             }
   83: 
   84:             return membership.Trim();
   85:         }
   86:     }
   87: }
