    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Topology;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Processing
    6: {
    7:     public sealed class SetNeighborDistanceInstruction : StateInstructionBase
    8:     {
    9:         public const string IntentName = "topology.neighbor.distance.set";
   10: 
   11:         public SetNeighborDistanceInstruction(
   12:             Guid neighborId,
   13:             string membership,
   14:             double distance,
   15:             IDictionary<string, object?>? metadata = null)
   16:             : base(IntentName, ValidateId(neighborId), CreateArguments(membership, distance), metadata)
   17:         {
   18:             NeighborId = neighborId;
   19:             Membership = ValidateMembership(membership);
   20:             Distance = NeighborDistance.Validate(distance, nameof(distance));
   21:         }
   22: 
   23:         public Guid NeighborId { get; }
   24:         public string Membership { get; }
   25:         public double Distance { get; }
   26: 
   27:         private static Guid ValidateId(Guid value)
   28:         {
   29:             if (value == Guid.Empty) throw new ArgumentException("Neighbor distance update requires a non-empty GUID.", nameof(value));
   30:             return value;
   31:         }
   32: 
   33:         private static string ValidateMembership(string value)
   34:         {
   35:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)) throw new ArgumentException("Neighbor membership cannot be empty.", nameof(value));
   36:             return value.Trim();
   37:         }
   38: 
   39:         private static IDictionary<string, object?> CreateArguments(string membership, double distance) =>
   40:             new Dictionary<string, object?>
   41:             {
   42:                 ["membership"] = ValidateMembership(membership),
   43:                 ["distance"] = NeighborDistance.Validate(distance, nameof(distance))
   44:             };
   45:     }
   46: }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Processor\InstructionsProcessor.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Threading;
    3: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Processor
    7: {
    8:     /// <summary>
    9:     /// Processes instructions using the instruction registry
   10:     /// and delegates task work through the task processor pool.
   11:     /// </summary>
   12:     public class InstructionsProcessor :
   13:         ProcessorBase,
   14:         IInstructionsProcessor
   15:     {
   16:         private readonly IObjectPool<ITaskProcessor> _taskProcessors;
   17: 
   18:         public InstructionsProcessor(
   19:             IComponent component,
   20:             string layer,
   21:             IInstructionsRegistry registry,
   22:             IObjectPool<ITaskProcessor> taskProcessors)
   23:             : base(
   24:                 component,
   25:                 layer)
   26:         {
   27:             Registry = registry ??
   28:                        throw new ArgumentNullException(
   29:                            nameof(registry));
   30: 
   31:             _taskProcessors = taskProcessors ??
   32:                               throw new ArgumentNullException(
   33:                                   nameof(taskProcessors));
   34:         }
   35: 
   36:         public IInstructionsRegistry Registry { get; }
   37: 
   38:         /// <summary>
   39:         /// Rents a task processor for delegated task processing.
   40:         /// The caller owns the returned lease and must dispose it.
   41:         /// </summary>
   42:         public ValueTask<ObjectLease<ITaskProcessor>>
   43:             RentTaskProcessorAsync(
   44:                 CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
   45:         {
   46:             return _taskProcessors.RentAsync(
   47:                 cancellationToken);
   48:         }
   49: 
   50:         /// <summary>
   51:         /// Clears transient processor state before this processor
   52:         /// is returned to its pool.
   53:         /// </summary>
   54:         protected override bool OnReset()
   55:         {
   56:             return true;
   57:         }
   58:     }
   59: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Processing\Processor\TaskProcessor.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Processing.Processor
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Processes task work using a task registry.
    8:     /// Provides the task lifecycle boundary used by
    9:     /// pooled task processors.
   10:     /// </summary>
   11:     public class TaskProcessor :
   12:         ProcessorBase,
   13:         ITaskProcessor
   14:     {
   15:         public TaskProcessor(
   16:             IComponent component,
   17:             string layer,
   18:             ITaskRegistry registry)
   19:             : base(
   20:                 component,
   21:                 layer)
   22:         {
   23:             Registry = registry ??
   24:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   25:                     nameof(registry));
   26:         }
   27: 
   28:         public ITaskRegistry Registry { get; }
   29: 
   30:         /// <summary>
   31:         /// Parks a task for processing.
   32:         /// </summary>
   33:         public bool RegisterTask(
   34:             ITask task)
   35:         {
   36:             if (task == null)
   37:             {
   38:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   39:                     nameof(task));
   40:             }
   41: 
   42:             return Registry.Register(
   43:                 task);
   44:         }
   45: 
   46:         /// <summary>
   47:         /// Determines whether a task is currently parked.
   48:         /// </summary>
   49:         public bool ContainsTask(
   50:             Guid id)
   51:         {
   52:             return Registry.Contains(
   53:                 id);
   54:         }
   55: 
   56:         /// <summary>
   57:         /// Retrieves a parked task by identity.
   58:         /// </summary>
   59:         public ITask? GetTask(
   60:             Guid id)
   61:         {
   62:             return Registry.Get(
   63:                 id);
   64:         }
   65: 
   66:         /// <summary>
   67:         /// Attempts to retrieve a parked task by identity.
   68:         /// </summary>
   69:         public bool TryGetTask(
   70:             Guid id,
   71:             out ITask? task)
   72:         {
   73:             return Registry.TryGet(
   74:                 id,
   75:                 out task);
   76:         }
   77: 
   78:         /// <summary>
   79:         /// Removes a parked task after processing completes.
   80:         /// </summary>
   81:         public bool RemoveTask(
   82:             Guid id)
   83:         {
   84:             return Registry.Remove(
   85:                 id);
   86:         }
   87: 
   88:         /// <summary>
   89:         /// Clears transient processor state before this
   90:         /// processor is returned to its pool.
   91:         ///
   92:         /// Registry contents intentionally survive processor
   93:         /// reuse because task lifetime is independent from
   94:         /// processor lifetime.
   95:         /// </summary>
   96:         protected override bool OnReset()
   97:         {
   98:             return true;
   99:         }
  100:     }
  101: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\States\WildsState.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Objects;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Processing;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Core.States
    7: {
    8:     public abstract class WildsState :
    9:         BaseObject,
   10:         IState
   11:     {
   12:         private readonly IComponent _component;
   13: 
   14:         protected WildsState(
   15:             IComponent component,
   16:             IContext context,
   17:             string layer)
   18:             : base(layer)
   19:         {
   20:             _component =
   21:                 component ??
   22:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   23:                     nameof(component));
   24: 
   25:             Context =
   26:                 context ??
   27:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   28:                     nameof(context));
   29:         }
   30: 
   31:         protected WildsState(
   32:             Guid id,
   33:             IComponent component,
   34:             IContext context,
   35:             string layer)
   36:             : base(
   37:                 id,
   38:                 layer)
   39:         {
   40:             _component =
   41:                 component ??
   42:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   43:                     nameof(component));
   44: 
   45:             Context =
   46:                 context ??
   47:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   48:                     nameof(context));
   49:         }
   50: 
   51:         public sealed override IComponent Component =>
   52:             _component;
   53: 
   54:         public IContext Context { get; }
   55: 
   56:         public abstract bool Process(
   57:             IStateInstruction instruction);
   58: 
   59:         /// <summary>
   60:         /// Processes Neighbor-topology instructions that are
   61:         /// common to all Wilds states.
   62:         ///
   63:         /// Concrete states should invoke this before handling
   64:         /// state-specific instruction types.
   65:         /// </summary>
   66:         protected bool TryProcessNeighborInstruction(
   67:             IStateInstruction instruction)
   68:         {
   69:             if (instruction == null)
   70:             {
   71:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   72:                     nameof(instruction));
   73:             }
   74: 
   75:             if (instruction is
   76:                 AddNeighborInstruction addNeighbor)
   77:             {
   78:                 return Component.Neighbors.Add(
   79:                     addNeighbor.Neighbor,
   80:                     addNeighbor.Membership,
   81:                     addNeighbor.Distance);
   82:             }
   83: 
   84:             if (instruction is
   85:                 RemoveNeighborInstruction removeNeighbor)
   86:             {
   87:                 return Component.Neighbors.Remove(
   88:                     removeNeighbor.NeighborId,
   89:                     removeNeighbor.Membership);
   90:             }
   91: 
   92:             if (instruction is
   93:                 SetNeighborDistanceInstruction setDistance)
   94:             {
   95:                 return Component.Neighbors.SetDistance(
   96:                     setDistance.NeighborId,
   97:                     setDistance.Membership,
   98:                     setDistance.Distance);
   99:             }
  100: 
  101:             return false;
  102:         }
  103:     }
  104: }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Topology\NeighborClusterer.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Topology
    5: {
    6:     public sealed class NeighborClusterer : INeighborClusterer
    7:     {
    8:         public IReadOnlyList<INeighborCluster> FindClusters(IReadOnlyList<IBaseObject> objects,string membership)
    9:         {
   10:             if(objects==null)throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(objects));if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(membership))throw new ArgumentException("Membership cannot be empty.",nameof(membership));membership=membership.Trim();
   11:             var known=new Dictionary<Guid,IBaseObject>();for(var i=0;i<objects.Count;i++){var o=objects[i]??throw new ArgumentException("Objects cannot contain null.",nameof(objects));known[o.Id]=o;}var visited=new HashSet<Guid>();var clusters=new List<INeighborCluster>();
   12:             foreach(var pair in known){if(visited.Contains(pair.Key))continue;var members=new List<IBaseObject>();var queue=new Queue<IBaseObject>();queue.Enqueue(pair.Value);visited.Add(pair.Key);while(queue.Count>0){var current=queue.Dequeue();members.Add(current);foreach(var candidate in known.Values){if(visited.Contains(candidate.Id))continue;if(Connected(current,candidate,membership)){visited.Add(candidate.Id);queue.Enqueue(candidate);}}}members.Sort((a,b)=>a.Id.CompareTo(b.Id));clusters.Add(new NeighborCluster(membership,members.ToArray()));}
   13:             return clusters.ToArray();
   14:         }
   15:         private static bool Connected(IBaseObject a,IBaseObject b,string membership){return Has(a,b.Id,membership)||Has(b,a.Id,membership);}
   16:         private static bool Has(IBaseObject a,Guid id,string membership){var r=a.Neighbors.Relationships;for(var i=0;i<r.Count;i++)if(r[i].Neighbor.Id==id&&string.Equals(r[i].Membership,membership,StringComparison.Ordinal))return true;return false;}
   17:     }
   18: }

    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Topology
    5: {
    6:     public sealed class NeighborPropagation<TResult> : INeighborPropagation<TResult>
    7:     {
    8:         private readonly INeighborPropagationRule<TResult> _rule;
    9:         public NeighborPropagation(INeighborPropagationRule<TResult> rule){_rule=rule??throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(rule));}
   10:         public IReadOnlyList<TResult> Propagate(IBaseObject origin,string membership,double maximumDistance)
   11:         {
   12:             if(origin==null)throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(origin));if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(membership))throw new ArgumentException("Membership cannot be empty.",nameof(membership));membership=membership.Trim();NeighborDistance.Validate(maximumDistance,nameof(maximumDistance));
   13:             var results=new List<TResult>();var best=new Dictionary<Guid,double>();var queue=new List<Entry>();best[origin.Id]=0d;queue.Add(new Entry(origin,0d));while(queue.Count>0){var bi=0;for(var i=1;i<queue.Count;i++)if(queue[i].Distance<queue[bi].Distance)bi=i;var e=queue[bi];queue.RemoveAt(bi);var rels=e.Object.Neighbors.Relationships;for(var i=0;i<rels.Count;i++){var r=rels[i];if(!string.Equals(r.Membership,membership,StringComparison.Ordinal))continue;var next=e.Distance+r.Distance;if(next>maximumDistance)continue;double old;if(best.TryGetValue(r.Neighbor.Id,out old)&&old<=next)continue;best[r.Neighbor.Id]=next;results.Add(_rule.Apply(origin,e.Object,r,next));queue.Add(new Entry(r.Neighbor,next));}}return results.ToArray();
   14:         }
   15:         private sealed class Entry{public Entry(IBaseObject o,double d){Object=o;Distance=d;}public IBaseObject Object{get;}public double Distance{get;}}
   16:     }
   17: }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Topology\NeighborRouter.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Topology
    5: {
    6:     public sealed class NeighborRouter : INeighborRouter
    7:     {
    8:         public bool TryFindRoute(IBaseObject origin,IBaseObject target,string membership,out INeighborRoute? route)
    9:         {
   10:             if(origin==null)throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(origin));if(target==null)throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(target));if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(membership))throw new ArgumentException("Membership cannot be empty.",nameof(membership));membership=membership.Trim();route=null;
   11:             if(origin.Id==target.Id){route=new NeighborRoute(membership,new[]{origin},0d);return true;}
   12:             var dist=new Dictionary<Guid,double>();var objects=new Dictionary<Guid,IBaseObject>();var previous=new Dictionary<Guid,Guid>();var open=new List<IBaseObject>();dist[origin.Id]=0d;objects[origin.Id]=origin;open.Add(origin);
   13:             while(open.Count>0){var bi=0;for(var i=1;i<open.Count;i++){var c=dist[open[i].Id].CompareTo(dist[open[bi].Id]);if(c<0||(c==0&&open[i].Id.CompareTo(open[bi].Id)<0))bi=i;}var current=open[bi];open.RemoveAt(bi);if(current.Id==target.Id)break;var rels=current.Neighbors.Relationships;for(var i=0;i<rels.Count;i++){var r=rels[i];if(!string.Equals(r.Membership,membership,StringComparison.Ordinal))continue;var nd=dist[current.Id]+r.Distance;double old;if(dist.TryGetValue(r.Neighbor.Id,out old)&&old<=nd)continue;dist[r.Neighbor.Id]=nd;objects[r.Neighbor.Id]=r.Neighbor;previous[r.Neighbor.Id]=current.Id;if(!Contains(open,r.Neighbor.Id))open.Add(r.Neighbor);}}
   14:             double total;if(!dist.TryGetValue(target.Id,out total))return false;var reverse=new List<IBaseObject>();var id=target.Id;reverse.Add(objects[id]);while(id!=origin.Id){id=previous[id];reverse.Add(objects[id]);}reverse.Reverse();route=new NeighborRoute(membership,reverse.ToArray(),total);return true;
   15:         }
   16:         private static bool Contains(List<IBaseObject> items,Guid id){for(var i=0;i<items.Count;i++)if(items[i].Id==id)return true;return false;}
   17:     }
   18: }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Topology\NeighborTraversal.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Topology
    5: {
    6:     public sealed class NeighborTraversal : INeighborTraversal
    7:     {
    8:         public IReadOnlyList<IBaseObject> Traverse(IBaseObject origin,string membership,double maximumDistance)
    9:         {
   10:             if(origin==null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(origin)); membership=Normalize(membership); NeighborDistance.Validate(maximumDistance,nameof(maximumDistance));
   11:             var result=new List<IBaseObject>(); var best=new Dictionary<Guid,double>(); var queue=new List<Entry>(); best[origin.Id]=0d; queue.Add(new Entry(origin,0d));
   12:             while(queue.Count>0){var bestIndex=0;for(var i=1;i<queue.Count;i++)if(Compare(queue[i],queue[bestIndex])<0)bestIndex=i;var entry=queue[bestIndex];queue.RemoveAt(bestIndex);double known;if(!best.TryGetValue(entry.Object.Id,out known)||entry.Distance!=known)continue;if(entry.Object.Id!=origin.Id)result.Add(entry.Object);var rels=entry.Object.Neighbors.Relationships;for(var i=0;i<rels.Count;i++){var r=rels[i];if(!string.Equals(r.Membership,membership,StringComparison.Ordinal))continue;var next=entry.Distance+r.Distance;if(next>maximumDistance)continue;double old;if(best.TryGetValue(r.Neighbor.Id,out old)&&old<=next)continue;best[r.Neighbor.Id]=next;queue.Add(new Entry(r.Neighbor,next));}}
   13:             return result.ToArray();
   14:         }
   15:         public bool IsReachable(IBaseObject origin,IBaseObject target,string membership,double maximumDistance){if(target==null)throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(target));if(origin!=null&&origin.Id==target.Id)return true;var nodes=Traverse(origin,membership,maximumDistance);for(var i=0;i<nodes.Count;i++)if(nodes[i].Id==target.Id)return true;return false;}
   16:         private static string Normalize(string membership){if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(membership))throw new ArgumentException("Membership cannot be empty.",nameof(membership));return membership.Trim();}
   17:         private static int Compare(Entry a,Entry b){var x=a.Distance.CompareTo(b.Distance);return x!=0?x:a.Object.Id.CompareTo(b.Object.Id);}
   18:         private sealed class Entry{public Entry(IBaseObject o,double d){Object=o;Distance=d;}public IBaseObject Object{get;}public double Distance{get;}}
   19:     }
   20: }

    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Provides stable identity for Wilds temporal and
    8:     /// processing units.
    9:     ///
   10:     /// Units intentionally do not inherit from BaseObject.
   11:     /// A unit is not required to own a component, state,
   12:     /// signal, metadata collection, or neighbor graph.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public abstract class BaseUnit :
   15:         IBaseUnit
   16:     {
   17:         protected BaseUnit()
   18:             : this(
   19:                 Guid.NewGuid())
   20:         {
   21:         }
   22: 
   23:         protected BaseUnit(
   24:             Guid id)
   25:         {
   26:             if (id == Guid.Empty)
   27:             {
   28:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   29:                     "Wilds units require a non-empty GUID.",
   30:                     nameof(id));
   31:             }
   32: 
   33:             Id = id;
   34:         }
   35: 
   36:         public Guid Id { get; }
   37:     }
   38: }

    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Maintains a collection of Wilds timelines.
    9:     ///
   10:     /// An epoch is the highest currently defined Wilds
   11:     /// temporal unit.
   12:     /// </summary>
   13:     public sealed class Epoch :
   14:         BaseUnit,
   15:         IEpoch

   18:             new object();

   20:         private readonly List<ITimeline> _timelines =
   21:             new List<ITimeline>();
   22: 
   23:         private readonly HashSet<Guid> _timelineIds =
   24:             new HashSet<Guid>();
   25: 
   26:         public Epoch()
   27:             : base()
   28:         {
   29:         }
   30: 
   31:         public Epoch(
   32:             Guid id)
   33:             : base(
   34:                 id)
   35:         {
   36:         }
   37: 
   38:         public int Count
   39:         {
   40:             get
   41:             {
   42:                 lock (_sync)
   43:                 {
   44:                     return _timelines.Count;
   45:                 }
   46:             }
   47:         }

   49:         public IReadOnlyList<ITimeline> Timelines
   50:         {
   51:             get

   53:                 lock (_sync)

   55:                     return _timelines.ToArray();
   56:                 }
   57:             }
   58:         }

   60:         public bool AddTimeline(
   61:             ITimeline timeline)

   63:             if (timeline == null)
   64:             {
   65:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   66:                     nameof(timeline));
   67:             }
   68: 
   69:             lock (_sync)
   70:             {
   71:                 if (!_timelineIds.Add(
   72:                         timeline.Id))
   73:                 {
   74:                     return false;
   75:                 }
   76: 
   77:                 _timelines.Add(
   78:                     timeline);
   79: 
   80:                 return true;
   81:             }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Units\Moment.cs

    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using System.Linq;
    4: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    5: 
    6: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    7: {
    8:     /// <summary>
    9:     /// Immutable record of a terminal Pulse outcome.
   10:     ///
   11:     /// A Moment connects the Pulse identity, contributing
   12:     /// WorkUnits, final result, and terminal outcome.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public sealed class Moment :
   15:         BaseUnit,
   16:         IMoment
   17:     {
   18:         private readonly IReadOnlyList<Guid>
   19:             _workUnitIds;
   20: 
   21:         public Moment(
   22:             Guid pulseId,
   23:             object? result,
   24:             IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds = null)
   25:             : this(
   26:                 Guid.NewGuid(),
   27:                 pulseId,
   28:                 MomentOutcome.Completed,
   29:                 result,
   30:                 null,
   31:                 workUnitIds)
   32:         {
   33:         }
   34: 
   35:         public Moment(
   36:             Guid id,
   37:             Guid pulseId,
   38:             object? result,
   39:             IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds = null)
   40:             : this(
   41:                 id,
   42:                 pulseId,
   43:                 MomentOutcome.Completed,
   44:                 result,
   45:                 null,
   46:                 workUnitIds)
   47:         {
   48:         }
   49: 
   50:         public Moment(
   51:             Guid pulseId,
   52:             MomentOutcome outcome,
   53:             object? result,
   54:             Exception? exception,
   55:             IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds = null)
   56:             : this(
   57:                 Guid.NewGuid(),
   58:                 pulseId,
   59:                 outcome,
   60:                 result,
   61:                 exception,
   62:                 workUnitIds)
   63:         {
   64:         }
   65: 
   66:         public Moment(
   67:             Guid id,
   68:             Guid pulseId,
   69:             MomentOutcome outcome,
   70:             object? result,
   71:             Exception? exception,
   72:             IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds = null)
   73:             : base(
   74:                 id)
   75:         {
   76:             PulseId =
   77:                 ValidatePulseId(
   78:                     pulseId);
   79: 
   80:             ValidateOutcome(
   81:                 outcome,
   82:                 exception);
   83: 
   84:             Outcome = outcome;
   85:             Result = result;
   86:             Exception = exception;
   87: 
   88:             _workUnitIds =
   89:                 CopyWorkUnitIds(
   90:                     workUnitIds);
   91:         }
   92: 
   93:         public Guid PulseId { get; }
   94: 
   95:         public MomentOutcome Outcome { get; }
   96: 
   97:         public object? Result { get; }
   98: 
   99:         public Exception? Exception { get; }
  100: 
  101:         public IReadOnlyList<Guid> WorkUnitIds =>
  102:             _workUnitIds;
  103: 
  104:         private static Guid ValidatePulseId(
  105:             Guid pulseId)
  106:         {
  107:             if (pulseId == Guid.Empty)
  108:             {
  109:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  110:                     "Moments require a non-empty Pulse ID.",
  111:                     nameof(pulseId));
  112:             }
  113: 
  114:             return pulseId;
  115:         }
  116: 
  117:         private static void ValidateOutcome(
  118:             MomentOutcome outcome,
  119:             Exception? exception)
  120:         {
  121:             if (outcome ==
  122:                 MomentOutcome.Failed &&
  123:                 exception == null)
  124:             {
  125:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  126:                     "Failed Moments require an exception.",
  127:                     nameof(exception));
  128:             }
  129: 
  130:             if (outcome !=
  131:                 MomentOutcome.Failed &&
  132:                 exception != null)
  133:             {
  134:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  135:                     "Only failed Moments may carry an exception.",
  136:                     nameof(exception));
  137:             }
  138:         }
  139: 
  140:         private static IReadOnlyList<Guid>
  141:             CopyWorkUnitIds(
  142:                 IEnumerable<Guid>? workUnitIds)
  143:         {
  144:             if (workUnitIds == null)
  145:             {
  146:                 return Array.Empty<Guid>();
  147:             }
  148: 
  149:             var copy =
  150:                 workUnitIds
  151:                     .Where(
  152:                         id => id != Guid.Empty)
  153:                     .Distinct()
  154:                     .ToArray();
  155: 
  156:             return copy.Length == 0
  157:                 ? Array.Empty<Guid>()
  158:                 : copy;
  159:         }
  160:     }
  161: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Units\Pulse.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Represents one complete consumer request.
    8:     ///
    9:     /// The pulse identity is the canonical correlation
   10:     /// identity for every work unit, instruction, task,
   11:     /// and eventual moment produced while resolving the
   12:     /// request.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public sealed class Pulse :
   15:         BaseUnit,
   16:         IPulse
   17:     {
   18:         private readonly object _sync =
   19:             new object();
   20: 
   21:         private PulseStatus _status;
   22: 
   23:         public Pulse(
   24:             object? request)
   25:             : base()
   26:         {
   27:             Request = request;
   28: 
   29:             _status =
   30:                 PulseStatus.Created;
   31:         }
   32: 
   33:         public Pulse(
   34:             Guid id,
   35:             object? request)
   36:             : base(
   37:                 id)
   38:         {
   39:             Request = request;
   40: 
   41:             _status =
   42:                 PulseStatus.Created;
   43:         }
   44: 
   45:         public object? Request { get; }
   46: 
   47:         public PulseStatus Status
   48:         {
   49:             get
   50:             {
   51:                 lock (_sync)
   52:                 {
   53:                     return _status;
   54:                 }
   55:             }
   56:         }
   57: 
   58:         public bool IsTerminal
   59:         {
   60:             get
   61:             {
   62:                 lock (_sync)
   63:                 {
   64:                     return IsTerminalStatus(
   65:                         _status);
   66:                 }
   67:             }
   68:         }
   69: 
   70:         public bool TryBeginDecomposition()
   71:         {
   72:             return TryTransition(
   73:                 PulseStatus.Created,
   74:                 PulseStatus.Decomposing);
   75:         }
   76: 
   77:         public bool TryMarkPending()
   78:         {
   79:             return TryTransition(
   80:                 PulseStatus.Decomposing,
   81:                 PulseStatus.Pending);
   82:         }
   83: 
   84:         public bool TryMarkReady()
   85:         {
   86:             return TryTransition(
   87:                 PulseStatus.Pending,
   88:                 PulseStatus.Ready);
   89:         }
   90: 
   91:         public bool TryBeginProcessing()
   92:         {
   93:             return TryTransition(
   94:                 PulseStatus.Ready,
   95:                 PulseStatus.Processing);
   96:         }
   97: 
   98:         public bool TryComplete()
   99:         {
  100:             return TryTransition(
  101:                 PulseStatus.Processing,
  102:                 PulseStatus.Completed);
  103:         }
  104: 
  105:         public bool TryFail()
  106:         {
  107:             return TryTerminalTransition(
  108:                 PulseStatus.Failed);
  109:         }
  110: 
  111:         public bool TryCancel()
  112:         {
  113:             return TryTerminalTransition(
  114:                 PulseStatus.Cancelled);
  115:         }
  116: 
  117:         private bool TryTransition(
  118:             PulseStatus expected,
  119:             PulseStatus next)
  120:         {
  121:             lock (_sync)
  122:             {
  123:                 if (_status != expected)
  124:                 {
  125:                     return false;
  126:                 }
  127: 
  128:                 _status = next;
  129: 
  130:                 return true;
  131:             }
  132:         }
  133: 
  134:         private bool TryTerminalTransition(
  135:             PulseStatus terminalStatus)
  136:         {
  137:             lock (_sync)
  138:             {
  139:                 if (IsTerminalStatus(
  140:                         _status))
  141:                 {
  142:                     return false;
  143:                 }
  144: 
  145:                 _status = terminalStatus;
  146: 
  147:                 return true;
  148:             }
  149:         }
  150: 
  151:         private static bool IsTerminalStatus(
  152:             PulseStatus status)
  153:         {
  154:             return status ==
  155:                    PulseStatus.Completed ||
  156:                    status ==
  157:                    PulseStatus.Failed ||
  158:                    status ==
  159:                    PulseStatus.Cancelled;
  160:         }
  161:     }
  162: }

    1: using System;
    2: using System.Collections.Generic;
    3: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    4: 
    5: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    6: {
    7:     /// <summary>
    8:     /// Maintains an ordered collection of completed moments.
    9:     ///
   10:     /// Timeline order reflects the order in which moments
   11:     /// are recorded. It does not depend on system clock time.
   12:     /// </summary>
   13:     public sealed class Timeline :
   14:         BaseUnit,
   15:         ITimeline
   16:     {
   17:         private readonly object _sync =
   18:             new object();
   19: 
   20:         private readonly List<IMoment> _moments =
   21:             new List<IMoment>();
   22: 
   23:         private readonly HashSet<Guid> _momentIds =
   24:             new HashSet<Guid>();
   25: 
   26:         public Timeline()
   27:             : base()
   28:         {
   29:         }
   30: 
   31:         public Timeline(
   32:             Guid id)
   33:             : base(
   34:                 id)
   35:         {
   36:         }
   37: 
   38:         public int Count
   39:         {
   40:             get
   41:             {
   42:                 lock (_sync)
   43:                 {
   44:                     return _moments.Count;
   45:                 }
   46:             }
   47:         }
   48: 
   49:         public IReadOnlyList<IMoment> Moments
   50:         {
   51:             get
   52:             {
   53:                 lock (_sync)
   54:                 {
   55:                     return _moments.ToArray();
   56:                 }
   57:             }
   58:         }
   59: 
   60:         public bool AddMoment(
   61:             IMoment moment)
   62:         {
   63:             if (moment == null)
   64:             {
   65:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   66:                     nameof(moment));
   67:             }
   68: 
   69:             lock (_sync)
   70:             {
   71:                 if (!_momentIds.Add(
   72:                         moment.Id))
   73:                 {
   74:                     return false;
   75:                 }
   76: 
   77:                 _moments.Add(
   78:                     moment);
   79: 
   80:                 return true;
   81:             }
   82:         }
   83:     }
   84: }

FILE: WildsEngine.Core\Units\WorkUnit.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Core.Units
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Represents one internal piece of processing required
    8:     /// to resolve a pulse.
    9:     ///
   10:     /// Work units are implementation details of Wilds.
   11:     /// Consumers submit pulses and are not responsible for
   12:     /// estimating, creating, or scheduling work units.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public sealed class WorkUnit :
   15:         BaseUnit,
   16:         IWorkUnit
   17:     {
   18:         private readonly object _sync =
   19:             new object();
   20: 
   21:         private WorkUnitStatus _status;
   22:         private object? _result;
   23:         private Exception? _exception;
   24: 
   25:         public WorkUnit(
   26:             Guid pulseId,
   27:             object? payload)
   28:             : base()
   29:         {
   30:             PulseId =
   31:                 ValidatePulseId(
   32:                     pulseId);
   33: 
   34:             Payload = payload;
   35: 
   36:             _status =
   37:                 WorkUnitStatus.Created;
   38:         }
   39: 
   40:         public WorkUnit(
   41:             Guid id,
   42:             Guid pulseId,
   43:             object? payload)
   44:             : base(
   45:                 id)
   46:         {
   47:             PulseId =
   48:                 ValidatePulseId(
   49:                     pulseId);
   50: 
   51:             Payload = payload;
   52: 
   53:             _status =
   54:                 WorkUnitStatus.Created;
   55:         }
   56: 
   57:         public Guid PulseId { get; }
   58: 
   59:         public object? Payload { get; }
   60: 
   61:         public WorkUnitStatus Status
   62:         {
   63:             get
   64:             {
   65:                 lock (_sync)
   66:                 {
   67:                     return _status;
   68:                 }
   69:             }
   70:         }
   71: 
   72:         public object? Result
   73:         {
   74:             get
   75:             {
   76:                 lock (_sync)
   77:                 {
   78:                     return _result;
   79:                 }
   80:             }
   81:         }
   82: 
   83:         public Exception? Exception
   84:         {
   85:             get
   86:             {
   87:                 lock (_sync)
   88:                 {
   89:                     return _exception;
   90:                 }
   91:             }
   92:         }
   93: 
   94:         public bool IsTerminal
   95:         {
   96:             get
   97:             {
   98:                 lock (_sync)
   99:                 {
  100:                     return IsTerminalStatus(
  101:                         _status);
  102:                 }
  103:             }
  104:         }
  105: 
  106:         public bool TryMarkPending()
  107:         {
  108:             return TryTransition(
  109:                 WorkUnitStatus.Created,
  110:                 WorkUnitStatus.Pending);
  111:         }
  112: 
  113:         public bool TryBeginProcessing()
  114:         {
  115:             return TryTransition(
  116:                 WorkUnitStatus.Pending,
  117:                 WorkUnitStatus.Processing);
  118:         }
  119: 
  120:         public bool TryComplete(
  121:             object? result)
  122:         {
  123:             lock (_sync)
  124:             {
  125:                 if (_status !=
  126:                     WorkUnitStatus.Processing)
  127:                 {
  128:                     return false;
  129:                 }
  130: 
  131:                 _result = result;
  132:                 _exception = null;
  133: 
  134:                 _status =
  135:                     WorkUnitStatus.Completed;
  136: 
  137:                 return true;
  138:             }
  139:         }
  140: 
  141:         public bool TryFail(
  142:             Exception exception)
  143:         {
  144:             if (exception == null)
  145:             {
  146:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
  147:                     nameof(exception));
  148:             }
  149: 
  150:             lock (_sync)
  151:             {
  152:                 if (IsTerminalStatus(
  153:                         _status))
  154:                 {
  155:                     return false;
  156:                 }
  157: 
  158:                 _exception = exception;
  159: 
  160:                 _status =
  161:                     WorkUnitStatus.Failed;
  162: 
  163:                 return true;
  164:             }
  165:         }
  166: 
  167:         public bool TryCancel()
  168:         {
  169:             lock (_sync)
  170:             {
  171:                 if (IsTerminalStatus(
  172:                         _status))
  173:                 {
  174:                     return false;
  175:                 }
  176: 
  177:                 _status =
  178:                     WorkUnitStatus.Cancelled;
  179: 
  180:                 return true;
  181:             }
  182:         }
  183: 
  184:         private bool TryTransition(
  185:             WorkUnitStatus expected,
  186:             WorkUnitStatus next)
  187:         {
  188:             lock (_sync)
  189:             {
  190:                 if (_status != expected)
  191:                 {
  192:                     return false;
  193:                 }
  194: 
  195:                 _status = next;
  196: 
  197:                 return true;
  198:             }
  199:         }
  200: 
  201:         private static Guid ValidatePulseId(
  202:             Guid pulseId)
  203:         {
  204:             if (pulseId == Guid.Empty)
  205:             {
  206:                 throw new ArgumentException(
  207:                     "Work units require a non-empty pulse ID.",
  208:                     nameof(pulseId));
  209:             }
  210: 
  211:             return pulseId;
  212:         }
  213: 
  214:         private static bool IsTerminalStatus(
  215:             WorkUnitStatus status)
  216:         {
  217:             return status ==
  218:                    WorkUnitStatus.Completed ||
  219:                    status ==
  220:                    WorkUnitStatus.Failed ||
  221:                    status ==
  222:                    WorkUnitStatus.Cancelled;
  223:         }
  224:     }
  225: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Qsl\WildsQslExecutionException.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using QSL.Core;
    3: 
    4: namespace WildsEngine.Qsl
    5: {
    6:     /// <summary>
    7:     /// Represents an unsuccessful QSL processing result
    8:     /// encountered while resolving a Wilds WorkUnit.
    9:     ///
   10:     /// Throwing this exception allows WildsEngine to record
   11:     /// the QSL failure as a failed WorkUnit, failed Pulse,
   12:     /// and failed Moment.
   13:     /// </summary>
   14:     public sealed class WildsQslExecutionException :
   15:         Exception
   16:     {
   17:         public WildsQslExecutionException(
   18:             QslProcessingResult result)
   19:             : base(
   20:                 CreateMessage(
   21:                     result),
   22:                 result != null
   23:                     ? result.Exception
   24:                     : null)
   25:         {
   26:             Result = result ??
   27:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   28:                     nameof(result));
   29:         }
   30: 
   31:         public QslProcessingResult Result { get; }
   32: 
   33:         private static string CreateMessage(
   34:             QslProcessingResult result)
   35:         {
   36:             if (result == null)
   37:             {
   38:                 return "QSL processing failed without a result.";
   39:             }
   40: 
   41:             if (result.HasException &&
   42:                 result.Exception != null)
   43:             {
   44:                 return
   45:                     "QSL processing raised an exception: " +
   46:                     result.Exception.Message;
   47:             }
   48: 
   49:             if (result.ParseFailed)
   50:             {
   51:                 return
   52:                     "QSL parsing failed with " +
   53:                     result.ParserDiagnostics.Count +
   54:                     " diagnostic(s).";
   55:             }
   56: 
   57:             if (result.EvaluationFailed)
   58:             {
   59:                 return
   60:                     "QSL evaluation failed with " +
   61:                     result.RuntimeDiagnostics.Count +
   62:                     " runtime diagnostic(s).";
   63:             }
   64: 
   65:             return "QSL processing did not succeed.";
   66:         }
   67:     }
   68: }

############################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Qsl\WildsQslExecutor.cs
############################################################
    1: using System;
    2: using System.Threading;
    3: using System.Threading.Tasks;
    4: using QSL.Core;
    5: using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
    6: 
    7: namespace WildsEngine.Qsl
    8: {
    9:     /// <summary>
   10:     /// Executes QSL source through the complete WildsEngine
   11:     /// request lifecycle.
   12:     ///
   13:     /// source
   14:     ///     -> Pulse
   15:     ///     -> WorkUnit
   16:     ///     -> QSL evaluation
   17:     ///     -> Moment
   18:     ///     -> Timeline
   19:     ///     -> Epoch
   20:     /// </summary>
   21:     public sealed class WildsQslExecutor
   22:     {
   23:         private readonly QslEngine _qsl;
   24:         private readonly Core.WildsEngine _wilds;
   25: 
   26:         public WildsQslExecutor(
   27:             QslEngine qsl,
   28:             Core.WildsEngine wilds)
   29:         {
   30:             _qsl = qsl ??
   31:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   32:                     nameof(qsl));
   33: 
   34:             _wilds = wilds ??
   35:                 throw new ArgumentNullException(
   36:                     nameof(wilds));
   37:         }
   38: 
   39:         /// <summary>
   40:         /// Executes one complete QSL request through Wilds.
   41:         ///
   42:         /// Wilds remains responsible for the Pulse,
   43:         /// WorkUnit, terminal Moment, Timeline, and Epoch.
   44:         /// QSL remains responsible only for processing the
   45:         /// supplied QSL source.
   46:         /// </summary>
   47:         public ValueTask<IMoment> ExecuteAsync(
   48:             string source,
   49:             CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
   50:         {
   51:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
   52:                     source))
   53:             {
   54:                 throw new ArgumentException(
   55:                     "QSL source cannot be empty.",
   56:                     nameof(source));
   57:             }
   58: 
   59:             return _wilds.ExecuteAsync(
   60:                 source,
   61:                 ExecuteQslAsync,
   62:                 cancellationToken);
   63:         }
   64: 
   65:         private ValueTask<object?> ExecuteQslAsync(
   66:             object? request,
   67:             CancellationToken cancellationToken)
   68:         {
   69:             cancellationToken
   70:                 .ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
   71: 
   72:             var source =
   73:                 request as string;
   74: 
   75:             if (source == null)
   76:             {
   77:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
   78:                     "WildsQslExecutor expected a QSL " +
   79:                     "source string.");
   80:             }
   81: 
   82:             var result =
   83:                 _qsl.Evaluate(
   84:                     source);
   85: 
   86:             if (!result.Succeeded)
   87:             {
   88:                 throw new WildsQslExecutionException(
   89:                     result);
   90:             }
   91: 
   92:             return new ValueTask<object?>(
   93:                 result.Value);
   94:         }
   95:     }
   96: }
